--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/notification.py ---
import asyncio
from loguru import logger
from bot.core.event_bus import EventBus

class NotificationWorker:
    """Асинхронная очередь уведомлений Telegram с защитой от превышения лимитов."""
    
    def __init__(self, bus: EventBus, send_chat_func):
        self.bus = bus
        self.send_chat = send_chat_func
        self.queue = self.bus.subscribe("NOTIFY", maxsize=200)
        
        # Лимит одного сообщения в Telegram - 4096 символов. 
        # Берем 3800 с запасом на HTML-теги и форматирование.
        self.MAX_MSG_LENGTH = 3800

    async def run(self):
        logger.info("📬 Notification Worker запущен: умный батчинг сообщений")
        buffer = []
        current_length = 0
        
        while True:
            try:
                # Ждем сообщение с таймаутом (2 сек), чтобы периодически сбрасывать буфер
                try:
                    event = await asyncio.wait_for(self.queue.get(), timeout=2.0)
                    payload = event.payload
                    
                    text = payload.get("text", "")
                    urgent = payload.get("urgent", False)
                    
                    if urgent:
                        # Экстренные сообщения (паника/дамп) отправляем немедленно
                        await self._send_chunks(text)
                    else:
                        text_len = len(text) + 2  # +2 для разделителя \n\n
                        
                        # Если буфер переполняется - отправляем то, что накопили
                        if current_length + text_len > self.MAX_MSG_LENGTH:
                            await self._flush(buffer)
                            buffer = []
                            current_length = 0
                            
                        buffer.append(text)
                        current_length += text_len
                        
                        # Батчинг по 5 сообщений для снижения количества запросов к API
                        if len(buffer) >= 5:
                            await self._flush(buffer)
                            buffer = []
                            current_length = 0
                            
                    self.queue.task_done()
                except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                    # Раз в 2 секунды сбрасываем всё, что зависло в буфере
                    if buffer:
                        await self._flush(buffer)
                        buffer = []
                        current_length = 0
                        
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"NotificationWorker error: {e}")

    async def _flush(self, buffer: list):
        """Склеивает и отправляет накопившийся буфер."""
        if not buffer:
            return
        msg = "\n\n".join(buffer)
        await self._send_chunks(msg)

    async def _send_chunks(self, text: str):
        """Безопасная отправка длинных сообщений с разбивкой."""
        while len(text) > 0:
            chunk = text[:self.MAX_MSG_LENGTH]
            
            if len(text) > self.MAX_MSG_LENGTH:
                # Ищем последний перенос строки, чтобы не рвать слова и HTML-теги пополам
                last_newline = chunk.rfind('\n')
                if last_newline > 0:
                    chunk = chunk[:last_newline]
            
            try:
                await self.send_chat(chunk)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Telegram send chunk error: {e}")
                
            text = text[len(chunk):].lstrip()
            # Защита от Flood Limit'ов Telegram API (не более 20 сообщений в минуту в 1 чат)
            await asyncio.sleep(0.2)

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/notification.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/order_manager.py ---
import time
import asyncio
from loguru import logger

from bot.core.event_bus import EventBus
from bot.exchange.paper_exchange import paper
from bot.strategy.sizing import buy_size, portfolio_limits, entry_offset, min_order_threshold
from bot.strategy.scanner import get_thresholds
from bot.strategy.learner import learner
from bot.strategy.risk import (
    calc_initial_sl_tp, get_trade_policy,
    evaluate_setup_grade, calc_total_open_risk_pct
)
from bot.news.cmc import get_coin_name
from bot.news.rss_news import fetch_news_cache, check_sentiment
from bot.utils.format import pair_html, corr_txt, usd, fmt_price, fmt_pct, grade_badge
from bot.core.state import bot_state


class OrderManagerWorker:
    """Управляет портфелем: контроль Portfolio VaR, 1H Gatekeeper, свободный USDT и Умная Ротация Ордеров."""

    def __init__(self, bus: EventBus):
        self.bus = bus
        self.signal_queue = self.bus.subscribe("SIGNALS_READY", maxsize=2)

    async def run(self):
        logger.info("💼 Order Manager запущен: контроль Portfolio VaR, 1H Gatekeeper, Ротация и свободный баланс...")
        while True:
            try:
                event = await self.signal_queue.get()
                await self._process_signals(event.payload)
                self.signal_queue.task_done()
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception(f"OrderManager error: {e}")

    def _notify(self, text: str, urgent: bool = False):
        self.bus.publish("NOTIFY", {"text": text, "urgent": urgent})

    def _try_rotate_order(self, cand: dict, cand_grade: str, tickers: dict, filter_sector: str = None, reason_tag: str = "ротация") -> bool:
        """
        Умная ротация лимитных ордеров:
        Если лимит сектора, бюджета риска, свободного USDT или слотов ордеров заполнен,
        проверяет, есть ли среди висящих ордеров более слабый/старый кандидат, который не находится
        в зоне немедленного исполнения (> 0.45% до цены), и заменяет его на более сильный сетап.
        """
        if not paper.orders:
            return False

        now_ts = int(time.time())
        cand_score = cand.get("score", 0.0)
        eligible = []

        for o in paper.orders:
            if filter_sector and (o.get("sector") or "Other") != filter_sector:
                continue

            o_age = now_ts - o.get("created", now_ts)
            o_grade = o.get("grade", "B")
            o_score = o.get("score", 0.0)

            # Минимальный возраст ордера для ротации: 5 мин (если новый [💎] A+ против [B]), иначе 10 мин
            min_age = 300 if (cand_grade == "A+" and o_grade != "A+") else 600
            if o_age < min_age:
                continue

            # Не трогаем ордер, если цена уже вплотную подошла к нему (Freeze Zone <= 0.40%)
            last_p = tickers.get(o["symbol"], {}).get("last", 0.0)
            if last_p > 0 and abs(last_p - o["price"]) / o["price"] * 100 <= 0.40:
                continue

            score_diff = cand_score - o_score
            is_upgrade = (score_diff >= 1.0) or (cand_grade == "A+" and o_grade != "A+" and score_diff >= 0.3)
            if is_upgrade:
                # Приоритет на снятие: сначала [B], затем самый низкий скор и самый старый
                priority_key = (1 if o_grade == "A+" else 0, o_score, -o_age)
                eligible.append((priority_key, o))

        if not eligible:
            return False

        eligible.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        victim = eligible[0][1]
        v_sym = victim["symbol"]
        v_last = tickers.get(v_sym, {}).get("last", victim["price"])

        paper.cancel_order(victim["id"])
        paper.log_event(
            v_sym, "cancel", v_last,
            f"Ротация -> {cand['symbol'][:-4]} ({victim.get('score', 0):.1f} -> {cand_score:.1f})",
            tp=victim.get("tp"), sl=victim.get("sl"),
            sup=victim.get("nearest_sup"), res=victim.get("nearest_res")
        )
        logger.info(
            f"OrderManager: 🔄 РОТАЦИЯ ОРДЕРА ({reason_tag}): снят {v_sym} "
            f"(score {victim.get('score', 0):.1f}{grade_badge(victim)}) ради {cand['symbol']} "
            f"(score {cand_score:.1f}{' [💎]' if cand_grade == 'A+' else ''})"
        )
        self._notify(
            f"🔄 <b>Ротация</b> · снят {pair_html(v_sym, victim)}{grade_badge(victim)} ({victim.get('score', 0):.1f}) "
            f"➡️ под <b>{cand['symbol'][:-4]}</b>{' [💎]' if cand_grade == 'A+' else ''} ({cand_score:.1f})"
        )
        return True

    async def _process_signals(self, payload: dict):
        candidates = payload["candidates"]
        tickers = payload["tickers"]
        regime = payload["regime"]

        equity = paper.equity(tickers)
        thrs = get_thresholds(regime)
        sec_lim, other_lim = portfolio_limits(equity)
        news_items = await fetch_news_cache()

        risk_mode = getattr(learner, "current_risk_mode", "NORMAL")
        policy = get_trade_policy(risk_mode)
        min_usdt_order = min_order_threshold(equity)
        max_active_orders = 2 if risk_mode == "STRICT" else (3 if risk_mode == "CAUTIOUS" else 4)

        logger.info(
            f"💼 OrderManager [{risk_mode}]: кандидатов {len(candidates)}, "
            f"открытый риск {calc_total_open_risk_pct(paper.positions, paper.orders, equity):.2f}% / {policy['max_portfolio_risk_pct']}%"
        )

        for cand in candidates:
            sym = cand["symbol"]
            base = sym[:-4]

            if sym in paper.positions or any(o["symbol"] == sym for o in paper.orders):
                continue

            patterns = cand.get("patterns", [])
            is_accum = "vcp" in patterns
            e21_15m = cand.get("e21", 0.0)
            e50_15m = cand.get("e50", 0.0)

            bot_state.check_structural_reset(sym, cand["last"], e21_15m, e50_15m)
            if bot_state.is_on_cooldown(sym):
                continue

            sector = cand.get("sector", "Other")
            tier = cand.get("tier") or "SMALL"
            entry_mode = cand.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
            is_mom = cand.get("is_momentum", False)

            if entry_mode == "reversal":
                can_rev, rev_reason = bot_state.can_enter_reversal(sym, cand["last"], e21_15m)
                if not can_rev:
                    logger.info(f"OrderManager: {sym} reversal заблокирован -> {rev_reason}")
                    continue

            # Оценка класса сетапа Grade A+ [💎]
            grade = evaluate_setup_grade(cand)
            if policy["require_grade_a_for_alt"] and entry_mode in ("reversal", "rocket"):
                if grade != "A+":
                    logger.info(f"OrderManager: {sym} пропущен -> режим {risk_mode} требует [💎] A+ для {entry_mode} (текущий: {grade})")
                    continue

            # Предварительный фильтр новостей
            name = await get_coin_name(base)
            neg, pos_news, mentions, _ = check_sentiment(news_items, [base, name])
            if neg > 0 and neg >= (pos_news * 2) and neg >= (mentions * 0.33):
                logger.info(f"OrderManager: {sym} отклонен -> токсичные новости ({neg}/{mentions})")
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 7200)
                continue

            # 1. Контроль лимита сектора (с попыткой умной ротации внутри сектора)
            lim = other_lim if sector == "Other" else sec_lim
            sector_count = sum(1 for p in paper.positions.values() if (p.get("sector") or "Other") == sector)
            sector_orders = sum(1 for o in paper.orders if (o.get("sector") or "Other") == sector)
            if (sector_count + sector_orders) >= lim:
                if sector_orders > 0 and self._try_rotate_order(cand, grade, tickers, filter_sector=sector, reason_tag=f"лимит сектора {sector}"):
                    pass
                else:
                    logger.debug(f"OrderManager: {sym} пропущен (лимит сектора {sector}: {lim})")
                    continue

            # 2. Проактивная ротация при заполненном пуле активных ордеров
            if len(paper.orders) >= max_active_orders:
                self._try_rotate_order(cand, grade, tickers, reason_tag="апгрейд пула ордеров")

            # 3. Контроль реально свободного USDT (с попыткой ротации, если баланс заперт в старых ордерах)
            locked_usdt = sum(o["qty"] * o["price"] * (1.0 + paper.FEE_PCT / 100.0) for o in paper.orders)
            free_usdt = max(0.0, paper.usdt - locked_usdt)
            if free_usdt < min_usdt_order:
                if self._try_rotate_order(cand, grade, tickers, reason_tag="освобождение USDT"):
                    locked_usdt = sum(o["qty"] * o["price"] * (1.0 + paper.FEE_PCT / 100.0) for o in paper.orders)
                    free_usdt = max(0.0, paper.usdt - locked_usdt)
                if free_usdt < min_usdt_order:
                    logger.debug(f"OrderManager: свободный баланс {free_usdt:.2f} USDT < мин. ордера {min_usdt_order:.2f} USDT — стоп набора")
                    break

            thr = cand.get("thr") or thrs.get(entry_mode, 6.0)
            off = entry_offset(cand["score"], thr, regime, cand["atr_pct"], entry_mode, tier=tier, is_accumulation=is_accum)

            t_data = tickers.get(sym, {})
            bid1 = t_data.get("bid1", cand["last"])
            ideal_entry = cand["last"] * (1 + off)
            entry = min(ideal_entry, bid1)

            wick_entry = cand.get("wick_entry_price")
            if entry_mode == "reversal" and wick_entry and 0 < wick_entry < entry:
                entry = max(wick_entry, cand["last"] * 0.985)
                off = (entry - cand["last"]) / cand["last"]

            sup = cand.get("nearest_sup")
            sup_touches = cand.get("sup_touches", 0)
            if sup and sup_touches >= 2 and not is_mom:
                retest_floor = sup * 1.0015
                if cand["last"] >= retest_floor:
                    entry = max(entry, retest_floor)
                    entry = min(entry, bid1)

            a = cand["atr"]
            if a <= 0:
                continue

            all_res = cand.get("signal_values", {}).get("all_res", [])
            res_above_entry = []
            for r_item in all_res:
                r_val, r_tch = r_item if isinstance(r_item, (list, tuple)) else (r_item, 2)
                if r_val > entry * 1.0025:
                    res_above_entry.append((r_val, r_tch))

            if res_above_entry:
                res_above_entry.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
                entry_res, res_touches = res_above_entry[0]
            else:
                entry_res = cand.get("nearest_res")
                res_touches = cand.get("res_touches", 0)

            min_rr = policy["min_rr_sniper"] if entry_mode == "sniper" else policy["min_rr_alt"]

            sl, tp, is_valid, rej_reason = calc_initial_sl_tp(
                symbol=sym, entry=entry, tier=tier, a=a,
                sup=sup, sup_touches=sup_touches,
                res=entry_res, res_touches=res_touches,
                is_mom=is_mom, entry_mode=entry_mode,
                min_rr=min_rr,
                recent_low_6=cand.get("recent_low_6"),
                ema50=e50_15m,
                e21_1h=cand.get("e21_1h"),
                e50_1h=cand.get("e50_1h"),
            )

            if not is_valid:
                logger.info(f"OrderManager: {sym} отклонён -> {rej_reason}")
                continue

            # 4. Бюджетирование совокупного риска (Portfolio VaR) с поддержкой ротации
            sl_dist_pct = (entry - sl) / entry * 100
            if sl_dist_pct <= 0:
                continue

            trade_risk_budget_pct = policy["risk_per_trade_core"] if entry_mode == "sniper" else policy["risk_per_trade_alt"]
            current_risk_pct = calc_total_open_risk_pct(paper.positions, paper.orders, equity)
            avail_risk_budget_pct = policy["max_portfolio_risk_pct"] - current_risk_pct

            if avail_risk_budget_pct <= 0.15:
                if self._try_rotate_order(cand, grade, tickers, reason_tag="лимит Portfolio VaR"):
                    current_risk_pct = calc_total_open_risk_pct(paper.positions, paper.orders, equity)
                    avail_risk_budget_pct = policy["max_portfolio_risk_pct"] - current_risk_pct
                    locked_usdt = sum(o["qty"] * o["price"] * (1.0 + paper.FEE_PCT / 100.0) for o in paper.orders)
                    free_usdt = max(0.0, paper.usdt - locked_usdt)

            if avail_risk_budget_pct <= 0.15:
                logger.debug(f"OrderManager: {sym} пропущен -> лимит риска {policy['max_portfolio_risk_pct']}% исчерпан")
                continue

            effective_risk_pct = min(trade_risk_budget_pct, avail_risk_budget_pct)
            size_by_risk = (equity * (effective_risk_pct / 100.0)) / (sl_dist_pct / 100.0)

            size_base = buy_size(
                equity, cand["score"], thr, cand["liquidity"], free_usdt,
                entry_mode=entry_mode, grade=grade
            )

            size = min(size_base, size_by_risk, free_usdt * 0.98, equity * 0.12)
            size = round(size, 2)

            if size < min_usdt_order:
                logger.debug(f"OrderManager: {sym} пропущен (размер {size:.2f} < мин. порога {min_usdt_order:.2f} USDT)")
                continue

            qty = size / entry
            order = paper.place_limit_buy(sym, qty, entry, tp=tp, sl=sl, score=cand["score"], reason_keys=cand.get("reason_keys", []))

            order.update({
                "sector": sector, "tier": tier, "corr": cand.get("corr"),
                "regime": regime, "risk_mode": risk_mode,
                "thr": thr,
                "gate_1h": cand.get("gate_1h", "1H_TREND_OK"),
                "reason_1h": cand.get("reason_1h", ""),
                "chg_48h": cand.get("chg_48h", 0.0),
                "pos_in_48h": cand.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5),
                "reasons": cand.get("reasons", []),
                "is_momentum": is_mom, "entry_mode": entry_mode,
                "nearest_res": entry_res,
                "res_touches": res_touches,
                "nearest_sup": sup,
                "sup_touches": sup_touches,
                "sl_state": "SHIELD",
                "patterns": patterns,
                "grade": grade,
                "initial_score": cand["score"],
                "last_move_ts": int(order["created"]),
                "_ema21": e21_15m,
                "_ema50": e50_15m,
                "_atr15": a,
            })
            paper.save()

            reasons_html = "<br>".join([f"• {r}" for r in cand.get("reasons", [])])
            paper.log_event(sym, "order_placed", entry, text=reasons_html, mode=entry_mode, tp=tp, sl=sl, sup=sup, res=entry_res)

            tp_pct = (tp - entry) / entry * 100
            sl_pct = (sl - entry) / entry * 100
            g_badge = " [💎]" if grade == "A+" else ""

            logger.info(
                f"OrderManager: 🎯 {sym} ОРДЕР {entry_mode}{g_badge}: entry=${fmt_price(entry)}, "
                f"TP=${fmt_price(tp)}, SL=${fmt_price(sl)} | 1H: {cand.get('reason_1h', '')}"
            )

            self._notify(
                f"📋 <b>Ордер</b> · {pair_html(sym, order)}{g_badge}\n"
                f"💵 {usd(size)} · 📥 {fmt_price(entry)} ({off * 100:+.2f}%){corr_txt(cand)}\n"
                f"🎯 {fmt_price(tp)} ({fmt_pct(tp_pct)}) · 🛡📉 {fmt_price(sl)} ({fmt_pct(sl_pct)})\n"
                f"⭐ {cand['score']:.1f}/{thr:g} · 🕐 {cand.get('reason_1h', '')}\n"
                f"🧠 {'; '.join(cand['reasons'][:3])}"
            )

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/order_manager.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/position_manager.py ---
import time
import asyncio
from loguru import logger

from bot.core.event_bus import EventBus
from bot.exchange.market_data import market_data
from bot.exchange.paper_exchange import paper
from bot.strategy.scanner import live_score, get_thresholds, _returns
from bot.strategy.indicators import atr, ema, find_sr_levels, get_all_sr_levels
from bot.strategy.learner import learner
from bot.strategy.shadow import shadow
from bot.strategy.risk import calc_initial_sl_tp, calc_runner_target, update_position_sl, get_trade_policy
from bot.news.cmc import get_coin_name
from bot.news.rss_news import fetch_news_cache, check_sentiment
from bot.strategy.sizing import entry_offset
from bot.utils.format import (
    pair_html, format_coin_compact, sl_emoji, grade_badge,
    usd, fmt_price, fmt_pct, pnl_emoji
)
from bot.core.state import bot_state


class PositionManagerWorker:
    """Управление позициями: Дышащий Коридор по свечам 15m/1H, Зона Заморозки лимиток и защита баланса."""

    def __init__(self, bus: EventBus):
        self.bus = bus
        self.regime_queue = self.bus.subscribe("REGIME_UPDATED", maxsize=2)
        self.current_regime = "neutral"

    async def run(self):
        logger.info("🛡 Position Manager запущен: Дышащий Коридор и Freeze Zone для ордеров...")
        asyncio.create_task(self._regime_updater())

        while True:
            if bot_state.paused or not bot_state.trading_enabled:
                await asyncio.sleep(10)
                continue

            try:
                await self._maintenance_cycle()
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception(f"PositionManager error: {e}")

            await asyncio.sleep(60)

    async def _regime_updater(self):
        while True:
            event = await self.regime_queue.get()
            self.current_regime = event.payload["regime"]
            self.regime_queue.task_done()

    def _notify(self, text: str, urgent: bool = False):
        self.bus.publish("NOTIFY", {"text": text, "urgent": urgent})

    async def _maintenance_cycle(self):
        tickers = await market_data.get_tickers()
        deriv_tickers = await market_data.get_derivatives_tickers()
        if not tickers:
            return

        btc_candles = await market_data.get_kline("BTCUSDT", "15", 120)
        btc_ret = _returns([c["close"] for c in btc_candles])

        metrics_24h = paper.get_metrics(tickers, hours=24)
        old_mode = getattr(learner, "current_risk_mode", "NORMAL")
        learner.update_threshold(metrics_24h["profit_factor"], metrics_24h["max_drawdown_pct"], metrics_24h["total_trades"])
        new_mode = getattr(learner, "current_risk_mode", "NORMAL")

        if old_mode != new_mode and metrics_24h["total_trades"] > 0:
            mode_icons = {"NORMAL": "🟢", "CAUTIOUS": "🟡", "STRICT": "🔴", "AGGRESSIVE": "🚀"}
            self._notify(f"🎚 <b>Режим риска (ИИ) изменен:</b> {mode_icons.get(new_mode, '⚪')} {new_mode}\n<i>(строгость {learner.threshold_adj:+.2f})</i>")

        news_items = await fetch_news_cache()
        regime = self.current_regime
        thrs = get_thresholds(regime)
        policy = get_trade_policy(new_mode)
        current_time = int(time.time())

        # =====================================================================
        # 1. ПРОВЕРКА ОТКРЫТЫХ ПОЗИЦИЙ И ОБНОВЛЕНИЕ «ДЫШАЩЕГО КОРИДОРА»
        # =====================================================================
        for sym, pos in list(paper.positions.items()):
            t = tickers.get(sym)
            if not t:
                continue

            score_pos, candles = await live_score(sym, t, regime, btc_ret, news_items, deriv_t=deriv_tickers.get(sym), is_open_pos=True, btc_candles=btc_candles)
            if score_pos is None or not candles:
                continue

            closes = [c["close"] for c in candles]
            a = atr(candles)
            if a <= 0:
                continue

            last = t["last"]
            pnl_pct = (last - pos["avg"]) / pos["avg"] * 100 if pos["avg"] else 0
            e21, e50 = ema(closes, 21)[-1], ema(closes, 50)[-1]

            base = sym[:-4]
            name = await get_coin_name(base)
            neg, pos_news, mentions, _ = check_sentiment(news_items, [base, name])
            is_toxic = neg > 0 and neg >= (pos_news * 2) and neg >= (mentions * 0.33)

            if is_toxic:
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 7200)
                if pnl_pct >= 1.0 or pnl_pct <= 0:
                    reason = "✂📰 Новости"
                    ex = paper._sell(sym, last, reason, regime_now=regime)
                    paper.log_event(sym, "sell", last, f"Новости {neg}/{mentions}", tp=pos.get("tp"), sl=pos.get("sl"), sup=pos.get("nearest_sup"), res=pos.get("nearest_res"))
                    self._notify(f"💸 <b>Продажа</b> · {pair_html(sym, ex)}{grade_badge(ex)} · {reason} {neg}/{mentions}\n{pnl_emoji(ex['pnl_pct'])} {fmt_pct(ex['pnl_pct'])} · 💵 {usd(ex['pnl'])}")
                    continue

            entry_mode = pos.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
            trend_broken = (last < e50 * 0.996 and e21 < (e50 * 0.997))
            pos_age = current_time - pos.get("entry_time", current_time)

            should_exit_early = False
            if pos_age >= 900 and not is_toxic:
                if entry_mode != "reversal" and trend_broken:
                    should_exit_early = True

            if should_exit_early:
                reason = "🪫 Слом тренда" if pnl_pct > 0 else "✂️📉 Слом тренда"
                ex = paper._sell(sym, last, reason, regime_now=regime)
                ev_type = "sell_profit" if ex["pnl"] > 0 else "sell_loss"
                paper.log_event(sym, ev_type, last, reason, tp=pos.get("tp"), sl=pos.get("sl"), sup=pos.get("nearest_sup"), res=pos.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.record_trade_exit(sym, ex["pnl_pct"], last, is_sl=(ex["pnl_pct"] < -0.15))
                if ex["pnl"] > 0:
                    shadow.mark_success(sym)
                self._notify(f"💸 <b>Продажа</b> · {pair_html(sym, ex)}{grade_badge(ex)} · {reason}\n{pnl_emoji(ex['pnl_pct'])} {fmt_pct(ex['pnl_pct'])} · 💵 {usd(ex['pnl'])}")
                continue

            max_p = max(pos.get("max_price", last), last)
            pos["max_price"] = max_p
            sl_updated, new_sl, new_state, tag_emoji = update_position_sl(pos, max_p, last_price=last, candles_15m=candles)
            if sl_updated:
                old_sl_str = pos.get("_last_notified_sl_str") or fmt_price(pos.get("sl", 0.0))
                new_sl_str = fmt_price(new_sl)
                old_state = pos.get("_last_notified_sl_state") or pos.get("sl_state", "SHIELD")
                pos["sl"] = new_sl
                pos["sl_state"] = new_state
                pos["max_sl"] = new_sl
                paper.save()
                paper.log_event(sym, "sl_moved", pos["sl"], tag_emoji, tp=pos.get("tp"), sl=pos["sl"], sup=pos.get("nearest_sup"), res=pos.get("nearest_res"))

                last_notify_ts = float(pos.get("_last_sl_notify_ts", 0.0))
                if new_sl_str != old_sl_str and ((current_time - last_notify_ts >= 180) or new_state != old_state):
                    pos["_last_notified_sl_str"] = new_sl_str
                    pos["_last_notified_sl_state"] = new_state
                    pos["_last_sl_notify_ts"] = current_time
                    coin_compact = format_coin_compact(sym, pos)
                    self._notify(f"🛡 SL поднят по {coin_compact} до {new_sl_str} ({tag_emoji})")

            if pos.get("tp1_done") and last >= (pos["tp"] - 0.5 * a):
                candles_1h = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "60", 250)
                next_target, can_expand, _ = calc_runner_target(sym, last, candles, candles_1h, pos=pos)
                if can_expand and next_target > pos["tp"] * 1.006:
                    old_tp = pos["tp"]
                    pos["tp"] = next_target
                    pos["sl"] = max(pos["sl"], round(old_tp * 0.995, 8))
                    tp1_p = pos.get("tp1_price") or pos.get("initial_tp") or 0.0
                    pos["sl_state"] = "ABOVE_TP1" if (tp1_p > 0 and pos["sl"] >= tp1_p) else "TRAILING_RUNNER"
                    sl_tag = sl_emoji(pos["sl_state"])
                    paper.save()
                    paper.log_event(sym, "tp_runner_raised", pos["tp"], "TP🏃🏻‍➡️ поднят", mode="TP🏃🏻‍➡️", tp=pos["tp"], sl=pos["sl"], sup=pos.get("nearest_sup"), res=pos.get("nearest_res"))
                    self._notify(f"TP🏃🏻‍➡️ <b>Потолок коридора поднят</b> · {pair_html(sym, pos)}\n🎯 {fmt_price(pos['tp'])} · {sl_tag} {fmt_price(pos['sl'])}")

        # =====================================================================
        # 2. ПРОВЕРКА НЕИСПОЛНЕННЫХ ОРДЕРОВ (С ПРОЗРАЧНЫМ ЛОГИРОВАНИЕМ СНЯТИЯ)
        # =====================================================================
        for order in list(paper.orders):
            sym = order["symbol"]
            t = tickers.get(sym)
            if not t:
                continue

            base = sym[:-4]
            name = await get_coin_name(base)
            neg, pos_news, mentions, _ = check_sentiment(news_items, [base, name])

            if neg > 0 and neg >= (pos_news * 2) and neg >= (mentions * 0.33):
                paper.cancel_order(order["id"])
                paper.log_event(sym, "cancel", t["last"], "Токсичные новости", tp=order.get("tp"), sl=order.get("sl"), sup=order.get("nearest_sup"), res=order.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 7200)
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⚠️ Ордер {sym} снят -> токсичные новости ({neg}/{mentions})")
                self._notify(f"⚠️ Снят · {pair_html(sym, order)} · ☠️⚰️ (новости)")
                continue

            entry_mode = order.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
            patterns = order.get("patterns", [])

            score_now, candles = await live_score(
                sym, t, regime, btc_ret, news_items,
                deriv_t=deriv_tickers.get(sym),
                saved_patterns=patterns,
                saved_mode=entry_mode,
                btc_candles=btc_candles,
            )
            if score_now is None or not candles:
                continue

            closes = [c["close"] for c in candles]
            e21, e50 = ema(closes, 21)[-1], ema(closes, 50)[-1]
            a = atr(candles)
            if a <= 0:
                continue

            order_age = current_time - order["created"]
            tier = order.get("tier") or "SMALL"
            is_accum = "vcp" in patterns
            thr = thrs.get(entry_mode, 6.0)
            init_score = order.get("initial_score", order.get("score", thr))

            base_intact = (entry_mode != "reversal" and t["last"] >= e50 * 0.996 and e21 >= e50 * 0.996)
            trend_broken_order = (entry_mode != "reversal" and t["last"] < e50 * 0.990 and e21 < e50 * 0.997)

            if trend_broken_order:
                paper.cancel_order(order["id"])
                paper.log_event(sym, "cancel", t["last"], f"Умер ({init_score:.1f}-{score_now:.1f})", tp=order.get("tp"), sl=order.get("sl"), sup=order.get("nearest_sup"), res=order.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 300)
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⚠️ Ордер {sym} снят -> ☠️⚰️ слом тренда ({init_score:.1f} -> {score_now:.1f})")
                self._notify(f"⚠️ Снят · {pair_html(sym, order)} · ☠️⚰️ ({init_score:.1f}-{score_now:.1f})")
                continue

            if not base_intact and score_now < (thr - 1.5):
                paper.cancel_order(order["id"])
                paper.log_event(sym, "cancel", t["last"], f"Ослаб ({score_now:.1f})", tp=order.get("tp"), sl=order.get("sl"), sup=order.get("nearest_sup"), res=order.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 300)
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⚠️ Ордер {sym} снят -> 🪫📉 ослаб сигнал ({score_now:.1f} < {thr - 1.5:.2f})")
                self._notify(f"⚠️ Снят · {pair_html(sym, order)} · 🪫📉 ({score_now:.1f})")
                continue

            if t["last"] > order["price"] + 2.2 * a:
                paper.cancel_order(order["id"])
                paper.log_event(sym, "cancel", t["last"], "Улетела без нас", tp=order.get("tp"), sl=order.get("sl"), sup=order.get("nearest_sup"), res=order.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 300)
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⚠️ Ордер {sym} снят -> 🚀👋🏻 улетела без нас (last=${fmt_price(t['last'])} vs ord=${fmt_price(order['price'])})")
                self._notify(f"⚠️ Снят · {pair_html(sym, order)} · 🚀👋🏻")
                continue

            if order_age > 7200:
                paper.cancel_order(order["id"])
                paper.log_event(sym, "cancel", t["last"], "Тайм-аут 2ч", tp=order.get("tp"), sl=order.get("sl"), sup=order.get("nearest_sup"), res=order.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 420)
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⚠️ Ордер {sym} снят -> ☠️⚰️ тайм-аут 2ч ({init_score:.1f}-{score_now:.1f})")
                self._notify(f"⚠️ Снят · {pair_html(sym, order)} · ☠️⚰️ ({init_score:.1f}-{score_now:.1f})")
                continue

            # Зона заморозки (Freeze Zone): реквот не чаще раза в 8 минут
            last_move_ts = order.get("last_move_ts", order["created"])
            if (current_time - last_move_ts) < 480:
                continue

            atr_pct = a / t["last"] * 100
            off = entry_offset(score_now, thr, regime, atr_pct, entry_mode, tier=tier, is_accumulation=is_accum)
            ideal_price = t["last"] * (1 + off)
            old_price = order["price"]

            candles_1h = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "60", 250)
            nearest_res, res_touches, nearest_sup, sup_touches = find_sr_levels(candles, candles_1h)
            live_sup = nearest_sup if (nearest_sup and sup_touches >= 2) else order.get("nearest_sup")
            live_sup_touches = sup_touches if (nearest_sup and sup_touches >= 2) else order.get("sup_touches", 2 if order.get("nearest_sup") else 0)

            target_price = old_price
            action_type = None
            price_icon = ""
            dev_pct = abs(ideal_price - old_price) / old_price * 100
            in_freeze_zone = abs(t["last"] - old_price) <= (0.45 * a) or (abs(t["last"] - old_price) / old_price * 100) <= 0.38

            if ideal_price < old_price and dev_pct >= 0.40 and not in_freeze_zone:
                if not (live_sup and ideal_price < live_sup * 1.0015):
                    target_price = ideal_price
                    action_type = "correct"
                    price_icon = "⬇️"

            # В режиме BEAR и при политике STRICT запрещено двигать лимитки вверх (hunt_up)!
            elif ideal_price > old_price and dev_pct >= 0.38 and not in_freeze_zone and regime != "bear" and new_mode != "STRICT":
                can_shift_up = (is_accum and t["last"] <= (e50 * 1.012)) or (entry_mode == "sniper" and t["last"] <= (e21 * 1.008))
                if can_shift_up:
                    target_price = ideal_price
                    action_type = "hunt_up"
                    price_icon = "⬆️"

            if not action_type:
                continue

            all_res, all_sup = get_all_sr_levels(candles, candles_1h)
            res_above_target = [(r[0], r[1]) for r in all_res if r[0] > target_price * 1.0025]
            if res_above_target:
                res_above_target.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
                entry_res, entry_res_touches = res_above_target[0]
            else:
                entry_res, entry_res_touches = None, 0

            sup_below_target = [(s[0], s[1]) for s in all_sup if s[0] < target_price * 0.9985]
            if sup_below_target:
                sup_below_target.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
                entry_sup, entry_sup_touches = sup_below_target[0]
            else:
                entry_sup, entry_sup_touches = live_sup, live_sup_touches

            min_rr = policy["min_rr_sniper"] if entry_mode == "sniper" else policy["min_rr_alt"]
            recent_low_6 = min(c["low"] for c in candles[-6:]) if len(candles) >= 6 else None

            sl, tp, is_valid, _ = calc_initial_sl_tp(
                symbol=sym, entry=target_price, tier=tier, a=a,
                sup=entry_sup, sup_touches=entry_sup_touches,
                res=entry_res, res_touches=entry_res_touches,
                is_mom=(entry_mode == "rocket"), entry_mode=entry_mode,
                min_rr=min_rr,
                recent_low_6=recent_low_6,
                ema50=e50,
            )

            if is_valid and action_type:
                old_notional = order["qty"] * order["price"]
                order["price"] = target_price
                order["qty"] = old_notional / target_price if target_price > 0 else order["qty"]
                order["sl"] = sl
                order["tp"] = tp
                order["nearest_sup"] = entry_sup
                order["sup_touches"] = entry_sup_touches
                order["nearest_res"] = entry_res
                order["res_touches"] = entry_res_touches
                order["last_move_ts"] = current_time
                order["requotes"] = order.get("requotes", 0) + 1
                paper.save()

                msg_desc = "база" if action_type == "hunt_up" else f"{dev_pct:.2f}%"
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: 📐 Сдвиг ордера {sym} ({action_type}) -> new_entry=${fmt_price(target_price)}, SL=${fmt_price(sl)}")
                paper.log_event(sym, "order_moved", order["price"], f"Сдвиг: {action_type}", tp=order["tp"], sl=order["sl"], sup=entry_sup, res=entry_res)
                self._notify(f"📐 <b>Сдвиг {price_icon}</b> · {pair_html(sym, order)}\n📥 {fmt_price(order['price'])} ({off * 100:+.2f}%) · 🛡📉 {fmt_price(order['sl'])} · {msg_desc}")

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/position_manager.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/scanner.py ---
import asyncio
from loguru import logger
from bot.core.event_bus import EventBus
from bot.exchange.market_data import market_data
from bot.strategy.scanner import get_regime, scan
from bot.core.state import bot_state

class ScannerWorker:
    """Тяжелый I/O воркер: собирает метрики, анализирует RSI/EMA, генерирует сигналы."""
    
    def __init__(self, bus: EventBus):
        self.bus = bus

    async def run(self):
        logger.info("🔎 Scanner Worker запущен: параллельное сканирование рынка каждые 60с...")
        while True:
            if bot_state.paused or not bot_state.trading_enabled:
                await asyncio.sleep(10)
                continue
                
            try:
                tickers = await market_data.get_tickers()
                deriv_tickers = await market_data.get_derivatives_tickers()
                
                if tickers:
                    regime, info = await get_regime()
                    
                    # ⚡ Уведомление о смене рынка
                    if getattr(bot_state, "last_notified_regime", None) != regime:
                        bot_state.last_notified_regime = regime
                        reg_icon = "🟢" if regime == "bull" else "🔴" if regime == "bear" else "🟡"
                        self.bus.publish("NOTIFY", {"text": f"🧭 <b>Макро-тренд сменился:</b> {reg_icon} {regime.upper()}", "urgent": False})
                    
                    # ⚡ Кэшируем глобально, чтобы /status в Телеге отвечал моментально
                    bot_state.current_regime = regime 
                    self.bus.publish("REGIME_UPDATED", {"regime": regime, "info": info})
                    
                    candidates = await scan(regime, tickers, deriv_tickers, limit=20)
                    
                    if candidates:
                        self.bus.publish("SIGNALS_READY", {
                            "candidates": candidates,
                            "tickers": tickers,
                            "regime": regime
                        })
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception(f"ScannerWorker error: {e}")
                
            # БЫЛО: await asyncio.sleep(60)
            # СТАЛО: Сканируем раз в 3 минуты. Идеально для 15m таймфрейма.
            await asyncio.sleep(180)

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/scanner.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/services/__init__.py ---

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Паспорт бота «Капитан Рост» — секции команды /info и генератор Whitepaper."""


def _current_params():
    from bot.strategy.learner import learner
    from bot.strategy.shadow import shadow
    wr, _n = learner.winrate()
    a_stats = learner.get_grade_a_stats()
    t = shadow.tuning
    nudge_r = t.get("nudge_rocket", 0)
    nudge_s = t.get("nudge_sniper", 0)
    nudge_rev = t.get("nudge_reversal", 0)
    return (
        f"Чистый Winrate {wr:.0%} · 🛡🔒 БУ: {learner.be_count} · режим: <b>{learner.current_risk_mode}</b>.\n"
        f"💎 Доверие к [💎] A+: <b>×{a_stats['mult']:.2f}</b> (серия {a_stats['streak']:+d}).\n"
        f"⚙️ Тюнинг: 🚀{nudge_r:+.2f} 🏹{nudge_s:+.2f} 🧲{nudge_rev:+.2f} · SL ×{t.get('sl_mult', 1):.2f}"
    )


INFO_SECTIONS = [
    ("📌", "Текущий статус", _current_params),

    ("🤖", "О боте",
     "Алгоритмический спотовый бот для Bybit на асинхронном <b>EDA цикле</b>. "
     "Объединяет мультитаймфреймовый структурный анализ (48ч 1H Gatekeeper + 15m Price Action), "
     "динамическое сопровождение сделок «Дышащий Коридор» и интерактивный веб-дашборд <code>/audit</code>."),

     "• <b>1H_TREND_OK (🟢):</b> аптренд на 1H в рабочей зоне (≤72% 48ч-диапазона, без перегрева и шпилек) — разрешены 🏹 Снайпер и 🚀 Ракета.\n"
     "• <b>1H_SUPPORT_ONLY (🟡):</b> опора на многократную часовую плиту — разрешён 🧲 Ловец дна лимиткой в глубину тени с целью до 1H EMA 21.\n"
     "• <b>1H_BAN (🔴):</b> падение под падающей 1H EMA 50 без плиты, перегретые хаи (>72% канала) или остывание после пампа — полная блокировка."),

    ("🌬", "Дышащий Коридор (SL/TP)",
     "• Отказ от раннего прыжка в БУ на +0.8%.\n"
     "• <b>Асимметричный храповик SL:</b> плавно ползёт под EMA 50 (🏹) или плотно поджимается под EMA 21 (🚀).\n"
     "• <b>Адаптивный потолок TP🏃🏻‍➡️:</b> расширяется на разгоне тренда, замирает на полке и фиксирует профит на свече кульминации (RSI > 75)."),

    ("🗂", "Толковый словарь (Emoji Lexicon)",
     "• <b>Стопы:</b> 🛡📉 первичный · 🛡🔒 безубыток · 🛡💼 профит-лок (до TP1) · 🛡📈 трейлинг (после TP1) · 🛡🔥 стоп выше TP1.\n"
     "• <b>Цели и класс:</b> 🔥 TP1 · TP🏃🏻‍➡️ раннер · [💎] Grade A+.\n"
     "• <b>Снятие ордеров:</b> 🪫📉 ослаб · 🚀👋🏻 улетела · ☠️⚰️ слом структуры/таймаут."),
]


def section_body(body):
    return body() if callable(body) else body


def info_full_text():
    parts = []
    for emoji, title, body in INFO_SECTIONS:
        parts.append(f"{emoji} <b>{title}</b>\n{section_body(body)}")
    parts.append("\n📄 <i>Подробная техническая документация прикреплена файлом ниже.</i>")
    return "\n\n".join(parts)


def generate_whitepaper() -> str:
    doc = """=========================================================
CAPTAIN ROST: TECHNICAL WHITEPAPER & MECHANICS
=========================================================

1. МАТРИЦА 48-ЧАСОВОЙ ВАЛИДАЦИИ (1H GATEKEEPER)
---------------------------------------------------------
Перед поиском точки входа на 15m сканер валидирует 1H график за 48ч:
- 1H_TREND_OK: Close >= EMA50, EMA21 >= EMA50, позиция в рабочей зоне (pos_in_48h <= 72%, рост за 48ч <= +16%), RSI <= 75 -> разрешены sniper и rocket.
- 1H_SUPPORT_ONLY: опора на подтвержденную часовую плиту поддержки (вес >= 3) в нижней половине 48ч-диапазона -> только reversal лимиткой в глубину тени с потолком 1H EMA 21.
- 1H_BAN: падение под падающей 1H EMA50 без плиты, перекупленная крыша 48ч-диапазона (>72%) ИЛИ остывание сразу после вертикального пампа -> запрет торговли.

2. ДЫШАЩИЙ КОРИДОР СОПРОВОЖДЕНИЯ СДЕЛКИ И СТАТУС 🛡🔥
---------------------------------------------------------
- Красная линия SL (храповик): подтягивается под EMA50 - 0.8*ATR (sniper) или плотно под EMA21 - 0.45*ATR (rocket).
- Когда стоп-лосс после взятия 🔥 TP1 перешагивает цену первого тейка, он получает статус 🛡🔥 (Стоп выше TP1 — 100% гарантированный профит всей позиции).
- Зеленая линия TP (потолок): расширяется при разгоне тренда, замирает на полке и фиксирует сделку на пике при RSI > 75.

3. СТРОГИЙ VCP, HARD VETO И СТРУКТУРНЫЙ КАРАНТИН
---------------------------------------------------------
- VCP засчитывается только над растущей EMA21 и EMA50 с повышающимися минимумами (исключены ложные срабатывания при пробое EMA50 сверху вниз).
- Шпильки продаж на хаях (recent_rejection) и высокая корреляция с падающим BTC (corr >= 0.65 в BEAR) являются жесткими запретами (Hard Veto).
- Лимитные ордера защищены Freeze Zone (8 мин), а каждое снятие прозрачно логируется в bot.log.

4. ИНТЕРАКТИВНЫЙ ВЕБ-ДАШБОРД /audit
---------------------------------------------------------
- Доступен по веб-ссылке /audit и связан в 1 клик с графиками /chart?symbol=...
- Содержит KPI-карточки, графики Plotly (Equity Curve, сектора, структура выходов) и интерактивные таблицы сделок, монет и открытых позиций за 24 часа.
=========================================================
"""
    return doc

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/services/info.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/services/reports.py ---
import html as _html
import json
import time
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from bot.exchange.market_data import market_data
from bot.exchange.paper_exchange import paper
from bot.strategy.learner import learner, is_neutral_be
from bot.news.cmc import TIER_EMOJI
from bot.core.state import bot_state
from bot.utils.format import (
    format_coin, sl_emoji, grade_badge, mode_emoji,
    usd, fmt_price, fmt_pct, pnl_emoji
)

PERIODS = {
    "daily": ("#Дневной_отчёт", 1),
    "weekly": ("#Недельный_отчёт", 7),
    "monthly": ("#Месячный_отчёт", 30),
}


def _fmt_ts(ts: int, tz) -> str:
    if not ts:
        return "—"
    return datetime.fromtimestamp(int(ts), tz).strftime("%H:%M")


async def build_audit_html(tz, public_url: str = "") -> str:
    """
    Генерирует интерактивный веб-дашборд инфографики за 24 часа (/audit):
- KPI-карточки в шапке
- 3 интерактивных графика Plotly (Кривая капитала 24ч, PnL по секторам, Донат типов выходов)
- 3 интерактивные таблицы (Открытые позиции и ордера, Рейтинг монет, Хронология сделок)
      с прямыми кликабельными ссылками на свечной график каждой монеты (/chart?symbol=...).
    """
    prices = await market_data.get_tickers()
    eq = paper.equity(prices)
    now = datetime.now(tz)
    cutoff = int(time.time()) - 86400

    locked_usdt = sum(o["qty"] * o["price"] for o in paper.orders)
    avail_usdt = max(0.0, paper.usdt - locked_usdt)
    metrics_24h = paper.get_metrics(prices, hours=24)
    a_stats = learner.get_grade_a_stats()

    closed_24h = [
        r for r in paper.realized
        if not r.get("partial") and r.get("time", 0) >= cutoff
    ]
    closed_24h.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("time", 0))

    be_trades = [r for r in closed_24h if r.get("is_be") or is_neutral_be(r.get("pnl_pct", 0.0))]
    decisive = [r for r in closed_24h if r not in be_trades]
    wins = [r for r in decisive if r["pnl"] > 0]
    losses = [r for r in decisive if r["pnl"] <= 0]
    clean_wr = (len(wins) / len(decisive) * 100) if decisive else 0.0

    # --- ДАННЫЕ ДЛЯ ГРАФИКОВ PLOTLY ---
    # 1. Кривая накопленного PnL ($) за 24ч
    eq_times = ["Старт 24ч"]
    eq_vals = [0.0]
    eq_texts = ["Начало отсчёта"]
    cum_pnl = 0.0
    for t in closed_24h:
        cum_pnl = round(cum_pnl + float(t.get("pnl", 0.0)), 2)
        sym_b = t["symbol"][:-4] if t["symbol"].endswith("USDT") else t["symbol"]
        eq_times.append(_fmt_ts(t.get("time"), tz) + f" ({sym_b})")
        eq_vals.append(cum_pnl)
        eq_texts.append(f"{sym_b}: {t['pnl_pct']:+.2f}% ({usd(t['pnl'])}) · {t.get('exit_type', '')}")

    # 2. PnL по секторам ($)
    sec_pnl = defaultdict(float)
    sec_cnt = defaultdict(int)
    for t in closed_24h:
        s = t.get("sector", "Other")
        sec_pnl[s] += float(t.get("pnl", 0.0))
        sec_cnt[s] += 1
    sec_sorted = sorted(sec_pnl.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1])
    sec_labels = [f"{k} ({sec_cnt[k]})" for k, _ in sec_sorted] or ["Нет данных"]
    sec_values = [round(v, 2) for _, v in sec_sorted] or [0.0]
    sec_colors = ["#00e676" if v >= 0 else "#ff1744" for v in sec_values]

    # 3. Распределение выходов
    exit_cnt = defaultdict(int)
    for t in closed_24h:
        exit_cnt[t.get("exit_type", "🛡📉")] += 1
    exit_labels = list(exit_cnt.keys()) or ["Нет сделок"]
    exit_values = list(exit_cnt.values()) or [1]

    # --- ТАБЛИЦА 1: ОТКРЫТЫЕ ПОЗИЦИИ И ЛИМИТНЫЕ ОРДЕРА ---
    active_rows_html = []
    for sym, pos in sorted(paper.positions.items()):
        sym_base = sym[:-4] if sym.endswith("USDT") else sym
        last_p = prices.get(sym, {}).get("last", 0)
        val = pos["qty"] * last_p
        p_pct = (last_p - pos["avg"]) / pos["avg"] * 100 if pos.get("avg") and last_p else 0.0
        pnl_cls = "pos-val" if p_pct >= 0 else "neg-val"
        g_badge = "<span class='badge badge-a'>💎 A+</span>" if pos.get("grade") == "A+" else "<span class='badge badge-b'>B</span>"
        tp1_badge = "<span class='badge badge-tp1'>🔥 TP1</span>" if pos.get("tp1_done") else ""
        sl_tag = sl_emoji(pos.get("sl_state", "SHIELD"))
        sc_s = f"{pos.get('score', 0):.1f} / {pos.get('thr', 0):.2f}" if pos.get("score") else "—"
        r1h = _html.escape(f"{pos.get('gate_1h', '')} ({pos.get('reason_1h', '')})")
        corr_s = f"₿ {pos.get('corr', 0) or 0:.2f}"
        why_p = _html.escape("; ".join(pos.get("reasons", [])[:5]) or ", ".join(pos.get("reason_keys", [])))

        active_rows_html.append(
            f"<tr>"
            f"<td><span class='badge badge-open'>📦 ПОЗИЦИЯ</span></td>"
            f"<td><a class='coin-link' href='/chart?symbol={sym}&interval=60' target='_blank'>{mode_emoji(pos)} <b>{sym_base}</b></a> {TIER_EMOJI.get(pos.get('tier'), '🐭')} <span class='muted'>({pos.get('sector', 'Other')})</span></td>"
            f"<td>{pos.get('entry_mode', 'sniper')} {g_badge} {tp1_badge}</td>"
            f"<td><b>{usd(val)}</b></td>"
            f"<td>${fmt_price(pos['avg'])} &rarr; <b>${fmt_price(last_p)}</b> (<span class='{pnl_cls}'>{p_pct:+.2f}%</span>)</td>"
            f"<td>🎯 ${fmt_price(pos['tp'])}<br>{sl_tag} <b>${fmt_price(pos['sl'])}</b></td>"
            f"<td>{sc_s}</td>"
            f"<td>{r1h}<br><span class='muted'>{corr_s}</span></td>"
            f"<td class='reasons-cell'>{why_p}</td>"
            f"</tr>"
        )

    for o in paper.orders:
        sym = o["symbol"]
        sym_base = sym[:-4] if sym.endswith("USDT") else sym
        last_p = prices.get(sym, {}).get("last", 0)
        val = o["qty"] * o["price"]
        dist = (o["price"] - last_p) / last_p * 100 if last_p > 0 else 0.0
        g_badge = "<span class='badge badge-a'>💎 A+</span>" if o.get("grade") == "A+" else "<span class='badge badge-b'>B</span>"
        sc_s = f"{o.get('score', 0):.1f} / {o.get('thr', 0):.2f}" if o.get("score") else "—"
        r1h = _html.escape(f"{o.get('gate_1h', '')} ({o.get('reason_1h', '')})")
        corr_s = f"₿ {o.get('corr', 0) or 0:.2f}"
        why_o = _html.escape("; ".join(o.get("reasons", [])[:5]) or ", ".join(o.get("reason_keys", [])))

        active_rows_html.append(
            f"<tr>"
            f"<td><span class='badge badge-order'>📋 ОРДЕР</span></td>"
            f"<td><a class='coin-link' href='/chart?symbol={sym}&interval=60' target='_blank'>{mode_emoji(o)} <b>{sym_base}</b></a> {TIER_EMOJI.get(o.get('tier'), '🐭')} <span class='muted'>({o.get('sector', 'Other')})</span></td>"
            f"<td>{o.get('entry_mode', 'sniper')} {g_badge}</td>"
            f"<td><b>{usd(val)}</b></td>"
            f"<td>Лимитка <b>${fmt_price(o['price'])}</b> <span class='muted'>({dist:+.2f}% от ${fmt_price(last_p)})</span></td>"
            f"<td>🎯 ${fmt_price(o['tp'])}<br>🛡📉 ${fmt_price(o['sl'])}</td>"
            f"<td>{sc_s}</td>"
            f"<td>{r1h}<br><span class='muted'>{corr_s}</span></td>"
            f"<td class='reasons-cell'>{why_o}</td>"
            f"</tr>"
        )

    if not active_rows_html:
        active_rows_html.append("<tr><td colspan='9' class='empty-row'>Открытых позиций и активных ордеров сейчас нет</td></tr>")

    # --- ТАБЛИЦА 2: СВОДНЫЙ РЕЕСТР ПО МОНЕТАМ ЗА 24Ч ---
    by_coin = defaultdict(list)
    for t in closed_24h:
        by_coin[t["symbol"]].append(t)
    for sym in paper.positions:
        by_coin.setdefault(sym, [])

    coin_rows = []
    for sym, t_list in by_coin.items():
        sum_pct = sum(x["pnl_pct"] for x in t_list)
        sum_usd = sum(x["pnl"] for x in t_list)
        sample = t_list[-1] if t_list else paper.positions.get(sym, {})
        sec = sample.get("sector", "Other")
        t_em = TIER_EMOJI.get(sample.get("tier"), "🐭")

        trades_desc = []
        for x in t_list:
            ic = "🛡🔒" if (x.get("is_be") or is_neutral_be(x["pnl_pct"])) else ("✅" if x["pnl"] > 0 else "❌")
            gb = "💎" if x.get("grade") == "A+" else ""
            cls_c = "pos-val" if x["pnl"] > 0 else ("neg-val" if x["pnl"] < 0 else "muted")
            trades_desc.append(f"{ic} {mode_emoji(x)}{gb} <span class='{cls_c}'>{x['pnl_pct']:+.2f}%</span> ({x.get('exit_type', '')})")
        trades_str = " &middot; ".join(trades_desc) if trades_desc else "<span class='muted'>—</span>"

        pos = paper.positions.get(sym)
        if pos:
            last_p = prices.get(sym, {}).get("last", 0)
            p_pct = (last_p - pos["avg"]) / pos["avg"] * 100 if pos.get("avg") and last_p else 0.0
            p_cls = "pos-val" if p_pct >= 0 else "neg-val"
            pos_str = f"{mode_emoji(pos)}{grade_badge(pos)} <span class='{p_cls}'>{p_pct:+.2f}%</span> ({sl_emoji(pos.get('sl_state'))})"
        else:
            pos_str = "<span class='muted'>—</span>"

        coin_rows.append((sym, sec, t_em, len(t_list), trades_str, pos_str, sum_pct, sum_usd))

    coin_rows.sort(key=lambda r: r[6], reverse=True)
    coin_rows_html = []
    for sym, sec, t_em, cnt, trades_str, pos_str, sum_pct, sum_usd in coin_rows:
        sym_base = sym[:-4] if sym.endswith("USDT") else sym
        tot_cls = "pos-val" if sum_pct > 0.15 else ("neg-val" if sum_pct < -0.15 else "muted")
        coin_rows_html.append(
            f"<tr>"
            f"<td><a class='coin-link' href='/chart?symbol={sym}&interval=60' target='_blank'>📈 <b>{sym_base}</b></a></td>"
            f"<td>{sec} {t_em}</td>"
            f"<td style='text-align:center;'><b>{cnt}</b></td>"
            f"<td>{trades_str}</td>"
            f"<td>{pos_str}</td>"
            f"<td><span class='{tot_cls}'><b>{sum_pct:+.2f}%</b> ({usd(sum_usd)})</span></td>"
            f"</tr>"
        )

    if not coin_rows_html:
        coin_rows_html.append("<tr><td colspan='6' class='empty-row'>Нет данных по монетам за 24 часа</td></tr>")

    # --- ТАБЛИЦА 3: ХРОНОЛОГИЧЕСКИЙ ЖУРНАЛ ВСЕХ СДЕЛОК (24Ч) ---
    trades_rows_html = []
    for idx, t in enumerate(closed_24h, 1):
        sym = t["symbol"]
        sym_base = sym[:-4] if sym.endswith("USDT") else sym
        t_em = TIER_EMOJI.get(t.get("tier"), "🐭")
        sec = t.get("sector", "Other")
        m_em = mode_emoji(t)
        emode = t.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
        g_badge = "<span class='badge badge-a'>💎 A+</span>" if t.get("grade") == "A+" else "<span class='badge badge-b'>B</span>"
        reg_str = (t.get("regime") or "neutral").upper()
        rmode = t.get("risk_mode", "NORMAL")

        t_ord = _fmt_ts(t.get("order_created"), tz)
        t_in = _fmt_ts(t.get("entry_time"), tz)
        t_out = _fmt_ts(t.get("time"), tz)

        sc = t.get("score", 0.0)
        thr = t.get("thr", 0.0)
        sc_str = f"<b>{sc:.1f}</b> / {thr:.2f}" if sc > 0 else "—"

        g1h = _html.escape(t.get("gate_1h") or "1H_OK")
        r1h = _html.escape(t.get("reason_1h") or "—")
        corr_v = t.get("corr")
        corr_s = f"₿ {corr_v:.2f}" if corr_v is not None else ""

        reasons_list = t.get("reasons") or t.get("reason_keys") or []
        why_str = _html.escape("; ".join(str(x) for x in reasons_list[:6])) if reasons_list else "—"

        ep = t.get("entry_price")
        xp = t.get("exit_price")
        itp = t.get("initial_tp")
        isl = t.get("initial_sl")
        if ep and xp:
            prices_str = f"${fmt_price(ep)} &rarr; <b>${fmt_price(xp)}</b>"
            if itp and isl:
                prices_str += f"<br><span class='muted'>TP ${fmt_price(itp)} / SL ${fmt_price(isl)}</span>"
        else:
            prices_str = "—"

        is_be_flag = t.get("is_be") or is_neutral_be(t["pnl_pct"])
        res_icon = "🛡🔒" if is_be_flag else ("✅" if t["pnl"] > 0 else "❌")
        pnl_cls = "pos-val" if t["pnl"] > 0 else ("neg-val" if t["pnl"] < 0 else "muted")
        tp1_mark = " + <span class='badge badge-tp1'>🔥TP1</span>" if t.get("tp1_done") else ""

        trades_rows_html.append(
            f"<tr>"
            f"<td style='text-align:center;'>{idx}</td>"
            f"<td><a class='coin-link' href='/chart?symbol={sym}&interval=60' target='_blank'>📈 <b>{sym_base}</b></a> {t_em}<br><span class='muted'>{sec}</span></td>"
            f"<td>{t_ord} / {t_in} / <b>{t_out}</b></td>"
            f"<td>{m_em} {emode} {g_badge}</td>"
            f"<td>{reg_str}<br><span class='muted'>{rmode}</span></td>"
            f"<td>{sc_str}</td>"
            f"<td><b>{g1h}</b><br><span class='muted'>{r1h} {corr_s}</span></td>"
            f"<td class='reasons-cell'>{why_str}</td>"
            f"<td>{prices_str}</td>"
            f"<td>{res_icon} <span class='{pnl_cls}'><b>{t['pnl_pct']:+.2f}%</b> ({usd(t['pnl'])})</span><br>{t.get('exit_type', '')}{tp1_mark}</td>"
            f"</tr>"
        )

    if not trades_rows_html:
        trades_rows_html.append("<tr><td colspan='10' class='empty-row'>За последние 24 часа закрытых сделок нет</td></tr>")

    pf_val = metrics_24h["profit_factor"]
    pf_str = "—" if pf_val is None else ("∞" if pf_val == float("inf") else f"{pf_val:.2f}")
    tot_pnl_24 = metrics_24h["total_pnl"]
    tot_pnl_cls = "pos-val" if tot_pnl_24 >= 0 else "neg-val"
    curr_reg = bot_state.current_regime.upper()

    html_page = f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ru">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Капитан Рост — Интерактивный Аудит 24ч</title>
<script src="https://cdn.plot.ly/plotly-2.32.0.min.js"></script>
<style>
  :root {{
    --bg: #0f1218;
    --card: #171c26;
    --border: #262f40;
    --text: #e6edf3;
    --muted: #8b949e;
    --accent: #2962ff;
    --green: #00e676;
    --red: #ff5252;
  }}
  * {{ box-sizing: border-box; }}
  body {{
    margin: 0; padding: 20px;
    background: var(--bg); color: var(--text);
    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Arial, sans-serif;
    font-size: 13.5px;
  }}
  .header-bar {{
    display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;
    background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--border);
    padding: 14px 20px; border-radius: 10px; margin-bottom: 18px;
    flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px;
  }}
  .header-title {{ font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; }}
  .btn {{
    text-decoration: none; color: #fff; background: var(--accent);
    padding: 8px 14px; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: 600; font-size: 13px;
  }}
  .kpi-grid {{
    display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(185px, 1fr));
    gap: 12px; margin-bottom: 18px;
  }}
  .kpi-card {{
    background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--border);
    padding: 12px 15px; border-radius: 8px;
  }}
  .kpi-label {{ color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; margin-bottom: 5px; }}
  .kpi-val {{ font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; }}
  .charts-grid {{
    display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(340px, 1fr));
    gap: 14px; margin-bottom: 22px;
  }}
  .chart-box {{
    background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--border);
    border-radius: 10px; padding: 8px; min-height: 300px;
  }}
  .section-title {{
    font-size: 15px; font-weight: 700; margin: 22px 0 10px;
    padding-left: 10px; border-left: 3px solid var(--accent);
  }}
  .table-wrap {{
    background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--border);
    border-radius: 10px; overflow-x: auto; margin-bottom: 20px;
  }}
  table {{ width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; min-width: 850px; }}
  th, td {{
    padding: 9px 11px; text-align: left;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); vertical-align: top;
  }}
  th {{ background: #1d2432; color: #b8c4d4; font-weight: 600; font-size: 12px; text-transform: uppercase; }}
  tr:hover td {{ background: rgba(41, 98, 255, 0.06); }}

async def build_report(period, tz):
    title, days = PERIODS.get(period, PERIODS["daily"])
    prices = await market_data.get_tickers()
    eq = paper.equity(prices)
    now = datetime.now(tz)

    if period == "daily":
        start_dt = now.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    else:
        start_dt = now - timedelta(days=days)
    start_ts = int(start_dt.timestamp())

    trades = [
        r for r in paper.realized
        if not r.get("partial") and r.get("time", 0) >= start_ts
    ]

    be_trades = [r for r in trades if r.get("is_be") or is_neutral_be(r.get("pnl_pct", 0.0))]
    decisive = [r for r in trades if r not in be_trades]
    wins = [r for r in decisive if r["pnl"] > 0]
    losses = [r for r in decisive if r["pnl"] <= 0]
    total_pnl = sum(r["pnl"] for r in trades)
    clean_wr = (len(wins) / len(decisive) * 100) if decisive else 0.0

    locked_usdt = sum(o["qty"] * o["price"] for o in paper.orders)
    avail_usdt = max(0.0, paper.usdt - locked_usdt)
    free_pct = avail_usdt / eq * 100 if eq else 0

    lines = [f"📅 <b>{title}</b> 🕘", ""]
    lines.append(f"💰 Свободно: <b>{usd(avail_usdt)}</b> ({free_pct:.0f}%)")
    if locked_usdt > 0:
        lines.append(f"🔒 В ордерах: <b>{usd(locked_usdt)}</b>")
    lines.append(f"🏦 Накопления: <b>{usd(paper.funding)}</b>")
    lines.append(f"📈 Капитал: <b>{usd(eq)}</b>")
    lines.append("")

    history = [h for h in getattr(paper, "market_history", []) if h.get("ts", 0) >= start_ts]
    if history:
        total_h = len(history)
        r_counts, m_counts = {}, {}
        for h in history:
            r, m = h.get("regime", "neutral"), h.get("mode", "NORMAL")
            r_counts[r] = r_counts.get(r, 0) + 1
            m_counts[m] = m_counts.get(m, 0) + 1

        r_map = {"bull": "🟢", "neutral": "🟡", "bear": "🔴"}
        m_map = {"NORMAL": "🟢 NORMAL", "CAUTIOUS": "🟡 CAUTIOUS", "STRICT": "🔴 STRICT", "AGGRESSIVE": "🚀 AGGRESSIVE"}

        r_str = " · ".join(f"{r_map.get(k, k)} {v/total_h*100:.0f}%" for k, v in sorted(r_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True))
        m_str = " · ".join(f"{m_map.get(k, k)} {v/total_h*100:.0f}%" for k, v in sorted(m_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True))

        lines.append(f"🌍 <b>Фон:</b> 🧭 {r_str} | 🎚 {m_str}")
        lines.append("")

    lines.append(
        f"📊 <b>Сделки ({len(trades)}):</b> ✅ {len(wins)} · ❌ {len(losses)} · 🛡🔒 {len(be_trades)} "
        f"<i>(чистый WR {clean_wr:.0f}%)</i>"
    )
    lines.append(f"💵 PnL: {pnl_emoji(total_pnl)} <b>{usd(total_pnl)}</b>")

    if trades:
        exit_counts = {}
        for r in trades:
            et = r.get("exit_type", "🛡📉")
            exit_counts[et] = exit_counts.get(et, 0) + 1
        ex_str = " · ".join(f"{k}: {v}" for k, v in sorted(exit_counts.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True))
        lines.append(f"🚪 <b>Выходы:</b> {ex_str}")

    if decisive:
        best = max(decisive, key=lambda r: r["pnl_pct"])
        worst = min(decisive, key=lambda r: r["pnl_pct"])
        lines.append(f"🏆 {format_coin(best['symbol'], best)}{grade_badge(best)} · {pnl_emoji(best['pnl_pct'])} {best['pnl_pct']:+.1f}% ({best.get('exit_type', '')})")
        lines.append(f"📉 {format_coin(worst['symbol'], worst)}{grade_badge(worst)} · {pnl_emoji(worst['pnl_pct'])} {worst['pnl_pct']:+.1f}% ({worst.get('exit_type', '')})")
    lines.append("")

    a_trades = [r for r in trades if r.get("grade") == "A+"]
    a_stats_all = learner.get_grade_a_stats()
    lines.append("💎 <b>Элитные сетапы [💎]</b>")
    if a_trades:
        a_be = [r for r in a_trades if r.get("is_be") or is_neutral_be(r.get("pnl_pct", 0.0))]
        a_dec = [r for r in a_trades if r not in a_be]
        a_wins = [r for r in a_dec if r["pnl"] > 0]
        a_wr = (len(a_wins) / len(a_dec) * 100) if a_dec else 0.0
        a_pnl = sum(r["pnl"] for r in a_trades)
        lines.append(
            f"   За период: {len(a_trades)} (✅ {len(a_wins)} / ❌ {len(a_dec)-len(a_wins)} / 🛡🔒 {len(a_be)}) "
            f"· WR {a_wr:.0f}% · {usd(a_pnl)}"
        )
    lines.append(
        f"   ИИ-доверие [💎]: <b>×{a_stats_all['mult']:.2f}</b> "
        f"(серия {a_stats_all['streak']:+d} · общий WR {a_stats_all['wr']:.0%} за {a_stats_all['decisive']} сдел.)"
    )
    lines.append("")

    sector_agg = {}
    for r in decisive:
        sector_agg.setdefault(r.get("sector", "Other"), []).append(r["pnl_pct"])
    lines.append("🧭 <b>Сектора</b>")
    if sector_agg:
        rows = [(s, len(v), sum(v) / len(v)) for s, v in sector_agg.items()]
        rows.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)
        for s, cnt, avg in rows[:6]:
            lines.append(f"   {pnl_emoji(avg)} <i>{s}</i> · {cnt} · {avg:+.2f}%")
    else:
        lines.append("   <i>(нет результативных сделок)</i>")
    lines.append("")

    if paper.positions:
        lines.append(f"📦 <b>Позиции ({len(paper.positions)})</b>")
        for sym, pos in paper.positions.items():
            last = prices.get(sym, {}).get("last", 0)
            pnl_pct = (last - pos["avg"]) / pos["avg"] * 100 if pos["avg"] and last else 0
            tp_tag = " · 🔥 TP1 · TP🏃🏻‍➡️" if pos.get("tp1_done") else ""
            sl_tag = sl_emoji(pos.get("sl_state", "SHIELD"))
            lines.append(
                f"   {format_coin(sym, pos)}{grade_badge(pos)} · "
                f"{pnl_emoji(pnl_pct)} {fmt_pct(pnl_pct)} · {sl_tag} {fmt_price(pos['sl'])}{tp_tag}"
            )
    else:
        lines.append("📦 <b>Позиции</b>: нет")

    return "\n".join(lines)

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/core/__init__.py ---
# пакет core

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/core/__init__.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/core/event_bus.py ---
import asyncio
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Dict, Set
from loguru import logger

@dataclass
class Event:
    type: str
    payload: Any

class EventBus:
    def __init__(self):
        # Храним множество очередей для каждого типа события
        self.subscribers: Dict[str, Set[asyncio.Queue]] = defaultdict(set)

    def subscribe(self, event_type: str, maxsize: int = 100) -> asyncio.Queue:
        """
        Регистрация слушателя. maxsize защищает от утечек памяти (OOM) 
        и очередей из устаревших данных (например, потока цен).
        """
        queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=maxsize)
        self.subscribers[event_type].add(queue)
        logger.debug(f"EventBus: Создана подписка на событие '{event_type}'")
        return queue

    def publish(self, event_type: str, payload: Any) -> None:
        """
        Синхронный publish (non-blocking). 
        Позволяет публиковать события без await, мгновенно отдавая их в Event Loop.
        """
        if event_type not in self.subscribers:
            return

        event = Event(type=event_type, payload=payload)
        for queue in self.subscribers[event_type]:
            try:
                queue.put_nowait(event)
            except asyncio.QueueFull:
                # Паттерн "Drop Oldest": если очередь полна, выкидываем старое событие,
                # чтобы воркер получил самую актуальную информацию (важно для цен)
                try:
                    queue.get_nowait()
                    queue.put_nowait(event)
                except (asyncio.QueueEmpty, asyncio.QueueFull):
                    pass

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/core/event_bus.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/core/remote_state.py ---
import os
import json
import base64
import time
import threading
import queue

import httpx
from loguru import logger

GITHUB_API = "https://api.github.com"
_last_get_sha = {}

# Очередь задач на выгрузку, чтобы не блокировать Event Loop
_upload_queue = queue.Queue()


def _headers():
    token = os.getenv("GITHUB_TOKEN", "").strip()
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}", "Accept": "application/vnd.github+json"} if token else {}


def _repo():
    return os.getenv("GITHUB_REPO", "").strip()


def _do(method, url, **kwargs):
    try:
        with httpx.Client(timeout=15) as c:
            r = c.request(method, url, headers=_headers(), **kwargs)
        return r.status_code, r.json() if r.content else {}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"remote_state request error: {e}")
        return 0, {}


def ensure_branch():
    """Создаёт ветку learner-state, если её нет."""
    repo = _repo()
    if not repo or not os.getenv("GITHUB_TOKEN"):
        return
    code, main_ref = _do("GET", f"{GITHUB_API}/repos/{repo}/git/ref/heads/main")
    if code != 200:
        logger.error(f"remote_state: не удалось получить main ref: {code}")
        return
    sha = main_ref.get("object", {}).get("sha")
    code, _ = _do("POST", f"{GITHUB_API}/repos/{repo}/git/refs",
                  json={"ref": "refs/heads/learner-state", "sha": sha})
    if code in (201, 422):
        logger.info("remote_state: ветка learner-state готова")


def download_state(path):
    """Скачать JSON-файл из ветки learner-state."""
    repo = _repo()
    if not repo or not os.getenv("GITHUB_TOKEN"):
        return None
    try:
        code, data = _do("GET", f"{GITHUB_API}/repos/{repo}/contents/{path}",
                         params={"ref": "learner-state"})
        if code != 200:
            return None
        raw = base64.b64decode(data.get("content", "")).decode()
        return json.loads(raw)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"remote_state download error: {e}")
        return None


def _sync_upload_state(path, payload):
    """Выполняет реальную выгрузку в GitHub (Синхронно)"""
    repo = _repo()
    content = base64.b64encode(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False).encode()).decode()
    message = f"auto: update {path}"

    for attempt in range(3):
        # Запрашиваем свежий SHA файла перед апдейтом
        code, data = _do("GET", f"{GITHUB_API}/repos/{repo}/contents/{path}", params={"ref": "learner-state"})
        sha = data.get("sha") if code == 200 else _last_get_sha.get(path)

        body = {"message": message, "content": content, "branch": "learner-state"}
        if sha:
            body["sha"] = sha

        code, data = _do("PUT", f"{GITHUB_API}/repos/{repo}/contents/{path}", json=body)

        if code in (200, 201):
            new_sha = data.get("content", {}).get("sha")
            if new_sha:
                _last_get_sha[path] = new_sha
            return

        if code == 409:
            logger.warning(f"remote_state: 409 conflict on {path}, retry {attempt+1}/3")
            time.sleep(1.5)  # Спим в фоновом потоке, не вешая бота
            continue

        logger.error(f"remote_state upload error: {code} {data}")
        return


def _upload_worker():
    """Разгребает очередь выгрузок строго по одному."""
    while True:
        path, payload = _upload_queue.get()
        try:
            _sync_upload_state(path, payload)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"upload_worker error: {e}")
        finally:
            _upload_queue.task_done()


# Запускаем один фоновый поток (Daemon) при старте модуля
threading.Thread(target=_upload_worker, daemon=True).start()


def upload_state(path, payload):
    """Отправляет задачу в очередь, моментально возвращая управление Event Loop'у."""
    if os.getenv("GITHUB_TOKEN"):
        _upload_queue.put((path, payload))

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/core/remote_state.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/core/state.py ---
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path
from loguru import logger

PAUSED_FILE = Path("storage/paused_orders.json")


class BotState:
    def __init__(self):
        self.trading_enabled = True
        self.paused = False
        self.fomo_cooldowns = {}
        self.cooldown_meta = {}      # sym -> {"structural_reset": bool, "reason": str, "ts": int}
        self.consecutive_sl = {}     # sym -> count of consecutive stop-losses
        self.last_sl_price = {}      # sym -> price of last stop-loss
        self.current_regime = "neutral"

    def set_cooldown(self, sym: str, seconds: int, structural_reset: bool = False, reason: str = ""):
        until_ts = int(time.time()) + int(seconds)
        self.fomo_cooldowns[sym] = until_ts
        self.cooldown_meta[sym] = {
            "structural_reset": structural_reset,
            "reason": reason,
            "set_at": int(time.time()),
            "until": until_ts,
        }

    def record_trade_exit(self, sym: str, pnl_pct: float, exit_price: float, is_sl: bool = False):
        """
        Блок 4: Прогрессивный карантин монеты (Escalating Cooldown) со структурным сбросом:
        - Плюс или БУ (>= -0.15%) -> пауза 15 минут (900с), сброс серии стопов.
        - 1-й стоп-лосс -> пауза 45 минут (2700с), фиксация уровня стопа.
        - 2-й и более стоп подряд -> карантин до 4 часов (14400с) с правом досрочного
          структурного сброса при возврате цены над EMA 21 (кейс WAL).
        """
        if not is_sl or pnl_pct >= -0.15:
            self.consecutive_sl[sym] = 0
            self.last_sl_price.pop(sym, None)
            self.set_cooldown(sym, 900, structural_reset=True, reason="win_or_be")
            return

        streak = self.consecutive_sl.get(sym, 0) + 1
        self.consecutive_sl[sym] = streak
        self.last_sl_price[sym] = float(exit_price)

        if streak == 1:
            self.set_cooldown(sym, 2700, structural_reset=True, reason="sl_1")
            logger.info(f"Cooldown {sym}: 1-й стоп -> пауза 45 мин (или до пробоя EMA21 наверх)")
        else:
            self.set_cooldown(sym, 14400, structural_reset=True, reason=f"sl_{streak}")
            logger.info(f"Cooldown {sym}: {streak}-й стоп подряд -> карантин до 4ч (со структурным сбросом над EMA21)")

    def check_structural_reset(self, sym: str, last_price: float, ema21_15m: float, ema50_15m: float = 0.0) -> bool:
        """
        Если монета на карантине после стопов, но уже сформировала базу (минимум 15 мин прошло)
        и пробила EMA 21 наверх на 15m — карантин автоматически сгорает!
        """
        if sym not in self.fomo_cooldowns:
            return True

        now = time.time()
        if now > self.fomo_cooldowns[sym]:
            self.fomo_cooldowns.pop(sym, None)
            self.cooldown_meta.pop(sym, None)
            return True

        meta = self.cooldown_meta.get(sym, {})
        if not meta.get("structural_reset"):
            return False

        # Минимальная пауза 15 минут после стопа для формирования свежей базы
        elapsed = now - meta.get("set_at", 0)
        if elapsed < 900:
            return False

        if ema21_15m > 0 and last_price > ema21_15m * 1.0015:
            if ema50_15m <= 0 or last_price >= ema50_15m * 0.995:
                logger.info(f"🔓 Структурный сброс карантина {sym}: цена ${last_price:.4f} закрепилась над EMA21 ${ema21_15m:.4f}")
                self.fomo_cooldowns.pop(sym, None)
                self.cooldown_meta.pop(sym, None)
                return True

        return False

    def can_enter_reversal(self, sym: str, current_price: float, ema21_15m: float) -> tuple:
        """
        Блокировка повторной ловли ножа: запрет нового входа reversal,
        пока цена ниже уровня предыдущего стопа и не вернулась выше EMA 21 на 15m.
        """
        last_sl = self.last_sl_price.get(sym)
        if last_sl and current_price < last_sl:
            if ema21_15m <= 0 or current_price < ema21_15m:
                return False, f"цена ниже прошлого стопа (${last_sl:.4f}) и под EMA21"
        return True, "OK"

    def is_on_cooldown(self, sym: str) -> bool:
        if sym not in self.fomo_cooldowns:
            return False
        if time.time() > self.fomo_cooldowns[sym]:
            del self.fomo_cooldowns[sym]
            self.cooldown_meta.pop(sym, None)
            return False
        return True

    def pause(self, orders):
        self.paused = True
        try:
            PAUSED_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            PAUSED_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(orders, ensure_ascii=False))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"pause save error: {e}")

    def resume(self):
        self.paused = False
        orders = []
        try:
            if PAUSED_FILE.exists():
                orders = json.loads(PAUSED_FILE.read_text())
                PAUSED_FILE.unlink()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"resume load error: {e}")
        return orders

    def fresh_start(self):
        self.paused = False
        self.trading_enabled = True
        self.fomo_cooldowns.clear()
        self.cooldown_meta.clear()
        self.consecutive_sl.clear()
        self.last_sl_price.clear()
        try:
            if PAUSED_FILE.exists():
                PAUSED_FILE.unlink()
        except Exception:
            pass


bot_state = BotState()
