--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/exchange/bybit_client.py ---
import os
import asyncio

from loguru import logger


class BybitClient:
    """Клиент Bybit API v5 через официальную библиотеку pybit."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.api_key = os.getenv("BYBIT_API_KEY", "").strip()
        self.api_secret = os.getenv("BYBIT_API_SECRET", "").strip()
        self.testnet = os.getenv("BYBIT_TESTNET", "true").lower() == "true"
        self.base_url = (
            "https://api-testnet.bybit.com" if self.testnet else "https://api.bybit.com"
        )
        self.last_error = None

    def _session(self):
        from pybit.unified_trading import HTTP

        return HTTP(
            testnet=self.testnet,
            api_key=self.api_key,
            api_secret=self.api_secret,
            recv_window=5000,
            logging_level="ERROR",
        )

    async def get_wallet_balance(self, account_type: str):
        """Возвращает словарь кошелька или None."""
        self.last_error = None

        def _call():
            session = self._session()
            return session.get_wallet_balance(accountType=account_type)

        try:
            data = await asyncio.to_thread(_call)
        except Exception as e:
            self.last_error = str(e)
            logger.error(f"Bybit (pybit) ошибка: {e}")
            return None

        if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get("retCode") != 0:
            self.last_error = f"retCode={data.get('retCode')}, retMsg={data.get('retMsg')}"
            logger.error(f"Bybit API error: {data}")
            return None

        try:
            return data["result"]["list"][0]
        except Exception:
            self.last_error = f"неожиданный ответ: {str(data)[:200]}"
            logger.error(f"Bybit unexpected response: {data}")
            return None

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/exchange/bybit_client.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/exchange/market_data.py ---
import asyncio
import time
import httpx
from loguru import logger

MAINNET_PUBLIC = "https://api.bybit.com"

_tickers_cache = {}
_tickers_ts = 0
_kline_cache = {} # <-- ДОБАВИЛИ КЭШ ДЛЯ СВЕЧЕЙ

class MarketData:
    """Рыночные данные Bybit (Надежный REST API с пулом соединений)."""

    def __init__(self, base_url: str = MAINNET_PUBLIC):
        self.base_url = base_url
        self._client = None

    @property
    def client(self):
        # Ленивая инициализация клиента для удержания единого TCP/TLS туннеля (Keep-Alive)
        if self._client is None:
            limits = httpx.Limits(max_keepalive_connections=20, max_connections=50)
            self._client = httpx.AsyncClient(
                base_url=self.base_url,
                timeout=20,
                limits=limits
            )
        return self._client

    async def get_tickers(self) -> dict:
        global _tickers_cache, _tickers_ts
        now = time.time()
        
        if _tickers_cache and now - _tickers_ts < 5:
            return _tickers_cache
        
        try:
            resp = await self.client.get("/v5/market/tickers", params={"category": "spot"})
            data = resp.json()
            if data.get("retCode") != 0:
                logger.error(f"Tickers error: {data}")
                return _tickers_cache
            
            result = {}
            for t in data.get("result", {}).get("list", []):
                if t["symbol"].endswith("USDT"):
                    result[t["symbol"]] = {
                        "last": float(t["lastPrice"]),
                        "bid1": float(t.get("bid1Price") or t["lastPrice"]),
                        "ask1": float(t.get("ask1Price") or t["lastPrice"]),
                        "quote_volume": float(t.get("turnover24h", 0)),
                        "change_pct": float(t.get("price24hPcnt", 0)) * 100,
                        "high": float(t.get("highPrice24h", 0)),
                        "low": float(t.get("lowPrice24h", 0)),
                    }
            if result:
                _tickers_cache = result
                _tickers_ts = now
            return _tickers_cache
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"REST get_tickers error: {e}")
            return _tickers_cache

    async def get_derivatives_tickers(self) -> dict:
        try:
            resp = await self.client.get("/v5/market/tickers", params={"category": "linear"})
            data = resp.json()
            if data.get("retCode") != 0:
                return {}
            result = {}
            for t in data.get("result", {}).get("list", []):
                if t["symbol"].endswith("USDT"):
                    result[t["symbol"]] = {
                        "oi": float(t.get("openInterest") or 0),
                        "funding": float(t.get("fundingRate") or 0) * 100,
                    }
            return result
        except Exception:
            return {}

    async def get_kline(self, symbol: str, interval: str = "15", limit: int = 200) -> list:
        global _kline_cache
        cache_key = f"{symbol}_{interval}_{limit}"
        now = time.time()
        
        # Часовики ("60") кэшируем на 15 минут (900 сек). 15-минутки не кэшируем.
        ttl = 900 if interval == "60" else 0
        
        if ttl > 0 and cache_key in _kline_cache and now - _kline_cache[cache_key]['ts'] < ttl:
            return _kline_cache[cache_key]['data']

        try:
            resp = await self.client.get(
                "/v5/market/kline",
                params={"category": "spot", "symbol": symbol, "interval": interval, "limit": limit},
            )
            data = resp.json()
            if data.get("retCode") != 0:
                return []
            candles = []
            for r in data["result"]["list"]:
                candles.append({
                    "ts": int(r[0]),
                    "open": float(r[1]),
                    "high": float(r[2]),
                    "low": float(r[3]),
                    "close": float(r[4]),
                    "volume": float(r[5]),
                })
            candles.reverse()
            
            # Сохраняем в кэш, если это часовик
            if ttl > 0:
                _kline_cache[cache_key] = {'data': candles, 'ts': now}
                
            return candles
        except Exception:
            return []

async def start_ws_ticker_stream():
    logger.info("ℹ️ WebSocket стрим отключен. Перешли на пуленепробиваемый REST-кэш (5 сек).")
    while True:
        await asyncio.sleep(3600)

market_data = MarketData()

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/exchange/market_data.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/exchange/paper_exchange.py ---
import os
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path
from collections import defaultdict

from loguru import logger

from bot.strategy.learner import learner, is_neutral_be
from bot.core.remote_state import download_state, upload_state

REMOTE_PATH = "paper_state.json"
MAX_EVENTS_PER_SYMBOL = 150
MAX_TOTAL_EVENTS = 5000


class PaperExchange:
    """Виртуальная биржа с сохранением полной аналитики сделок (1H Gatekeeper, reasons, тайминги) для 24ч аудита."""

    FEE_PCT = 0.10

    def __init__(self, start_usdt: float = 1000.0):
        self.state_file = Path(os.getenv("STORAGE_DIR", "storage")) / "paper_state.json"
        self.start_usdt = start_usdt
        self.usdt = start_usdt
        self.funding = 0.0
        self.positions = {}
        self.orders = []
        self.trades = []
        self.funding_coins = {}
        self.realized = []
        self.market_history = []
        self.chart_events = []
        self._last_upload = 0.0
        self._load()

    def _load(self):
        data = None
        try:
            if self.state_file.exists():
                data = json.loads(self.state_file.read_text())
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Paper load error: {e}")
        if data is None:
            data = download_state(REMOTE_PATH)
        if data:
            self.usdt = data.get("usdt", self.start_usdt)
            self.funding = data.get("funding", 0.0)
            self.positions = data.get("positions", {})
            self.orders = data.get("orders", [])
            self.trades = data.get("trades", [])
            self.funding_coins = data.get("funding_coins", {})
            self.realized = data.get("realized", [])
            self.market_history = data.get("market_history", [])
            self.chart_events = data.get("chart_events", [])
            logger.info(f"Paper state загружен: USDT={self.usdt:.2f}, позиций={len(self.positions)}")
        else:
            self.chart_events = []

    def _prune_chart_events(self) -> list:
        if not hasattr(self, "chart_events") or not self.chart_events:
            return []
        by_sym = defaultdict(list)
        for ev in self.chart_events:
            by_sym[ev.get("sym", "UNKNOWN")].append(ev)

        pruned = []
        for sym, ev_list in by_sym.items():
            pruned.extend(ev_list[-MAX_EVENTS_PER_SYMBOL:])

        pruned.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("ts", 0))
        self.chart_events = pruned[-MAX_TOTAL_EVENTS:]
        return self.chart_events

    def save(self):
        payload = {
            "usdt": self.usdt,
            "funding": self.funding,
            "positions": self.positions,
            "orders": self.orders,
            "trades": self.trades,
            "funding_coins": getattr(self, "funding_coins", {}),
            "realized": self.realized,
            "market_history": self.market_history,
            "chart_events": self._prune_chart_events(),
        }
        try:
            self.state_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            self.state_file.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Paper save error: {e}")
        if time.time() - self._last_upload > 3600:
            self._last_upload = time.time()
            upload_state(REMOTE_PATH, payload)

    def log_event(self, symbol, ev_type, price, text="", rsi_val=None, mode=None, tp=None, sl=None, sup=None, res=None):
        if not hasattr(self, "chart_events"):
            self.chart_events = []
        self.chart_events.append({
            "ts": int(time.time()),
            "sym": symbol,
            "type": ev_type,
            "price": price,
            "text": text,
            "rsi": round(rsi_val, 1) if rsi_val else None,
            "mode": mode,
            "tp": round(float(tp), 8) if tp is not None else None,
            "sl": round(float(sl), 8) if sl is not None else None,
            "sup": round(float(sup), 8) if sup is not None else None,
            "res": round(float(res), 8) if res is not None else None,
        })

    def _resolve_sector(self, symbol, fallback_sector=None):
        if fallback_sector and fallback_sector != "Other":
            return fallback_sector
        try:
            from bot.news.cmc import sector_of
            base = symbol[:-4] if symbol.endswith("USDT") else symbol
            return sector_of(base) or "Other"
        except Exception:
            return "Other"

    def place_limit_buy(self, symbol, qty, price, tp, sl, score=0, reason_keys=None):
        now_ts = int(time.time())
        order = {
            "id": f"paper-{int(time.time() * 1000)}",
            "side": "Buy",
            "symbol": symbol,
            "qty": qty,
            "price": price,
            "tp": tp,
            "sl": sl,
            "score": score,
            "initial_score": score,
            "reason_keys": reason_keys or [],
            "requotes": 0,
            "created": now_ts,
            "last_move_ts": now_ts,
        }
        self.orders.append(order)
        self.save()
        return order

    def cancel_order(self, order_id):
        self.orders = [o for o in self.orders if o["id"] != order_id]
        self.save()

    def check_fills(self, prices):
        fills = []
        for order in list(self.orders):
            last = prices.get(order["symbol"], {}).get("last")
            if last is None:
                continue
            if order["side"] == "Buy" and last <= order["price"]:
                cost = order["qty"] * order["price"]
                fee = cost * self.FEE_PCT / 100
                if cost + fee <= self.usdt:
                    self.usdt -= cost + fee
                    pos = self.positions.setdefault(order["symbol"], {"qty": 0.0, "avg": 0.0})
                    total = pos["qty"] + order["qty"]
                    pos["avg"] = (pos["avg"] * pos["qty"] + cost) / total
                    pos["qty"] = total
                    pos["tp"] = order["tp"]
                    pos["sl"] = order["sl"]
                    pos["initial_tp"] = order["tp"]
                    pos["initial_sl"] = order["sl"]
                    pos["max_sl"] = order["sl"]
                    pos["score"] = order.get("score", 0)
                    pos["thr"] = order.get("thr", 0)
                    pos["risk_mode"] = order.get("risk_mode", getattr(learner, "current_risk_mode", "NORMAL"))
                    pos["gate_1h"] = order.get("gate_1h", "1H_TREND_OK")
                    pos["reason_1h"] = order.get("reason_1h", "")
                    pos["chg_48h"] = order.get("chg_48h", 0.0)
                    pos["pos_in_48h"] = order.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5)
                    pos["corr"] = order.get("corr")
                    pos["reasons"] = order.get("reasons", [])
                    pos["reason_keys"] = order.get("reason_keys", [])
                    pos["patterns"] = order.get("patterns", [])
                    pos["grade"] = order.get("grade", "B")
                    pos["sector"] = self._resolve_sector(order["symbol"], order.get("sector"))
                    pos["tier"] = order.get("tier")
                    pos["regime_entry"] = order.get("regime")
                    pos["is_momentum"] = order.get("is_momentum", False)
                    pos["entry_mode"] = order.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
                    pos["max_price"] = order["price"]
                    pos["order_created"] = order.get("created", int(time.time()))
                    pos["entry_time"] = int(time.time())
                    pos["nearest_sup"] = order.get("nearest_sup")
                    pos["nearest_res"] = order.get("nearest_res")
                    pos["sup_touches"] = order.get("sup_touches", 0)
                    pos["res_touches"] = order.get("res_touches", 0)
                    pos["sl_state"] = order.get("sl_state", "SHIELD")
                    pos["_last_sl_notify_ts"] = 0
                    pos["_ema21"] = order.get("_ema21", 0.0)
                    pos["_ema50"] = order.get("_ema50", 0.0)
                    pos["_atr15"] = order.get("_atr15", 0.0)
                    self.trades.append({
                        "side": "Buy",
                        "symbol": order["symbol"],
                        "qty": order["qty"],
                        "price": order["price"],
                        "time": int(time.time()),
                    })
                    self.orders.remove(order)
                    fills.append(order)
                    logger.info(f"PAPER FILL BUY {order['symbol']} {order['qty']} @ {order['price']}")
        self.save()
        return fills

    def check_exits(self, prices, regime_now=None):
        results = []
        for sym in list(self.positions):
            last = prices.get(sym, {}).get("last")
            if last is None:
                continue
            pos = self.positions[sym]
            if last >= pos["tp"]:
                results.append(self._sell(sym, pos["tp"], "TP🏃🏻‍➡️", regime_now=regime_now))
            elif last <= pos["sl"]:
                results.append(self._sell(sym, pos["sl"], "SL", regime_now=regime_now))
        return results

    def sell_partial(self, sym, qty_part, price, reason):
        pos = self.positions.get(sym)
        if not pos:
            return None
        qty_part = min(qty_part, pos["qty"])
        proceeds = qty_part * price
        cost_part = qty_part * pos["avg"]
        fee_buy = cost_part * self.FEE_PCT / 100
        fee_sell = proceeds * self.FEE_PCT / 100
        pnl = (proceeds - fee_sell) - (cost_part + fee_buy)
        pnl_pct = pnl / (cost_part + fee_buy) * 100 if cost_part else 0.0

        pos["partial_pnl"] = pos.get("partial_pnl", 0.0) + pnl
        pos["partial_cost"] = pos.get("partial_cost", 0.0) + (cost_part + fee_buy)
        pos["tp1_price"] = price
        pos["max_price"] = max(pos.get("max_price", 0.0), price)

        self.usdt += proceeds - fee_sell
        transferred = 0.0

        sector = self._resolve_sector(sym, pos.get("sector"))
        tier = pos.get("tier")
        entry_mode = pos.get("entry_mode", "rocket" if pos.get("is_momentum") else "sniper")

        pos["qty"] -= qty_part
        self.realized.append({
            "symbol": sym,
            "pnl": round(pnl, 4),
            "pnl_pct": round(pnl_pct, 2),
            "reason": reason,
            "time": int(time.time()),
            "exit_type": "🔥 TP1",
            "sector": sector,
            "tier": tier,
            "grade": pos.get("grade", "B"),
            "is_momentum": pos.get("is_momentum", False),
            "entry_mode": entry_mode,
            "regime": pos.get("regime_entry", "neutral"),
            "partial": True,
        })
        self.trades.append({
            "side": "Sell(part)",
            "symbol": sym,
            "qty": qty_part,
            "price": price,
            "time": int(time.time()),
        })
        self.save()
        return {
            "symbol": sym,
            "price": price,
            "pnl": pnl,
            "pnl_pct": pnl_pct,
            "reason": reason,
            "transferred": transferred,
            "sector": sector,
            "tier": tier,
            "grade": pos.get("grade", "B"),
            "is_momentum": pos.get("is_momentum", False),
            "entry_mode": entry_mode,
        }

    def _sell(self, sym, price, reason, regime_now=None):
        pos = self.positions.pop(sym)
        proceeds = pos["qty"] * price
        cost = pos["qty"] * pos["avg"]
        fee_buy = cost * self.FEE_PCT / 100
        fee_sell = proceeds * self.FEE_PCT / 100
        pnl_final = (proceeds - fee_sell) - (cost + fee_buy)
        pnl_final_pct = pnl_final / (cost + fee_buy) * 100 if (cost + fee_buy) else 0.0

        partial_pnl = pos.get("partial_pnl", 0.0)
        partial_cost = pos.get("partial_cost", 0.0)
        total_pnl = partial_pnl + pnl_final
        total_cost = partial_cost + (cost + fee_buy)
        total_pnl_pct = total_pnl / total_cost * 100 if total_cost else pnl_final_pct

        sl_state = pos.get("sl_state", "SHIELD")
        is_be = is_neutral_be(total_pnl_pct)
        tp1_price = pos.get("tp1_price") or pos.get("initial_tp")

        if "TP" in reason:
            exit_type = "TP🏃🏻‍➡️" if pos.get("tp1_done") else "🔥 TP1"
        elif "SL" in reason or "🛡" in reason:
            if is_be:
                exit_type = "🛡🔒"
            elif sl_state == "ABOVE_TP1" or (pos.get("tp1_done") and tp1_price and price >= tp1_price * 0.9995):
                exit_type = "🛡🔥"
            elif sl_state == "TRAILING_RUNNER":
                exit_type = "🛡📈"
            elif sl_state in ("PROFIT_LOCK", "BREAKEVEN") and total_pnl_pct > 0.25:
                exit_type = "🛡💼"
            else:
                exit_type = "🛡📉"
        else:
            exit_type = "EARLY"

        soft = False
        regime_changed = (
            regime_now is not None
            and pos.get("regime_entry")
            and regime_now != pos["regime_entry"]
        )
        if regime_changed and total_pnl < 0 and exit_type in ("🛡📉", "EARLY"):
            soft = True

        runner_bonus = 0.0
        max_price = pos.get("max_price", 0.0)
        if tp1_price and max_price > tp1_price:
            runner_bonus = (max_price - tp1_price) / tp1_price * 100

        sector = self._resolve_sector(sym, pos.get("sector"))
        tier = pos.get("tier")
        entry_mode = pos.get("entry_mode", "rocket" if pos.get("is_momentum") else "sniper")
        grade = pos.get("grade", "B")

        try:
            learner.record(
                pos.get("reason_keys", []), total_pnl > 0,
                total_pnl_pct, sector=sector, tier=tier,
                exit_type=exit_type, runner_bonus=runner_bonus,
                soft=soft, entry_mode=entry_mode,
                patterns=pos.get("patterns", []),
                grade=grade,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"learner record error: {e}")

        self.usdt += proceeds - fee_sell
        transferred = 0.0

        if total_pnl > 0 and not is_be:
            hodl_reserve_usdt = round(total_pnl * 0.30, 4)
            if tier == "TOP20" and price > 0:
                qty_kept = min(pos["qty"] * 0.5, hodl_reserve_usdt / price)
                if qty_kept > 0:
                    actual_reserved_usdt = round(qty_kept * price, 4)
                    self.usdt -= actual_reserved_usdt
                    if not hasattr(self, "funding_coins"):
                        self.funding_coins = {}
                    self.funding_coins[sym] = self.funding_coins.get(sym, 0.0) + qty_kept
                    reason += " 🏦"
            else:
                transferred = hodl_reserve_usdt
                self.usdt -= transferred
                self.funding += transferred

        exit_ts = int(time.time())
        self.realized.append({
            "symbol": sym,
            "pnl": round(total_pnl, 4),
            "pnl_pct": round(total_pnl_pct, 2),
            "reason": reason,
            "time": exit_ts,
            "order_created": pos.get("order_created", pos.get("entry_time", exit_ts)),
            "entry_time": pos.get("entry_time", exit_ts),
            "entry_price": pos.get("avg", price),
            "exit_price": price,
            "initial_tp": pos.get("initial_tp", pos.get("tp")),
            "initial_sl": pos.get("initial_sl", pos.get("sl")),
            "tp1_done": pos.get("tp1_done", False),
            "exit_type": exit_type,
            "sl_state": sl_state,
            "is_be": is_be,
            "grade": grade,
            "score": pos.get("score", 0.0),
            "thr": pos.get("thr", 0.0),
            "risk_mode": pos.get("risk_mode", "NORMAL"),
            "gate_1h": pos.get("gate_1h", ""),
            "reason_1h": pos.get("reason_1h", ""),
            "chg_48h": pos.get("chg_48h", 0.0),
            "pos_in_48h": pos.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5),
            "corr": pos.get("corr"),
            "reasons": pos.get("reasons", []),
            "reason_keys": pos.get("reason_keys", []),
            "sector": sector,
            "tier": tier,
            "is_momentum": pos.get("is_momentum", False),
            "entry_mode": entry_mode,
            "patterns": pos.get("patterns", []),
            "regime": pos.get("regime_entry", "neutral"),
        })
        self.trades.append({
            "side": "Sell", "symbol": sym, "qty": pos["qty"],
            "price": price, "time": exit_ts,
        })
        self.save()
        return {
            "symbol": sym, "price": price, "pnl": total_pnl,
            "pnl_pct": total_pnl_pct, "reason": reason, "transferred": transferred,
            "exit_type": exit_type, "sl_state": sl_state, "is_be": is_be, "grade": grade,
            "runner_bonus": runner_bonus,
            "sector": sector, "tier": tier,
            "is_momentum": pos.get("is_momentum", False),
            "entry_mode": entry_mode,
        }

    def sell_all(self, prices):
        results = []
        for sym in list(self.positions):
            last = prices.get(sym, {}).get("last")
            if last:
                results.append(self._sell(sym, last, "EXITALL 🛑"))
        self.orders = []
        self.save()
        return results

    def reset_stats(self):
        self.realized.clear()
        self.trades.clear()
        self.market_history.clear()
        self.positions.clear()
        self.orders.clear()
        self.chart_events.clear()
        if hasattr(self, "funding_coins"):
            self.funding_coins.clear()
        self.usdt = self.start_usdt
        self.funding = 0.0
        self.save()
        logger.info("paper: торговая статистика, позиции, балансы и накопления сброшены")

    def equity(self, prices):
        eq = self.usdt + self.funding
        for sym, pos in self.positions.items():
            eq += pos["qty"] * prices.get(sym, {}).get("last", 0)
        return eq

    def get_metrics(self, prices=None, hours=None):
        if prices is None:
            prices = {}

        finals = [r for r in self.realized if not r.get("partial")]

        if hours is not None:
            cutoff = int(time.time()) - int(hours * 3600)
            finals = [r for r in finals if r.get("time", 0) >= cutoff]
            partials = [r for r in self.realized if r.get("partial") and r.get("time", 0) >= cutoff]
            partial_count = len(partials)
        else:
            partial_count = len(self.realized) - len(finals)

        be_trades = [r for r in finals if r.get("is_be") or is_neutral_be(r.get("pnl_pct", 0.0))]
        decisive = [r for r in finals if r not in be_trades]
        wins = [r for r in decisive if r["pnl"] > 0]
        losses = [r for r in decisive if r["pnl"] <= 0]

        sum_win = sum(r["pnl"] for r in wins)
        sum_loss = abs(sum(r["pnl"] for r in losses))
        if sum_loss == 0:
            profit_factor = None if sum_win == 0 else float("inf")
        else:
            profit_factor = sum_win / sum_loss

        eq = self.start_usdt
        peak = eq
        max_dd = 0.0
        for r in finals:
            eq += r["pnl"]
            if eq > peak:
                peak = eq
            if peak > 0:
                dd = (peak - eq) / peak * 100
                if dd > max_dd:
                    max_dd = dd

        total_pnl = sum(r["pnl"] for r in finals)
        total_trades = len(finals)
        decisive_count = len(decisive)

        if decisive_count > 0:
            avg_win = sum_win / len(wins) if wins else 0
            avg_loss = sum_loss / len(losses) if losses else 0
            winrate = len(wins) / decisive_count
            lossrate = 1 - winrate
            expectancy = (winrate * avg_win) - (lossrate * avg_loss)

            if max_dd > 0:
                recovery_factor = total_pnl / max_dd
            else:
                recovery_factor = float("inf") if total_pnl > 0 else 0.0
        else:
            expectancy = None
            recovery_factor = None

        wr, _ = learner.winrate()
        return {
            "profit_factor": profit_factor,
            "max_drawdown_pct": max_dd,
            "winrate": wr,
            "win_count": len(wins),
            "loss_count": len(losses),
            "be_count": len(be_trades),
            "total_trades": total_trades,
            "partial_count": partial_count,
            "total_pnl": total_pnl,
            "expectancy": expectancy,
            "recovery_factor": recovery_factor,
        }


paper = PaperExchange()

import os
import time
import asyncio
import httpx
from loguru import logger

_cache = {
    "stablecoins": {"trend": "neutral", "ts": 0},
    "hot_sectors": {"data": [], "ts": 0},
    "fear_and_greed": {"value": 50, "ts": 0}
}

CACHE_TTL = 3600


async def _fetch_stablecoin_flows():
    """Анализирует приток/отток стейблкоинов (DefiLlama)."""
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15) as c:
            r = await c.get("https://stablecoins.llama.fi/stablecoincharts/all")
        data = r.json()
        if len(data) >= 8:
            today = data[-1]["totalCirculatingUSD"]["peggedUSD"]
            week_ago = data[-8]["totalCirculatingUSD"]["peggedUSD"]
            change_pct = (today - week_ago) / week_ago * 100
            
            # Реальный макро-тренд фиксируем только при значимом сдвиге ликвидности (> 1.0% / < -1.5%)
            if change_pct >= 1.0:
                _cache["stablecoins"]["trend"] = "bull"
            elif change_pct <= -1.5:
                _cache["stablecoins"]["trend"] = "bear"
            else:
                _cache["stablecoins"]["trend"] = "neutral"
            
            logger.info(f"DefiLlama | Stablecoin Flow: {change_pct:+.2f}% -> {_cache['stablecoins']['trend']}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"DefiLlama API error: {e}")


async def _fetch_defillama_sectors():
    """Смотрит, куда перетекает TVL по чейнам/секторам."""
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15) as c:
            r = await c.get("https://api.llama.fi/v2/chains")
        data = r.json()
        hot = []
        for chain in data:
            if chain.get("tvl", 0) > 100_000_000:
                chg = chain.get("tvlChange_7d", 0) or 0
                if chg > 10.0:  
                    name = chain["name"].upper()
                    if name in ("ETHEREUM", "SOLANA", "AVALANCHE", "SUI", "APTOS", "NEAR"):
                        hot.append("L1")
                    elif name in ("ARBITRUM", "OPTIMISM", "BASE", "POLYGON", "STARKNET"):
                        hot.append("L2")
        
        _cache["hot_sectors"]["data"] = list(set(hot))
        if hot:
            logger.info(f"DefiLlama | Hot Sectors (TVL Inflow): {', '.join(set(hot))}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"DefiLlama Chains API error: {e}")


async def _fetch_fear_and_greed():
    """Запрашивает индекс Страха и Жадности."""
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10) as c:
            r = await c.get("https://api.alternative.me/fng/?limit=1")
        data = r.json()
        val = int(data["data"][0]["value"])
        _cache["fear_and_greed"]["value"] = val
        logger.info(f"Alternative.me | Fear & Greed Index: {val}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"Fear & Greed API error: {e}")


async def fetch_macro_data():
    await asyncio.gather(
        _fetch_stablecoin_flows(),
        _fetch_defillama_sectors(),
        _fetch_fear_and_greed()
    )

def get_macro_trend():
    return _cache["stablecoins"]["trend"]

def is_sector_hot(sector):
    return sector in _cache["hot_sectors"]["data"]

def get_fear_and_greed():
    return _cache["fear_and_greed"]["value"]


async def check_coinglass_liquidation_threat(symbol):
    api_key = os.getenv("COINGLASS_API_KEY")
    if not api_key:
        return False
        
    base = symbol[:-4] if symbol.endswith("USDT") else symbol
    try:
        headers = {"coinglassSecret": api_key}
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10) as c:
            r = await c.get("https://open-api.coinglass.com/public/v2/indicator/long_short_ratio", 
                            params={"symbol": base, "time_type": "h1"}, headers=headers)
        data = r.json().get("data", [])
        if data and data[-1].get("longShortRatio", 1.0) > 3.0:
            return True 
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"Coinglass API error: {e}")
    return False

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/indicators.py ---
def ema(values, period):
    if not values:
        return []
    k = 2 / (period + 1)
    result = [values[0]]
    for v in values[1:]:
        result.append(v * k + result[-1] * (1 - k))
    return result


def rsi(closes, period=14):
    if len(closes) < period + 1:
        return 50.0
    gains, losses = [], []
    for i in range(1, len(closes)):
        d = closes[i] - closes[i - 1]
        gains.append(max(d, 0))
        losses.append(max(-d, 0))
    avg_gain = sum(gains[:period]) / period
    avg_loss = sum(losses[:period]) / period
    for i in range(period, len(gains)):
        avg_gain = (avg_gain * (period - 1) + gains[i]) / period
        avg_loss = (avg_loss * (period - 1) + losses[i]) / period
    if avg_loss == 0:
        return 100.0
    rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
    return 100 - 100 / (1 + rs)


def atr(candles, period=14):
    if len(candles) < 2:
        return 0.0
    trs = []
    for i in range(1, len(candles)):
        h, l, pc = candles[i]["high"], candles[i]["low"], candles[i - 1]["close"]
        trs.append(max(h - l, abs(h - pc), abs(l - pc)))
    a = sum(trs[:period]) / min(period, len(trs))
    for tr in trs[period:]:
        a = (a * (period - 1) + tr) / period
    return a


def _dedup_levels(levels):
    """Объединяет близкие уровни в пределах 0.5%, оставляя уровень с максимальным весом касаний."""
    if not levels:
        return []
    deduped = []
    for lvl in levels:
        match = next((d for d in deduped if abs(d[0] - lvl[0]) / lvl[0] <= 0.005), None)
        if match:
            if lvl[1] > match[1]:
                deduped.remove(match)
                deduped.append(lvl)
        else:
            deduped.append(lvl)
    return deduped


def _count_spaced_touches(price_target: float, points: list, tol: float = 0.005) -> int:
    """
    Считает структурный вес уровня с требованием разнесения касаний во времени:
    - Независимые часовые (1H) свинги (разнесенные минимум на 3 часа) дают вес 2.
    - Независимые 15m свинги (разнесенные минимум на 4 свечи = 1 час) дают вес 1.
    Благодаря этому 3 мелких касания на 15m внутри локальной пилы имеют вес 3 (обычный уровень),
    а статус «Капитальной стены» (вес >= 4) получают только реальные часовые и многократные плиты.
    """
    if price_target <= 0:
        return 0
    matched = [pt for pt in points if abs(pt[0] - price_target) / price_target <= tol]
    if not matched:
        return 0

    touches_weight = 0
    last_idx_by_tf = {}
    for item in sorted(matched, key=lambda x: (x[1], x[2])):
        p_val, tf_tag, idx, min_gap = item[0], item[1], item[2], item[3]
        w = item[4] if len(item) > 4 else 1
        prev_idx = last_idx_by_tf.get(tf_tag)
        if prev_idx is None or (idx - prev_idx) >= min_gap:
            touches_weight += w
            last_idx_by_tf[tf_tag] = idx
    return touches_weight


def get_all_sr_levels(candles_15m, candles_1h=None, window_15m=120, window_1h=72):
    """Возвращает подтвержденные уровни S/R (сопротивления и поддержки) за 48-72ч."""
    if not candles_15m or len(candles_15m) < 15:
        return [], []

    high_points = []
    low_points = []

    # 1. Анализ 1H свечей (разнесенные часовые фракталы имеют структурный вес 2)
    if candles_1h and isinstance(candles_1h, list) and len(candles_1h) >= 20:
        recent_1h = candles_1h[-window_1h:] if len(candles_1h) >= window_1h else candles_1h
        n1 = len(recent_1h)
        for i in range(2, n1 - 2):
            h = recent_1h[i]["high"]
            l = recent_1h[i]["low"]
            if all(h >= recent_1h[i + j]["high"] for j in (-2, -1, 1, 2)):
                high_points.append((h, "1h", i, 3, 2))
            if all(l <= recent_1h[i + j]["low"] for j in (-2, -1, 1, 2)):
                low_points.append((l, "1h", i, 3, 2))

    # 2. Анализ 15m свечей (локальные 15m фракталы имеют вес 1, шаг >= 4 свечи)
    recent_15m = candles_15m[-window_15m:] if len(candles_15m) >= window_15m else candles_15m
    n15 = len(recent_15m)
    for i in range(3, n15 - 1):
        h = recent_15m[i]["high"]
        l = recent_15m[i]["low"]
        if all(h >= recent_15m[i - j]["high"] for j in (1, 2, 3)) and h >= recent_15m[i + 1]["high"]:
            high_points.append((h, "15m", i, 4, 1))
        if all(l <= recent_15m[i - j]["low"] for j in (1, 2, 3)) and l <= recent_15m[i + 1]["low"]:
            low_points.append((l, "15m", i, 4, 1))

    unique_highs = {pt[0] for pt in high_points if pt[0] > 0}
    clusters_res = []
    for h in unique_highs:
        touches = _count_spaced_touches(h, high_points, tol=0.005)
        if touches >= 2:
            clusters_res.append((h, touches))
    clusters_res.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

    unique_lows = {pt[0] for pt in low_points if pt[0] > 0}
    clusters_sup = []
    for l in unique_lows:
        touches = _count_spaced_touches(l, low_points, tol=0.005)
        if touches >= 2:
            clusters_sup.append((l, touches))
    clusters_sup.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)

    return _dedup_levels(clusters_res), _dedup_levels(clusters_sup)


def find_sr_levels(candles_15m, candles_1h=None, window_15m=120, window_1h=72, window=None):
    """
    Поиск ближайших подтвержденных уровней:
    - Сопротивление: выше текущей цены (> +0.25%)
    - Поддержка: ниже текущей цены (< -0.15%)
    """
    if window is not None:
        window_15m = window

    if not candles_15m or len(candles_15m) < 15:
        return None, 0, None, 0

    last_price = candles_15m[-1]["close"]
    confirmed_res, confirmed_sup = get_all_sr_levels(candles_15m, candles_1h, window_15m, window_1h)

    res_above = [c for c in confirmed_res if c[0] > last_price * 1.0025]
    nearest_res = None
    res_touches = 0
    if res_above:
        res_above.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        nearest_res, res_touches = res_above[0]

    sup_below = [c for c in confirmed_sup if c[0] < last_price * 0.9985]
    nearest_sup = None
    sup_touches = 0
    if sup_below:
        sup_below.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
        nearest_sup, sup_touches = sup_below[0]

    return nearest_res, res_touches, nearest_sup, sup_touches

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/learner.py ---
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

from loguru import logger

from bot.core.remote_state import download_state, upload_state

STATE_FILE = Path("storage/learner.json")
REMOTE_PATH = "learner.json"

KEYS = ["ema50", "ema21", "impulse", "rsi", "volume", "chg24h", "indep", "news_pos", "hype"]

PATTERN_NAMES = {
    "pinbar": "🔨 Пин-бар выкупа (Spring)",
    "divergence": "🧲 Дивергенция RSI",
    "vcp": "📦 Сжатие волатильности (VCP)",
    "breakout": "🚀 Импульсный пробой",
    "engulfing": "⚡ Бычье поглощение",
}

TIERS = ["TOP20", "MID", "SMALL", "MICRO"]

BE_LOW_PCT = -0.15
BE_HIGH_PCT = 0.25


def is_neutral_be(pnl_pct: float) -> bool:
    return BE_LOW_PCT <= float(pnl_pct) <= BE_HIGH_PCT


class Learner:
    """Самообучение: сбалансированные веса с Mean Reversion + нейтральная зона БУ + управление Grade A+ [💎]."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.weights = {k: 1.0 for k in KEYS}
        self.results = []
        self.be_count = 0
        self.threshold_adj = 0.0
        self.current_risk_mode = "NORMAL"
        self.sector_stats = {}
        self.tier_stats = {}
        self.exit_stats = {}
        self.entry_stats = {}
        self.pattern_stats = {}
        self.grade_stats = {"A+": [], "B": []}
        self.grade_a_streak = 0
        self._last_upload = 0.0
        self._load()

    def _load(self):
        data = None
        try:
            if STATE_FILE.exists():
                data = json.loads(STATE_FILE.read_text())
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"learner load error: {e}")
        if data is None:
            data = download_state(REMOTE_PATH)
        if data:
            loaded_weights = data.get("weights", {})
            for k in KEYS:
                w_val = float(loaded_weights.get(k, 1.0))
                self.weights[k] = round(min(1.45, max(0.55, w_val)), 3)

            # Авто-выравнивание исторического перекоса весов (если трендовые ключи упали < 0.60, а редкие > 1.40)
            if min(self.weights.values()) < 0.60 or max(self.weights.values()) > 1.40:
                for k in KEYS:
                    self.weights[k] = round(0.5 * self.weights[k] + 0.5 * 1.0, 3)

            self.results = data.get("results", [])
            self.be_count = int(data.get("be_count", 0))
            self.threshold_adj = float(data.get("threshold_adj", 0))
            self.sector_stats = data.get("sector_stats", {})
            self.tier_stats = data.get("tier_stats", {})
            self.exit_stats = data.get("exit_stats", {})
            # Склеиваем дублирующиеся ключи 🛡🔒 и 🛡🔒 БУ из прошлых сессий
            if "🛡🔒" in self.exit_stats:
                old_be = self.exit_stats.pop("🛡🔒")
                for p in old_be:
                    target_k = "🛡💼" if p > BE_HIGH_PCT else "🛡🔒 БУ"
                    self.exit_stats.setdefault(target_k, []).append(p)
            self.entry_stats = data.get("entry_stats", {})
            self.pattern_stats = data.get("pattern_stats", {})
            self.grade_stats = data.get("grade_stats", {"A+": [], "B": []})
            self.grade_a_streak = int(data.get("grade_a_streak", 0))
            logger.info("learner: опыт загружен и сбалансирован")

    def save(self):
        payload = {
            "weights": self.weights,
            "results": self.results[-200:],
            "be_count": self.be_count,
            "threshold_adj": self.threshold_adj,
            "sector_stats": {k: v[-50:] for k, v in self.sector_stats.items()},
            "tier_stats": {k: v[-50:] for k, v in self.tier_stats.items()},
            "exit_stats": {k: v[-50:] for k, v in self.exit_stats.items()},
            "entry_stats": {k: v[-50:] for k, v in self.entry_stats.items()},
            "pattern_stats": {k: v[-50:] for k, v in self.pattern_stats.items()},
            "grade_stats": {k: v[-100:] for k, v in self.grade_stats.items()},
            "grade_a_streak": self.grade_a_streak,
        }
        try:
            STATE_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            STATE_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"learner save error: {e}")
        if time.time() - self._last_upload > 3600:
            self._last_upload = time.time()
            upload_state(REMOTE_PATH, payload)

    def weight(self, key):
        return self.weights.get(key, 1.0)

    def grade_a_trust_mult(self) -> float:
        if self.grade_a_streak <= -3:
            return 0.70
        if self.grade_a_streak == -2:
            return 0.82
        if self.grade_a_streak >= 3:
            return 1.15
        if self.grade_a_streak == 2:
            return 1.08
        return 1.0

    def get_grade_a_stats(self) -> dict:
        hist = self.grade_stats.get("A+", [])
        decisive = [p for p in hist if not is_neutral_be(p)]
        be_cnt = len(hist) - len(decisive)
        wins = [p for p in decisive if p > 0]
        wr = (len(wins) / len(decisive)) if decisive else 0.0
        avg = (sum(decisive) / len(decisive)) if decisive else 0.0
        return {
            "total": len(hist),
            "decisive": len(decisive),
            "be": be_cnt,
            "wr": wr,
            "avg": avg,
            "streak": self.grade_a_streak,
            "mult": self.grade_a_trust_mult(),
        }

    def record(self, keys, win, pnl_pct=0.0, sector=None, tier=None,
               exit_type=None, runner_bonus=0.0, soft=False,
               entry_mode=None, patterns=None, grade="B"):
        g_key = "A+" if grade == "A+" else "B"
        gh = self.grade_stats.setdefault(g_key, [])
        gh.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
        self.grade_stats[g_key] = gh[-100:]

        if is_neutral_be(pnl_pct):
            self.be_count += 1
            eh = self.exit_stats.setdefault("🛡🔒 БУ", [])
            eh.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
            self.exit_stats["🛡🔒 БУ"] = eh[-50:]
            self.save()
            logger.info(f"learner: нейтральный выход 🛡🔒 БУ ({pnl_pct:+.2f}%) — веса и Winrate не затронуты")
            return

        real_win = pnl_pct > BE_HIGH_PCT
        self.results.append(1 if real_win else 0)
        self.results = self.results[-200:]

        if g_key == "A+":
            if real_win:
                self.grade_a_streak = max(1, self.grade_a_streak + 1)
            else:
                self.grade_a_streak = min(-1, self.grade_a_streak - 1)

        if sector:
            hist = self.sector_stats.setdefault(sector, [])
            hist.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
            self.sector_stats[sector] = hist[-50:]
        if tier:
            hist = self.tier_stats.setdefault(tier, [])
            hist.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
            self.tier_stats[tier] = hist[-50:]
        if exit_type:
            norm_exit = "🛡💼" if (exit_type == "🛡🔒" and real_win) else ("🛡📉" if (exit_type == "🛡🔒" and not real_win) else exit_type)
            eh = self.exit_stats.setdefault(norm_exit, [])
            eh.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
            self.exit_stats[norm_exit] = eh[-50:]
        if entry_mode:
            em_h = self.entry_stats.setdefault(entry_mode, [])
            em_h.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
            self.entry_stats[entry_mode] = em_h[-50:]

        if patterns:
            for p in patterns:
                ph = self.pattern_stats.setdefault(p, [])
                ph.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
                self.pattern_stats[p] = ph[-50:]

        abs_pnl = abs(pnl_pct)
        if abs_pnl >= 3.0:
            delta = 0.06 if real_win else -0.06
        elif abs_pnl >= 1.0:
            delta = 0.035 if real_win else -0.035
        else:
            delta = 0.02 if real_win else -0.02
        if runner_bonus > 5.0:
            delta += 0.03
        if soft and not real_win:
            delta *= 0.5

        # Обновляем только участвовавшие ключи + мягкий возврат всех весов к 1.0 (Mean Reversion 2%),
        # чтобы редкие ключи (hype/news_pos) не раздувались за счет падения трендовых (ema21/ema50)
        for k in KEYS:
            w_cur = self.weights.get(k, 1.0)
            if k in keys:
                w_cur += delta
            w_reverted = w_cur * 0.98 + 1.0 * 0.02
            self.weights[k] = round(min(1.45, max(0.55, w_reverted)), 3)

        self.save()
        logger.info(f"learner: win={real_win} pnl={pnl_pct:+.2f}% grade={g_key} delta={delta:+.3f} keys={keys}")

    def pattern_multiplier(self, pattern_key: str) -> float:
        hist = [p for p in (self.pattern_stats.get(pattern_key) or []) if not is_neutral_be(p)]
        if len(hist) < 3:
            return 1.0
        wr = sum(1 for p in hist if p > 0) / len(hist)
        avg = sum(hist) / len(hist)
        mult = 1.0 + (wr - 0.5) * 0.5 + min(0.2, max(-0.2, avg * 0.05))
        return round(max(0.65, min(1.35, mult)), 2)

    def sector_bias(self, sector):
        hist = [p for p in (self.sector_stats.get(sector) or []) if not is_neutral_be(p)]
        if len(hist) < 3:
            return 0.0
        wr = sum(1 for p in hist if p > 0) / len(hist)
        avg = sum(hist) / len(hist)
        bias = (wr - 0.5) + max(-0.5, min(0.5, avg * 0.25))
        return round(max(-1.0, min(1.0, bias)), 2)

    def tier_bias(self, tier):
        hist = [p for p in (self.tier_stats.get(tier) or []) if not is_neutral_be(p)]
        if len(hist) < 5:
            return 0.0
        wr = sum(1 for p in hist if p > 0) / len(hist)
        avg = sum(hist) / len(hist)
        bias = (wr - 0.5) + max(-0.25, min(0.25, avg * 0.25))
        return round(max(-0.5, min(0.5, bias)), 2)

    def reset(self):
        self.weights = {k: 1.0 for k in KEYS}
        self.results = []
        self.be_count = 0
        self.threshold_adj = 0.0
        self.current_risk_mode = "NORMAL"
        self.sector_stats = {}
        self.tier_stats = {}
        self.exit_stats = {}
        self.entry_stats = {}
        self.pattern_stats = {}
        self.grade_stats = {"A+": [], "B": []}
        self.grade_a_streak = 0
        self.save()
        logger.info("learner: опыт сброшен")

    def reset_stats(self):
        self.results = []
        self.be_count = 0
        self.threshold_adj = 0.0
        self.sector_stats = {}
        self.tier_stats = {}
        self.exit_stats = {}
        self.entry_stats = {}
        self.pattern_stats = {}
        self.grade_stats = {"A+": [], "B": []}
        self.grade_a_streak = 0
        self.save()
        logger.info("learner: статистика сброшена")

    def winrate(self):
        last = self.results[-20:]
        return (sum(last) / len(last), len(last)) if last else (0.0, 0)

    def risk_mode(self, profit_factor, max_dd_pct, total_trades=0):
        adj = 0.0
        enough = total_trades >= 3
        if enough and profit_factor is not None:
            if profit_factor < 0.6:
                adj += 1.5
            elif profit_factor < 1.0:
                adj += 0.5
            elif profit_factor > 1.5 and max_dd_pct and max_dd_pct < 8:
                adj -= 0.5
        if max_dd_pct and max_dd_pct > 12:
            adj += 1.0
        elif max_dd_pct and max_dd_pct > 8:
            adj += 0.5

        if adj >= 1.5:
            mode = "STRICT"
        elif adj >= 0.5:
            mode = "CAUTIOUS"
        elif adj <= -0.5:
            mode = "AGGRESSIVE"
        else:
            mode = "NORMAL"
        return mode, adj

    def update_threshold(self, profit_factor, max_dd_pct, total_trades=0):
        mode, dyn_adj = self.risk_mode(profit_factor, max_dd_pct, total_trades)
        self.current_risk_mode = mode

        wr_adj = 0.0
        if total_trades >= 5:
            last = self.results[-20:]
            if last:
                wr = sum(last) / len(last)
                if wr < 0.35:
                    wr_adj = 0.25

        raw_adj = dyn_adj + wr_adj
        self.threshold_adj = round(max(-0.25, min(0.25, raw_adj * 0.2)), 2)
        self.save()
        return self.threshold_adj

    def summary(self):
        wr, n = self.winrate()
        top = sorted(self.weights.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)[:3]
        txt = ", ".join(f"{k} {v:.2f}" for k, v in top[:3])
        return f"wr {wr:.0%} ({n}) · 🛡🔒 БУ: {self.be_count} · топ: {txt} · режим: {self.current_risk_mode}"

    def kelly_multiplier(self, mode):
        hist = [p for p in (self.entry_stats.get(mode) or []) if not is_neutral_be(p)]
        if len(hist) < 10:
            return None
        wins = [p for p in hist if p > 0]
        losses = [p for p in hist if p <= 0]
        if not wins or not losses:
            return None
        p = len(wins) / len(hist)
        avg_win = sum(wins) / len(wins)
        avg_loss = abs(sum(losses) / len(losses))
        b = avg_win / avg_loss if avg_loss else 0
        if b <= 0:
            return 0.0
        kelly = (b * p - (1 - p)) / b
        return round(max(0.0, min(kelly * 0.5, 1.0)), 3)


learner = Learner()

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/learner.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/patterns.py ---
from bot.strategy.indicators import rsi, ema


def detect_patterns(candles_15m: list, a15: float, e50: float, e21: float, recent_high: float) -> list:
    """
    Price Action & Liquidity Pattern Engine.
    Включает:
    1. Подтвержденный Spring Pinbar и W-базу (Second Test) с расчетом глубокой лимитки wick_entry_price.
    2. Истинный VCP (строго над EMA50 и растущей EMA21 с повышением минимумов — без ложных срабатываний на пробое вниз).
    3. Фильтр «истощения импульса» для rocket 🚀.
    """
    if not candles_15m or len(candles_15m) < 25:
        return []

    patterns = []
    curr = candles_15m[-1]   # Текущая (формирующаяся) свеча
    closed = candles_15m[-2] # Последняя полностью закрытая 15m свеча
    prev2 = candles_15m[-3]  # Предшествующая закрытая свеча
    closes = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]

    vols = [c["volume"] for c in candles_15m]
    avg_vol = sum(vols[-22:-2]) / 20 if len(vols) >= 22 else (sum(vols) / max(1, len(vols)))
    vol_ratio_curr = (curr["volume"] / avg_vol) if avg_vol > 0 else 1.0
    vol_ratio_closed = (closed["volume"] / avg_vol) if avg_vol > 0 else 1.0
    r = rsi(closes)

    # =========================================================================
    # 1. SPRING PINBAR (с подтверждением остановки ножа и лимиткой в глубину тени)
    # =========================================================================
    pinbar_found = False
    pin_wick_pct = 0.0
    pin_vol = 1.0
    wick_entry_price = None

    closed_range = closed["high"] - closed["low"]
    if closed_range > 0:
        closed_body_low = min(closed["open"], closed["close"])
        closed_lwick = closed_body_low - closed["low"]
        # Требуем закрытия свечи в верхней половине диапазона и удержания уровня текущей свечой
        if (closed_lwick / closed_range) >= 0.48 and (closed["close"] >= closed["low"] + 0.52 * closed_range) and vol_ratio_closed >= 1.20:
            if curr["close"] >= closed_body_low and curr["low"] >= closed["low"] * 0.9985:
                pinbar_found = True
                pin_wick_pct = (closed_lwick / closed_range) * 100
                pin_vol = vol_ratio_closed
                # Целимся лимиткой на 45% вглубь тени от низа тела свечи (Second Test)
                wick_entry_price = round(closed_body_low - 0.45 * closed_lwick, 8)

    curr_range = curr["high"] - curr["low"]
    if not pinbar_found and curr_range > 0:
        curr_body_low = min(curr["open"], curr["close"])
        curr_lwick = curr_body_low - curr["low"]
        if (curr_lwick / curr_range) >= 0.50 and (curr["close"] >= curr["open"]) and (curr["close"] >= curr["low"] + 0.58 * curr_range) and vol_ratio_curr >= 1.45:
            pinbar_found = True
            pin_wick_pct = (curr_lwick / curr_range) * 100
            pin_vol = vol_ratio_curr
            wick_entry_price = round(curr_body_low - 0.45 * curr_lwick, 8)

    if pinbar_found:
        if r <= 42 or curr["close"] < e50:
            patterns.append({
                "key": "pinbar",
                "name": f"Пин-бар выкупа на дне (хвост {pin_wick_pct:.0f}%, vol x{pin_vol:.1f})",
                "mode": "reversal",
                "base_bonus": 3.0,
                "wick_entry_price": wick_entry_price,
            })
        elif e21 >= e50 and curr["close"] >= e50:
            patterns.append({
                "key": "pinbar",
                "name": f"Молот по тренду (хвост {pin_wick_pct:.0f}%, vol x{pin_vol:.1f})",
                "mode": "sniper",
                "base_bonus": 2.0,
                "wick_entry_price": wick_entry_price,
            })

    # =========================================================================
    # 2. BULLISH DIVERGENCE (Бычья дивергенция RSI + подтвержденная W-база)
    # =========================================================================
    rsi_now = r
    rsi_past = rsi(closes[:-6])
    low_now = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-6:])
    low_past = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-18:-6])
    # Подтверждение разворота: закрытая зеленая свеча ИЛИ выход выше максимума предыдущей свечи
    turning_up = (closed["close"] > closed["open"] and curr["close"] >= closed["close"] * 0.999) or (curr["close"] > closed["high"])

    if low_now <= low_past * 1.003 and rsi_now > (rsi_past + 3.0) and rsi_now <= 46 and turning_up:
        w_base_entry = round(low_now + (curr["close"] - low_now) * 0.32, 8)
        patterns.append({
            "key": "divergence",
            "name": f"Бычья дивергенция RSI ({rsi_past:.0f} -> {rsi_now:.0f})",
            "mode": "reversal",
            "base_bonus": 2.6,
            "wick_entry_price": w_base_entry,
        })

    # =========================================================================
    # 3. ИСТИННЫЙ VCP COMPRESSION (Защита от пробоя EMA50 сверху вниз — кейс UNI/RECALL)
    # =========================================================================
    e21_series = ema(closes, 21)
    e21_rising = len(e21_series) >= 5 and e21_series[-1] >= e21_series[-4] * 0.9998

    if e50 > 0 and e21 > (e50 * 1.0005) and e21_rising:
        # Все 4 закрытые свечи бокса обязаны закрываться ВЫШЕ EMA50, а текущая цена — удерживать EMA21
        box_candles = candles_15m[-5:-1]
        all_closed_above_e50 = all(c["close"] >= e50 * 0.9995 for c in box_candles)
        curr_above_e21 = curr["close"] >= e21 * 0.9985
        is_near_ema = 1.000 <= (curr["close"] / e50) <= 1.016

        recent_box = (max(c["high"] for c in candles_15m[-5:]) - min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-5:])) / curr["close"]

        # Повышающиеся минимумы (Higher Lows — поджатие снизу вверх, а не сползание вниз)
        lows_recent = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-3:])
        lows_prev = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-6:-3])
        higher_lows = lows_recent >= lows_prev * 0.999

        closed_4_vol = sum(c["volume"] for c in box_candles) / 4
        vcp_vol_ratio = (closed_4_vol / avg_vol) if avg_vol > 0 else 1.0
        is_dry_volume = vcp_vol_ratio <= 1.10

        curr_body = abs(curr["close"] - curr["open"])
        not_dump = (closed["close"] >= closed["open"] or closed["close"] >= e21) and not (curr["close"] < curr["open"] and curr_body > 0.35 * a15)

        if all_closed_above_e50 and curr_above_e21 and is_near_ema and higher_lows and recent_box <= 0.020 and is_dry_volume and not_dump:
            patterns.append({
                "key": "vcp",
                "name": f"Сжатие VCP над EMA21/50 (бокс {recent_box*100:.1f}%, vol x{vcp_vol_ratio:.1f})",
                "mode": "sniper",
                "base_bonus": 3.0,
            })

    # =========================================================================
    # 4. CLEAN BREAKOUT (Истинный пробой + Фильтр «истощения импульса»)
    # =========================================================================
    if recent_high > 0 and a15 > 0 and e50 > 0:
        dist_from_ema50_atr = (curr["close"] - e50) / a15
        base_slice = candles_15m[-8:-2]
        pre_breakout_box = (
            (max(c["high"] for c in base_slice) - min(c["low"] for c in base_slice)) / curr["close"]
            if base_slice and curr["close"] > 0 else 0.0
        )
        not_exhausted = (dist_from_ema50_atr <= 2.8) and (pre_breakout_box <= 0.042) and (r <= 73)

        if not_exhausted:
            closed_green = closed["close"] > closed["open"]
            closed_body = abs(closed["close"] - closed["open"])
            closed_uwick = closed["high"] - max(closed["open"], closed["close"])
            closed_solid = (closed_body / closed_range) >= 0.55 if closed_range > 0 else False
            closed_clean = (closed_uwick / closed_range) <= 0.22 if closed_range > 0 else False

            brk_confirmed_closed = (
                closed["close"] > recent_high and closed_green and
                closed_solid and closed_clean and vol_ratio_closed >= 1.30 and
                curr["close"] >= recent_high * 0.999
            )

            curr_green = curr["close"] > curr["open"]
            curr_body = abs(curr["close"] - curr["open"])
            curr_uwick = curr["high"] - max(curr["open"], curr["close"])
            curr_solid = (curr_body / curr_range) >= 0.60 if curr_range > 0 else False
            curr_clean = (curr_uwick / curr_range) <= 0.20 if curr_range > 0 else False

            brk_live = (
                curr["close"] > recent_high and curr_green and
                curr_solid and curr_clean and vol_ratio_curr >= 1.40
            )

            if brk_confirmed_closed or brk_live:
                b_vol = vol_ratio_curr if brk_live else vol_ratio_closed
                patterns.append({
                    "key": "breakout",
                    "name": f"Истинный пробой базы ${recent_high:.4f} (vol x{b_vol:.1f})",
                    "mode": "rocket",
                    "base_bonus": 3.0,
                })

    # =========================================================================
    # 5. FAILED AUCTION (Бычье поглощение)
    # =========================================================================
    closed_engulf = (
        prev2["close"] < prev2["open"] and
        closed["close"] > closed["open"] and
        closed["close"] >= prev2["open"] and
        closed["open"] <= prev2["close"] and
        closed["volume"] >= prev2["volume"] and
        curr["close"] >= closed["open"]
    )
    live_engulf = (
        closed["close"] < closed["open"] and
        curr["close"] > curr["open"] and
        curr["close"] >= closed["open"] and
        curr["open"] <= closed["close"] and
        curr["volume"] >= closed["volume"] * 1.15
    )

    if closed_engulf or live_engulf:
        patterns.append({
            "key": "engulfing",
            "name": "Бычье поглощение (ловушка продавцов)",
            "mode": "sniper",
            "base_bonus": 2.0,
        })

    return patterns

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/patterns.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/risk.py ---
from bot.strategy.indicators import atr, ema, rsi, get_all_sr_levels
from bot.strategy.shadow import shadow
from bot.strategy.learner import learner
from bot.utils.format import sl_emoji

TIER_SL_FLOOR = {
    "TOP20": 0.009,  # 0.9%
    "MID":   0.012,  # 1.2%
    "SMALL": 0.015,  # 1.5%
    "MICRO": 0.018,  # 1.8%
}

TIER_TP_FLOOR = {
    "TOP20": 0.010,  # 1.0%
    "MID":   0.013,  # 1.3%
    "SMALL": 0.016,  # 1.6%
    "MICRO": 0.018,  # 1.8%
}

# Снижены потолки максимального стопа, чтобы исключить одиночные пробои по -4.15% (кейс ERA)
TIER_MAX_SL = {
    "TOP20": 2.0,
    "MID":   2.4,
    "SMALL": 2.6,
    "MICRO": 2.9,
}

TIER_TRAIL_CONFIG = {
    "TOP20": {"runner_dist": 0.009, "lock_trigger_pct": 1.6, "lock_dist": 0.006},
    "MID":   {"runner_dist": 0.011, "lock_trigger_pct": 2.0, "lock_dist": 0.008},
    "SMALL": {"runner_dist": 0.014, "lock_trigger_pct": 2.3, "lock_dist": 0.009},
    "MICRO": {"runner_dist": 0.018, "lock_trigger_pct": 2.6, "lock_dist": 0.012},
}

TRADE_POLICIES = {
    "STRICT": {
        "max_portfolio_risk_pct": 1.5,
        "risk_per_trade_core": 0.50,
        "risk_per_trade_alt": 0.25,
        "min_rr_sniper": 1.20,
        "min_rr_alt": 1.05,
        "require_grade_a_for_alt": True,
    },
    "CAUTIOUS": {
        "max_portfolio_risk_pct": 2.0,
        "risk_per_trade_core": 0.75,
        "risk_per_trade_alt": 0.50,
        "min_rr_sniper": 1.15,
        "min_rr_alt": 1.00,
        "require_grade_a_for_alt": True,
    },
    "NORMAL": {
        "max_portfolio_risk_pct": 3.0,
        "risk_per_trade_core": 1.00,
        "risk_per_trade_alt": 0.75,
        "min_rr_sniper": 1.05,
        "min_rr_alt": 0.95,
        "require_grade_a_for_alt": False,
    },
    "AGGRESSIVE": {
        "max_portfolio_risk_pct": 5.0,
        "risk_per_trade_core": 1.25,
        "risk_per_trade_alt": 1.00,
        "min_rr_sniper": 1.00,
        "min_rr_alt": 0.90,
        "require_grade_a_for_alt": False,
    },
}


def get_trade_policy(risk_mode: str = "NORMAL") -> dict:
    return TRADE_POLICIES.get(risk_mode, TRADE_POLICIES["NORMAL"])


def calc_breakeven_price(avg_price: float, fee_pct: float = 0.10) -> float:
    """Точный математический безубыток с учетом двойной комиссии Bybit + микро-запас."""
    if avg_price <= 0:
        return 0.0
    f = fee_pct / 100.0
    be = avg_price * (1.0 + f) / (1.0 - f) * 1.0005
    return round(be, 8)


def evaluate_setup_grade(cand: dict) -> str:
    """
    Строгий отбор сетапов высшей категории [💎] Grade A+:
    - Запрет выдавать [💎] A+ на крыше 48-часового диапазона (pos_in_48h > 0.70) или при высокой корреляции с BTC (> 0.55).
    - Обязательное схождение структурных факторов: сильный паттерн Price Action / часовая плита >= 4x + объем + MTF.
    """
    pos_in_48h = cand.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5)
    corr = abs(cand.get("corr", 1.0))
    entry_mode = cand.get("entry_mode", "sniper")

    if entry_mode in ("sniper", "rocket") and pos_in_48h > 0.70:
        return "B"
    if corr > 0.55:
        return "B"

    confluence = 0
    patterns = cand.get("patterns", [])
    keys = cand.get("reason_keys", [])
    sv = cand.get("signal_values", {})
    sup_touches = cand.get("sup_touches", 0)

    strong_pats = [p for p in patterns if p in ("pinbar", "divergence", "vcp", "breakout")]
    if len(strong_pats) >= 1:
        confluence += 2
    elif len(patterns) >= 1:
        confluence += 1

    if sup_touches >= 4:
        confluence += 2
    elif sup_touches == 3:
        confluence += 1

    if cand.get("gate_1h") == "1H_TREND_OK" and ("mtf_dip" in keys or pos_in_48h <= 0.65):
        confluence += 1

    if sv.get("volume", 1.0) >= 1.45:
        confluence += 1

    if corr < 0.25:
        confluence += 1

    if not strong_pats and sup_touches < 4:
        return "B"

    req_confluence = 5 if getattr(learner, "grade_a_streak", 0) <= -2 else 4
    return "A+" if confluence >= req_confluence else "B"


def calc_total_open_risk_pct(positions: dict, orders: list, equity: float) -> float:
    """Считает текущий открытый риск портфеля (Portfolio VaR) по активным стопам."""
    if equity <= 0:
        return 0.0
    total_risk_usdt = 0.0

    for pos in positions.values():
        avg = pos.get("avg", 0.0)
        sl = pos.get("sl", 0.0)
        qty = pos.get("qty", 0.0)
        if avg > sl > 0 and qty > 0:
            total_risk_usdt += (avg - sl) * qty

    for o in orders:
        price = o.get("price", 0.0)
        sl = o.get("sl", 0.0)
        qty = o.get("qty", 0.0)
        if price > sl > 0 and qty > 0:
            total_risk_usdt += (price - sl) * qty

    return round((total_risk_usdt / equity) * 100.0, 3)


def calc_initial_sl_tp(symbol: str, entry: float, tier: str, a: float,
                        sup: float, sup_touches: int, res: float, res_touches: int,
                        is_mom: bool = False, entry_mode: str = "sniper",
                        min_rr: float = None, recent_low_6: float = None,
                        ema50: float = None, e21_1h: float = None, e50_1h: float = None):
    """
    Умная постановка первичного Стоп-лосса и Тейк-профита:
    1. Wick Low Protection: стоп ниже хвоста последних 6 свечей (recent_low_6 * 0.997).
    2. Буфер под EMA 50 в аптренде (EMA 50 - 0.6 * ATR).
    3. Капитальной стеной сопротивления считается только уровень с весом >= 4 (часовая/многократная плита).
    """
    tier = tier or "SMALL"
    sl_floor_pct = TIER_SL_FLOOR.get(tier, 0.015)
    tp_floor_pct = TIER_TP_FLOOR.get(tier, 0.016)
    max_sl_allowed = TIER_MAX_SL.get(tier, 2.6)

    sl_tuning = shadow.sl_mult()
    tp_tuning = shadow.tp_mult()

    sl_dist = 0.0
    if sup and sup_touches >= 2 and sup < entry and (entry - sup) <= 1.8 * a:
        struct_sl = round(sup * 0.997, 8)
        candidate_dist = max(entry - struct_sl, entry * sl_floor_pct)
        if (candidate_dist / entry) * 100 <= max_sl_allowed:
            sl_dist = candidate_dist
            sl = round(entry - sl_dist, 8)

    if sl_dist <= 0:
        base_mult = 0.85 if is_mom else (1.50 if entry_mode == "reversal" else 1.20)
        sl_dist_raw = max(base_mult * a * sl_tuning, entry * sl_floor_pct)
        sl_dist = min(sl_dist_raw, entry * (max_sl_allowed / 100.0))
        sl = round(entry - sl_dist, 8)

    if recent_low_6 and recent_low_6 > 0 and recent_low_6 < entry:
        wick_safe_sl = round(recent_low_6 * 0.997, 8)
        if wick_safe_sl < sl:
            wick_dist_pct = (entry - wick_safe_sl) / entry * 100
            if wick_dist_pct <= max_sl_allowed:
                sl = wick_safe_sl
                sl_dist = entry - sl

    if ema50 and ema50 > 0 and entry > ema50:
        ema50_safe_sl = round(ema50 - 0.6 * a, 8)
        if sl > ema50_safe_sl and abs(sl - ema50) <= 0.6 * a:
            ema_dist_pct = (entry - ema50_safe_sl) / entry * 100
            if ema_dist_pct <= max_sl_allowed:
                sl = ema50_safe_sl
                sl_dist = entry - sl

    if sl >= entry or (sl_dist / entry * 100) > max_sl_allowed:
        return None, None, False, "Недопустимая дистанция SL"

    tp_dist_raw = max(1.65 * a * tp_tuning, sl_dist * 1.35, entry * tp_floor_pct)
    tp = round(entry + tp_dist_raw, 8)
    if min_rr is None:
        min_rr = 1.05 if entry_mode == "sniper" else 0.95

    if entry_mode == "reversal" and e21_1h and e50_1h and e21_1h < e50_1h and e21_1h > entry * 1.008:
        cap_1h = round(e21_1h * 0.998, 8)
        if cap_1h < tp and (cap_1h - entry) / sl_dist >= 0.85:
            tp = cap_1h
            min_rr = min(min_rr, 0.85)

    if res and res > entry and res_touches >= 4:
        res_dist_pct = (res - entry) / entry * 100
        if res_dist_pct < 0.35:
            return None, None, False, f"Капитальная стена 1H в упор ({res_dist_pct:.2f}% < 0.35%)"

        res_cap_tp = round(res * 0.997, 8)
        if res_cap_tp < tp:
            if (res_cap_tp - entry) / sl_dist >= 0.85:
                tp = res_cap_tp
                min_rr = min(min_rr, 0.85)
            else:
                return None, None, False, f"Капитальная стена 1H не дает забрать 0.85x RR (RR={(res_cap_tp - entry)/sl_dist:.2f})"

    rr = (tp - entry) / sl_dist
    if tp <= entry or round(rr, 2) < min_rr:
        return None, None, False, f"Недостаточный RR ({round(rr, 2)} < {min_rr})"

    return sl, tp, True, "OK"


def calc_runner_target(symbol: str, current_price: float, candles_15m: list, candles_1h: list = None, pos: dict = None) -> tuple:
    if not candles_15m or len(candles_15m) < 25:
        return round(current_price * 1.03, 8), True, "static"

    closes = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]
    e21 = ema(closes, 21)[-1]
    e50 = ema(closes, 50)[-1]
    r15 = rsi(closes)
    a15 = atr(candles_15m)

    dist_from_e50_atr = ((current_price - e50) / a15) if a15 > 0 else 0.0
    if r15 > 75 or dist_from_e50_atr > 3.5:
        climax_tp = round(max(current_price, candles_15m[-1]["high"]) * 1.001, 8)
        return climax_tp, False, "climax_freeze"

    entry_mode = (pos or {}).get("entry_mode", "sniper")
    if candles_1h and len(candles_1h) >= 50:
        closes_1h = [c["close"] for c in candles_1h]
        e21_1h = ema(closes_1h, 21)[-1]
        e50_1h = ema(closes_1h, 50)[-1]
        if entry_mode == "reversal" and e21_1h < e50_1h and current_price < e50_1h:
            if e21_1h > current_price * 1.005:
                return round(e21_1h * 0.998, 8), False, "1h_ema21_cap"

    recent_box = (max(c["high"] for c in candles_15m[-6:]) - min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-6:])) / current_price if current_price > 0 else 0.0
    if recent_box <= 0.015 and e21 <= e50 * 1.001:
        old_tp = (pos or {}).get("tp", current_price * 1.02)
        return round(max(old_tp, current_price * 1.012), 8), False, "shelf_freeze"

    all_res, _ = get_all_sr_levels(candles_15m, candles_1h)
    res_above = [r[0] for r in all_res if r[0] > current_price * 1.012 and r[1] >= 4]

    mult_atr = 3.2 if (e21 > e50 and r15 >= 58) else 2.5
    corridor_ceiling = e21 + mult_atr * a15
    target = max(corridor_ceiling, current_price + 2.2 * a15, current_price * 1.025)

    if res_above:
        res_above.sort()
        wall = res_above[0] * 0.997
        if wall > current_price * 1.015:
            target = min(target, wall)

    return round(target, 8), True, "trend_expand"


def update_position_sl(pos: dict, max_p: float, last_price: float = None, candles_15m: list = None) -> tuple:
    """
    Дышащий Коридор с 5 фазами стопа:
    - SHIELD (🛡📉): первичный стоп
    - BREAKEVEN (🛡🔒): безубыток
    - PROFIT_LOCK (🛡💼): фиксация прибыли ДО взятия TP1 (с ускоренным поджатием для 🚀 rocket — кейс MANA)
    - TRAILING_RUNNER (🛡📈): динамический трейлинг ПОСЛЕ взятия TP1 (ниже уровня TP1)
    - ABOVE_TP1 (🛡🔥): стоп-лосс перешагнул ВЫШЕ уровня первого тейка (TP1)!
    """
    avg_p = pos.get("avg", 0.0)
    if avg_p <= 0:
        return False, pos.get("sl"), pos.get("sl_state", "SHIELD"), ""

    last_p = last_price or max_p
    tier = pos.get("tier") or "SMALL"
    entry_mode = pos.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
    is_rocket = (entry_mode == "rocket" or pos.get("is_momentum", False))
    cfg = TIER_TRAIL_CONFIG.get(tier, TIER_TRAIL_CONFIG["SMALL"])

    current_sl = round(float(pos.get("sl", 0.0)), 8)
    new_sl = current_sl
    current_state = pos.get("sl_state", "SHIELD")
    new_state = current_state

    breakeven = calc_breakeven_price(avg_p)
    profit_pct = (max_p - avg_p) / avg_p * 100
    tp_price = float(pos.get("tp", 0.0))
    init_tp = float(pos.get("initial_tp") or tp_price)
    tp_target_pct = ((init_tp - avg_p) / avg_p * 100) if init_tp > avg_p else 3.0

    if candles_15m and len(candles_15m) >= 50:
        closes = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]
        pos["_ema21"] = ema(closes, 21)[-1]
        pos["_ema50"] = ema(closes, 50)[-1]
        pos["_atr15"] = atr(candles_15m)
        pos["_rsi15"] = rsi(closes)

    e21 = pos.get("_ema21", 0.0)
    e50 = pos.get("_ema50", 0.0)
    a15 = pos.get("_atr15", 0.0)
    r15 = pos.get("_rsi15", 50.0)

    is_climax = (r15 > 75) or (a15 > 0 and e50 > 0 and (last_p - e50) > 3.5 * a15)

    ema_ratchet_sl = 0.0
    if a15 > 0 and e21 > 0 and e50 > 0:
        if is_climax and profit_pct >= 1.2:
            ema_ratchet_sl = e21 - 0.40 * a15
        elif is_rocket:
            # Для ракеты 🚀 поджимаем стоп значительно плотнее под быструю EMA 21
            if last_p > e21:
                ema_ratchet_sl = e21 - 0.35 * a15
        elif entry_mode == "sniper":
            if last_p > e50:
                ema_ratchet_sl = e50 - 0.80 * a15
        elif entry_mode == "reversal":
            if pos.get("tp1_done") or (last_p > e50 and e21 >= e50 * 0.998):
                ema_ratchet_sl = e50 - 0.80 * a15

        if ema_ratchet_sl >= last_p * 0.996:
            ema_ratchet_sl = last_p * 0.995

    if pos.get("tp1_done"):
        tp1_ref = float(pos.get("tp1_price") or pos.get("initial_tp") or 0.0)
        runner_dist = cfg["runner_dist"] * (0.80 if is_rocket else 1.0)
        runner_trail = round(max_p * (1 - runner_dist), 8)
        candidate = max(breakeven, runner_trail, ema_ratchet_sl)
        if candidate > new_sl:
            new_sl = candidate

        # Если стоп поднялся до или выше цены первого тейка TP1 -> присваиваем элитный статус 🛡🔥 ABOVE_TP1!
        if tp1_ref > 0 and new_sl >= tp1_ref * 0.9995:
            new_state = "ABOVE_TP1"
        elif new_sl > breakeven * 1.003:
            new_state = "TRAILING_RUNNER"
        else:
            new_state = "BREAKEVEN"
    else:
        # 1. Агрессивная защита импульса для 🚀 Ракета ДО взятия TP1 (кейс MANA)
        if is_rocket:
            # При прохождении >= 45% пути к TP1 (или +1.15% профита) — перенос в безубыток 🛡🔒
            if profit_pct >= max(1.15, tp_target_pct * 0.45):
                if breakeven > new_sl:
                    new_sl = breakeven
                    new_state = "BREAKEVEN"
            # При прохождении >= 62% пути к TP1 (или +1.65% профита) — жесткий профит-лок 🛡💼 (фиксируем 48% импульса)
            if profit_pct >= max(1.65, tp_target_pct * 0.62):
                rocket_lock = round(avg_p + (max_p - avg_p) * 0.48, 8)
                rocket_trail = round(max_p * (1 - cfg["lock_dist"] * 0.80), 8)
                candidate = max(breakeven, rocket_lock, rocket_trail, ema_ratchet_sl)
                if candidate > new_sl:
                    new_sl = candidate
                    new_state = "PROFIT_LOCK" if new_sl > breakeven * 1.002 else "BREAKEVEN"

        # 2. Стандартный храповик по EMA и порогу lock_trigger_pct
        if ema_ratchet_sl > new_sl and profit_pct >= 0.8:
            new_sl = ema_ratchet_sl
            if new_sl >= breakeven * 1.003:
                new_state = "PROFIT_LOCK"
            elif new_sl >= breakeven:
                new_state = "BREAKEVEN"

        if profit_pct >= cfg["lock_trigger_pct"] or (is_climax and profit_pct >= 1.5):
            lock_sl = round(max_p * (1 - cfg["lock_dist"]), 8)
            candidate = max(breakeven, lock_sl, ema_ratchet_sl)
            if candidate > new_sl:
                new_sl = candidate
                new_state = "PROFIT_LOCK" if new_sl > breakeven * 1.002 else "BREAKEVEN"

    new_sl = round(new_sl, 8)

    if (new_sl > current_sl + 1e-7) or (new_state != current_state and new_sl >= current_sl):
        tag_desc = sl_emoji(new_state)
        return True, new_sl, new_state, tag_desc

    return False, current_sl, current_state, ""

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/risk.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/scanner.py ---
import os
import time
import asyncio

import httpx
from loguru import logger

from bot.exchange.market_data import market_data
from bot.exchange.paper_exchange import paper
from bot.strategy.indicators import ema, rsi, atr, find_sr_levels, get_all_sr_levels
from bot.strategy.learner import learner
from bot.strategy.shadow import shadow
from bot.strategy.patterns import detect_patterns
from bot.news.cmc import (get_coin_name, get_sectors_for_pool,
                          get_ranks_for_pool, tier_of, TIER_EMOJI, fetch_missing_names)
from bot.news.rss_news import fetch_news_cache, fetch_listings_cache, check_sentiment
from bot.strategy.fundamental import get_macro_trend, is_sector_hot, get_fear_and_greed, check_coinglass_liquidation_threat
from bot.utils.format import fmt_price

SCAN_SUMMARY = {"text": "", "thrs": {}, "ts": 0}
FILTERED_BY_NEWS = []

SCORE_MAX = 10.0

_instruments_cache = {"data": None, "ts": 0}

STABLE_BASES = {"USDC", "USDE", "DAI", "TUSD", "BUSD", "FDUSD", "USDP",
                "USD1", "USDD", "EUR", "EURT", "AEUR", "USDT",
                "RLUSD", "PYUSD", "EURI", "USDS", "USD0", "FRAX",
                "LUSD", "GUSD", "XUSD", "USDX", "CUSD", "SUSD"}

BASE_THRESHOLDS = {
    "bull": {"rocket": 6.0, "sniper": 5.5, "reversal": 5.2},
    "neutral": {"rocket": 6.5, "sniper": 6.0, "reversal": 5.4},
    "bear": {"rocket": 7.0, "sniper": 6.5, "reversal": 5.8},
}

TIER_CORR_LIMITS = {
    "TOP20": 0.92,
    "MID":   0.88,
    "SMALL": 0.82,
    "MICRO": 0.75,
}

_regime_tracker = {
    "prev_regime": "neutral",
    "last_bear_ts": 0.0,
    "bull_start_ts": 0.0,
    "breadth_streak": 0,
}


def _init_regime_tracker():
    if _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"] == 0.0 and hasattr(paper, "market_history") and paper.market_history:
        for h in reversed(paper.market_history):
            if h.get("regime") == "bear" and _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"] == 0.0:
                _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"] = float(h.get("ts", 0))
            if h.get("regime") == "bull" and _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] == 0.0:
                _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] = float(h.get("ts", 0))
        _regime_tracker["prev_regime"] = paper.market_history[-1].get("regime", "neutral")


def get_thresholds(regime):
    now = time.time()
    bull_start = _regime_tracker.get("bull_start_ts", 0.0)
    bull_age = (now - bull_start) if bull_start > 0 else 0

    if regime == "bull" and (bull_start == 0.0 or bull_age < 1800):
        base = BASE_THRESHOLDS["neutral"]
    else:
        base = BASE_THRESHOLDS.get(regime, BASE_THRESHOLDS["neutral"])

    adj = learner.threshold_adj
    t = shadow.tuning

    return {
        "rocket": round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX - 0.5, base["rocket"] + adj + t.get("nudge_rocket", 0.0))), 2),
        "sniper": round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX - 0.5, base["sniper"] + adj + t.get("nudge_sniper", 0.0))), 2),
        "reversal": round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX - 0.5, base["reversal"] + adj + t.get("nudge_reversal", 0.0))), 2),
    }


def is_tradable(symbol):
    if not symbol.endswith("USDT"):
        return False
    base = symbol[:-4]
    if base in STABLE_BASES:
        return False
    if base.endswith(("3L", "3S", "5L", "5S")):
        return False
    return True


def is_post_pump_dump(candles_15m: list) -> tuple:
    """
    Блокирует покупку падающего ножа сразу после вертикального пампа,
    НО снимает блокировку, если монета уже отстоялась в узкой горизонтальной базе >= 2 часов
    и вернулась над EMA 21 (кейс ICNT перед вторичным мега-пампом).
    """
    if not candles_15m or len(candles_15m) < 20:
        return False, ""
    recent = candles_15m[-16:]

    high_val = -1.0
    high_idx = -1
    low_val = float("inf")
    low_idx = -1

    for idx, c in enumerate(recent):
        if c["high"] > high_val:
            high_val = c["high"]
            high_idx = idx
        if c["low"] < low_val:
            low_val = c["low"]
            low_idx = idx

    if low_idx < high_idx and low_val > 0:
        pump_magnitude = (high_val - low_val) / low_val * 100
        if pump_magnitude >= 12.0 and high_idx <= len(recent) - 2:
            last_close = recent[-1]["close"]
            dump_depth = (high_val - last_close) / (high_val - low_val)
            if dump_depth >= 0.40:
