                # Проверяем, не сформировалась ли уже свежая 2-часовая база накопления над EMA21 (кейс ICNT)
                closes_all = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]
                e21_now = ema(closes_all, 21)[-1]
                last_8 = recent[-8:]
                box_8 = (max(c["high"] for c in last_8) - min(c["low"] for c in last_8)) / last_close if last_close > 0 else 1.0
                if high_idx <= 6 and box_8 <= 0.038 and last_close >= e21_now:
                    return False, ""
                return True, f"пост-памп дамп (+{pump_magnitude:.1f}%, слив {dump_depth*100:.0f}%)"

    return False, ""


def validate_1h_structure(candles_1h: list, nearest_sup: float, sup_touches: int, a15: float, candles_15m: list = None) -> tuple:
    """
    Матрица 48-часовой валидации часового графика (1H Gatekeeper за 2 суток = 48 свечей):
    Возвращает (gate_status, e21_1h, e50_1h, reason_1h, chg_48h, pos_in_48h)
    - 🟢 '1H_TREND_OK': Устойчивый 48ч аптренд, цена НАД растущей 1H EMA21 и EMA50, в рабочей зоне (35%..72% диапазона 48ч, рост за 48ч <= +16%).
    - 🟡 '1H_SUPPORT_ONLY': Опора на подтвержденную часовую плиту поддержки (вес >= 3) ИЛИ подтвержденный разворот W-базы в нижних 30% 48ч-диапазона (кейс UNI/RECALL в 20:00). Разрешен ТОЛЬКО reversal 🧲!
    - 🔴 '1H_BAN':
        А) Падение под падающей 1H EMA21/50 без плиты (кейсы UNI днем, RECALL днем, TIA #1).
        Б) Перекупленная «крыша» 48ч-диапазона (> 72%) или перегрев > +16% за 48ч (кейсы ERA, KMNO, MINA, EIGEN).
        В) Остывание сразу после вертикального часового пампа (ERA-фильтр).
    """
    if not candles_1h or len(candles_1h) < 50:
        return "1H_BAN", 0.0, 0.0, "недостаточно 1H истории (<48ч)", 0.0, 0.5

    closes_1h = [c["close"] for c in candles_1h]
    last_1h = closes_1h[-1]
    e21_series = ema(closes_1h, 21)
    e50_series = ema(closes_1h, 50)
    e21_1h = e21_series[-1]
    e50_1h = e50_series[-1]

    window_48h = candles_1h[-48:]
    open_48h = window_48h[0]["open"]
    chg_48h = ((last_1h - open_48h) / open_48h * 100) if open_48h > 0 else 0.0
    high_48h = max(c["high"] for c in window_48h)
    low_48h = min(c["low"] for c in window_48h)
    pos_in_48h = ((last_1h - low_48h) / (high_48h - low_48h)) if high_48h > low_48h else 0.5

    e21_3h_ago = e21_series[-4] if len(e21_series) >= 4 else e21_series[0]
    e50_12h_ago = e50_series[-13] if len(e50_series) >= 13 else e50_series[0]
    e50_24h_ago = e50_series[-25] if len(e50_series) >= 25 else e50_series[0]

    r_1h = rsi(closes_1h)
    a_1h = atr(candles_1h)

    # Проверка В: Остывание после свечи часовой кульминации за последние 8ч (ERA / GLMR фильтр)
    recent_8_1h = candles_1h[-8:]
    max_h_8 = max(c["high"] for c in recent_8_1h)
    min_l_8 = min(c["low"] for c in recent_8_1h)
    if min_l_8 > 0 and ((max_h_8 - min_l_8) / min_l_8 * 100) >= 10.0:
        pullback_from_peak = (max_h_8 - last_1h) / (max_h_8 - min_l_8) if max_h_8 > min_l_8 else 0.0
        # Если после вертикального выстрела идет серия красных часовых свечей или уход под 1H EMA21 — жесткий БАН
        if (0.20 <= pullback_from_peak <= 0.85) and (candles_1h[-1]["close"] < candles_1h[-1]["open"] or last_1h < e21_1h):
            return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H остывание после пампа (48ч {chg_48h:+.1f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    e21_falling_1h = (e21_1h < e21_3h_ago * 0.9985) and (last_1h < e21_1h)
    e50_rising_48h = (e50_1h >= e50_12h_ago * 0.999) and (e50_1h >= e50_24h_ago * 0.996)
    e50_falling_48h = (e50_1h < e50_12h_ago * 0.997) or (e50_1h < e50_24h_ago * 0.992)

    # Проверка А: Устойчивый 48-часовой аптренд в рабочей зоне (35%..72% диапазона, без покупки крыши!)
    is_uptrend_1h = (
        last_1h >= e50_1h and
        last_1h >= e21_1h * 0.998 and
        e21_1h >= e50_1h * 0.998 and
        not e21_falling_1h and
        not e50_falling_48h and
        0.35 <= pos_in_48h <= 0.72 and
        chg_48h <= 16.5 and
        r_1h <= 72
    )
    if is_uptrend_1h:
        return "1H_TREND_OK", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H аптренд 48ч ({chg_48h:+.1f}%, диап. {pos_in_48h*100:.0f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    # Если цена забралась на крышу 48ч-диапазона (> 72%) или перегрета за 48ч (> +16.5%) — блокируем покупку хаёв
    if pos_in_48h > 0.72 or chg_48h > 16.5:
        return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H крыша/перегрев 48ч ({chg_48h:+.1f}%, диап. {pos_in_48h*100:.0f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    # Проверка Б: Подтвержденная часовая плита поддержки ИЛИ разворот со дна 48ч-диапазона (кейс UNI/RECALL в 20:00)
    has_strong_slab = bool(nearest_sup and sup_touches >= 3 and a15 > 0 and 0 <= (last_1h - nearest_sup) <= 2.2 * a15)
    has_moderate_sup = bool(nearest_sup and sup_touches >= 2 and a15 > 0 and 0 <= (last_1h - nearest_sup) <= 1.5 * a15)

    # Проверка разворота со дна 48ч-канала (pos_in_48h <= 0.30) с возвратом над 15m EMA21
    bottom_reclaim = False
    if pos_in_48h <= 0.30 and has_moderate_sup and candles_15m and len(candles_15m) >= 25:
        c15_closes = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]
        e21_15m = ema(c15_closes, 21)[-1]
        if c15_closes[-1] >= e21_15m and candles_15m[-2]["close"] > candles_15m[-2]["open"]:
            bottom_reclaim = True

    if last_1h < e50_1h and e50_falling_48h:
        dist_below_e50_1h = (e50_1h - last_1h) / a_1h if a_1h > 0 else 0.0
        if (has_strong_slab or bottom_reclaim) and dist_below_e50_1h <= 3.0 and pos_in_48h <= 0.50:
            tag_str = "разворот со дна 48ч" if bottom_reclaim else f"1H плита в даунтренде ({sup_touches}x)"
            return "1H_SUPPORT_ONLY", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"{tag_str} (48ч {chg_48h:+.1f}%, диап. {pos_in_48h*100:.0f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)
        return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H 48ч даунтренд без плиты ({chg_48h:+.1f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    if (has_strong_slab or has_moderate_sup or bottom_reclaim) and pos_in_48h <= 0.55:
        return "1H_SUPPORT_ONLY", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H опора на плиту ({sup_touches}x, диап. {pos_in_48h*100:.0f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H нет тренда и нет плиты за 48ч ({chg_48h:+.1f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)


def raw_max_score(regime):
    """Реалистичный потолок сырых баллов (индикаторы + контекст + паттерн + структура S/R)."""
    m = sum(learner.weight(k) for k in ("ema50", "ema21", "impulse", "rsi", "volume", "chg24h"))
    m += max(learner.weight("indep"), learner.weight("news_pos"), learner.weight("hype"))
    m += 6.2
    return max(13.5, m)


def _returns(closes):
    return [(b - a) / a for a, b in zip(closes, closes[1:]) if a]


def _corr(a, b):
    n = min(len(a), len(b))
    if n < 10:
        return 0.0
    a, b = a[-n:], b[-n:]
    ma, mb = sum(a) / n, sum(b) / n
    cov = sum((x - ma) * (y - mb) for x, y in zip(a, b))
    va = sum((x - ma) ** 2 for x in a)
    vb = sum((y - mb) ** 2 for y in b)
    if va <= 0 or vb <= 0:
        return 0.0
    return cov / (va * vb) ** 0.5


def _corr_candles(candles_a: list, candles_b: list) -> float:
    """
    Считает корреляцию доходностей строго по совпадающим временным меткам (ts) ЗАКРЫТЫХ свечей.
    Полностью исключает фантомный скачок корреляции в минус на границе смены 15-минуток (аномалия 14:30 UTC).
    """
    if not candles_a or not candles_b or len(candles_a) < 15 or len(candles_b) < 15:
        return 0.0
    closed_a = candles_a[:-1]
    closed_b = candles_b[:-1]

    ret_a = {}
    for i in range(1, len(closed_a)):
        p0 = closed_a[i - 1]["close"]
        if p0 > 0:
            ret_a[closed_a[i]["ts"]] = (closed_a[i]["close"] - p0) / p0

    ret_b = {}
    for i in range(1, len(closed_b)):
        p0 = closed_b[i - 1]["close"]
        if p0 > 0:
            ret_b[closed_b[i]["ts"]] = (closed_b[i]["close"] - p0) / p0

    common_ts = sorted(set(ret_a.keys()) & set(ret_b.keys()))
    if len(common_ts) < 12:
        return _corr(_returns([c["close"] for c in closed_a]), _returns([c["close"] for c in closed_b]))

    common_ts = common_ts[-96:]
    return _corr([ret_a[t] for t in common_ts], [ret_b[t] for t in common_ts])


async def get_regime():
    now = time.time()
    _init_regime_tracker()

    candles = await market_data.get_kline("BTCUSDT", "60", 250)
    if len(candles) < 60:
        return _regime_tracker.get("prev_regime", "neutral"), {}
    closes = [c["close"] for c in candles]
    e21, e50, e200 = ema(closes, 21)[-1], ema(closes, 50)[-1], ema(closes, 200)[-1]
    last = closes[-1]

    if e21 > e50 > e200:
        raw_regime = "bull"
    elif e21 < e50 < e200:
        raw_regime = "bear"
    else:
        raw_regime = "neutral"

    breadth_bull = False
    tickers = await market_data.get_tickers()
    if tickers:
        tradable = [s for s, t in tickers.items() if is_tradable(s) and t["quote_volume"] >= 500_000]
        if tradable:
            green_alts = sum(1 for s in tradable if tickers[s]["change_pct"] > 3.0)
            breadth_pct = (green_alts / len(tradable)) * 100

            if breadth_pct >= 45.0:
                _regime_tracker["breadth_streak"] += 1
                if _regime_tracker["breadth_streak"] >= 2:
                    breadth_bull = True
                    if raw_regime == "neutral":
                        raw_regime = "bull"
                        logger.info(f"Market Breadth: {breadth_pct:.1f}% альтов зеленые (подтверждено 2-й цикл). Переход в 'bull'.")
                elif _regime_tracker["breadth_streak"] == 1 and raw_regime == "neutral":
                    logger.info(f"Market Breadth: {breadth_pct:.1f}% альтов зеленые (цикл 1/2). Ожидание подтверждения для BULL.")
            else:
                _regime_tracker["breadth_streak"] = 0

    stable_trend = get_macro_trend()
    fng = get_fear_and_greed()

    if stable_trend == "bull" and raw_regime == "neutral":
        raw_regime = "bull"
    elif stable_trend == "bear" and raw_regime == "bull" and not breadth_bull:
        raw_regime = "neutral"

    if fng < 35 and raw_regime == "bull":
        raw_regime = "neutral"
    elif fng > 78 and raw_regime == "bear":
        raw_regime = "neutral"

    regime = raw_regime

    if regime == "bear":
        _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"] = now
        _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] = 0.0
    elif regime == "bull":
        time_since_bear = now - _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"]
        if _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"] > 0 and time_since_bear < 2700:
            rem_min = (2700 - time_since_bear) / 60
            regime = "neutral"
            logger.info(f"🛡 Гистерезис: выход из BEAR заблокировал прямой прыжок в BULL. Буфер NEUTRAL еще {rem_min:.1f} мин.")
        else:
            if _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] == 0.0:
                _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] = now
                logger.info("🚀 Подтвержден старт фазы BULL. Запущен 30-минутный прогрев порогов.")
    else:
        _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] = 0.0

    _regime_tracker["prev_regime"] = regime
    return regime, {"btc": last, "ema50": e50, "ema200": e200}


async def fetch_new_listings():
    now = time.time()
    if _instruments_cache["data"] is not None and now - _instruments_cache["ts"] < 3600:
        raw = _instruments_cache["data"]
    else:
        raw = []
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15) as c:
                cursor = ""
                for page in range(5):
                    params = {"category": "spot", "limit": 1000}
                    if cursor:
                        params["cursor"] = cursor
                    r = await c.get("https://api.bybit.com/v5/market/instruments-info", params=params)
                    if r.status_code != 200:
                        break
                    data = r.json()
                    result = data.get("result", {})
                    items = result.get("list", [])
                    if not items:
                        break
                    raw += items
                    cursor = result.get("nextPageCursor", "") or ""
                    if not cursor:
                        break
            _instruments_cache["data"], _instruments_cache["ts"] = raw, now
        except Exception:
            raw = []

    now_ms = int(now * 1000)
    out = []
    for ins in raw:
        sym = ins.get("symbol", "")
        if not sym.endswith("USDT") or ins.get("status", "") != "Trading":
            continue
        try:
            launch = int(ins.get("launchTime", 0) or 0)
        except Exception:
            launch = 0
        if launch > 0:
            out.append((sym, (now_ms - launch) / 3600000))
    return out


def score_symbol(candles_15m, candles_1h, t, regime, is_open_pos=False, saved_patterns=None, saved_mode=None):
    closes = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]
    last = closes[-1]
    e21, e50 = ema(closes, 21)[-1], ema(closes, 50)[-1]
    e12, e26 = ema(closes, 12)[-1], ema(closes, 26)[-1]
    r = rsi(closes)
    a15 = atr(candles_15m)

    vols = [c["volume"] for c in candles_15m]
    base_vol = sum(vols[-22:-2]) / 20 if len(vols) > 22 else (sum(vols) / max(1, len(vols)))
    eff_vol = max(vols[-1], vols[-2]) if len(vols) >= 2 else vols[-1]
    vol_ratio = eff_vol / base_vol if base_vol else 1.0

    prior_candles = candles_15m[-26:-2] if len(candles_15m) >= 26 else candles_15m[:-1]
    recent_high = max(c["high"] for c in prior_candles) if prior_candles else last
    recent_low_2h = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-8:]) if len(candles_15m) >= 8 else min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m)
    recent_low_6 = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-6:]) if len(candles_15m) >= 6 else recent_low_2h

    w = shadow.signal_windows()
    score, reasons, keys = 0.0, [], []

    # 1. Трендовый контекст
    if last > e50:
        score += learner.weight("ema50")
        reasons.append("цена выше EMA50")
        keys.append("ema50")
    if e21 > e50:
        score += learner.weight("ema21")
        reasons.append("EMA21>EMA50")
        keys.append("ema21")
    if e12 > e26:
        score += learner.weight("impulse")
        reasons.append("импульс роста")
        keys.append("impulse")

    # 2. RSI без слепых зон
    if r < 38:
        score += learner.weight("rsi")
        reasons.append(f"перепроданность RSI {r:.0f}")
        keys.append("rsi")
    elif 38 <= r <= w["rsi_hi"]:
        score += learner.weight("rsi")
        reasons.append(f"RSI {r:.0f}")
        keys.append("rsi")
    elif r > w["rsi_hi"]:
        score -= 1.5
        reasons.append(f"перегрев RSI {r:.0f}")

    # 3. Объем
    last_candle = candles_15m[-1]
    prev_candle = candles_15m[-2]
    is_green = last_candle["close"] >= last_candle["open"] or (vols[-2] > vols[-1] and prev_candle["close"] >= prev_candle["open"])
    c_range = last_candle["high"] - last_candle["low"]
    lower_wick = min(last_candle["open"], last_candle["close"]) - last_candle["low"]
    is_panic_dip = (t["change_pct"] <= -6.0 or r < 38)

    if vol_ratio > w["vol_lo"]:
        has_bottom_wick = c_range > 0 and (lower_wick / c_range) >= 0.40
        if is_green or has_bottom_wick:
            score += learner.weight("volume")
            reasons.append(f"объём покупок x{vol_ratio:.1f}")
            keys.append("volume")
        elif not is_panic_dip:
            score -= 2.0
            reasons.append(f"сливной объём x{vol_ratio:.1f} (давление продаж)")

    # Hard Veto на шпильку продаж на хаях (кейс NEAR -1.64%)
    recent_rejection = False
    for c in candles_15m[-3:]:
        c_rng = c["high"] - c["low"]
        c_w = c["high"] - max(c["close"], c["open"])
        if c_rng > 0 and (c_w / c_rng) > 0.45 and c["high"] >= recent_high * 0.996:
            recent_rejection = True
            break
    if recent_rejection and not is_open_pos:
        score -= 4.5
        reasons.append("🚫 отвержение на хаях (шпилька продаж)")

    if not is_open_pos and a15 > 0 and e21 > 0:
        dist_from_ema = last - e21
        if dist_from_ema > 2.0 * a15:
            score -= 4.0
            reasons.append(f"FOMO-перегрев (+{dist_from_ema/a15:.1f} ATR от EMA21)")

    # 4. Триггеры Price Action
    detected_patterns = detect_patterns(candles_15m, a15, e50, e21, recent_high)
    matched_modes = []
    pattern_keys = []
    wick_entry_price = None

    for pat in detected_patterns:
        pkey = pat["key"]
        p_bonus = pat["base_bonus"] * learner.pattern_multiplier(pkey)
        score += p_bonus
        reasons.append(f"{pat['name']} (+{p_bonus:.1f})")
        pattern_keys.append(pkey)
        matched_modes.append(pat["mode"])
        if pat.get("wick_entry_price") and wick_entry_price is None:
            wick_entry_price = pat["wick_entry_price"]

    if saved_patterns:
        base_bonuses = {"pinbar": 2.5, "divergence": 2.5, "vcp": 3.0, "breakout": 3.0, "engulfing": 2.0}
        for sp in saved_patterns:
            if sp not in pattern_keys:
                p_bonus = base_bonuses.get(sp, 2.5) * learner.pattern_multiplier(sp)
                score += p_bonus
                pattern_keys.append(sp)
        if saved_mode and saved_mode not in matched_modes:
            matched_modes.append(saved_mode)

    # 5. Динамика 24h + локальный дисконт от свинга
    local_swing_drop = (recent_high - last) / recent_high * 100 if recent_high > last else 0.0
    if 0 < t["change_pct"] < w["chg_hi"]:
        score += learner.weight("chg24h")
        reasons.append(f"24ч +{t['change_pct']:.1f}%")
        keys.append("chg24h")
    elif (t["change_pct"] <= -5.0 or local_swing_drop >= 3.0) and ("reversal" in matched_modes or r < 40):
        score += learner.weight("chg24h")
        reasons.append(f"дисконт от свинга -{local_swing_drop:.1f}%")
        keys.append("chg24h")
    elif t["change_pct"] >= w["chg_hi"]:
        score -= 1.0
        reasons.append(f"памп +{t['change_pct']:.1f}% (уже поздно)")

    if t["quote_volume"] < 500_000:
        score -= 1.0

    # 6. Уровни S/R и Multi-Touch Support Bonus
    nearest_res, res_touches, nearest_sup, sup_touches = find_sr_levels(candles_15m, candles_1h)

    if nearest_res and res_touches >= 4 and a15 > 0 and not is_open_pos:
        dist_to_res = nearest_res - last
        if dist_to_res < 0.5 * a15 or (dist_to_res / last * 100) < 0.35:
            score -= 3.5
            reasons.append(f"в упор к капитальной стене ${fmt_price(nearest_res)} ({res_touches}x)")

    sup_is_near = False
    if nearest_sup and sup_touches >= 2 and a15 > 0:
        dist_to_sup = last - nearest_sup
        if 0 <= dist_to_sup <= 2.2 * a15:
            sup_is_near = True
            if not is_open_pos and (last > closes[-2] or "reversal" in matched_modes or sup_touches >= 3):
                sup_bonus = 2.6 if sup_touches >= 3 else 1.4
                score += sup_bonus
                reasons.append(f"плита поддержки ${fmt_price(nearest_sup)} ({sup_touches}x, +{sup_bonus:.1f})")
                keys.append("support_bounce")
                if sup_touches >= 3 and "reversal" not in matched_modes and last < e50:
                    matched_modes.append("reversal")

    # 7. Матрица 48-часовой валидации 1H (1H Gatekeeper)
    gate_1h, e21_1h, e50_1h, gate_reason, chg_48h, pos_in_48h = validate_1h_structure(
        candles_1h, nearest_sup, sup_touches, a15, candles_15m=candles_15m
    )

    # Запрет контртрендового reversal на перегретых 48ч-пампах (кейс ICNT +64% и TIA #1 +30%)
    if chg_48h > 18.0 or pos_in_48h > 0.55:
        matched_modes = [m for m in matched_modes if m != "reversal"]

    if candles_1h and len(candles_1h) >= 50:
        closes_1h = [c["close"] for c in candles_1h]
        r_1h = rsi(closes_1h)

        if gate_1h == "1H_TREND_OK" and (r_1h < 52 or abs(last - e50) <= 1.2 * a15) and e12 >= e26 * 0.999:
            score += 1.8
            reasons.append("MTF: выкуп отката в 48ч 1H аптренде (+1.8)")
            keys.append("mtf_dip")
        elif gate_1h == "1H_BAN" and not is_open_pos:
            score -= 5.0
            reasons.append(f"🚫 {gate_reason}")

        # В режиме 1H_SUPPORT_ONLY разрешен ТОЛЬКО reversal от плиты!
        if gate_1h == "1H_SUPPORT_ONLY":
            matched_modes = [m for m in matched_modes if m == "reversal"]
            if not matched_modes and sup_is_near and sup_touches >= 2 and (r <= 46 or pos_in_48h <= 0.30):
                matched_modes = ["reversal"]

    has_structure = (
        sup_is_near or
        len(pattern_keys) > 0 or
        ("support_bounce" in keys) or
        ("mtf_dip" in keys)
    )

    confirmed_res, confirmed_sup = get_all_sr_levels(candles_15m, candles_1h)

    signal_values = {
        "rsi": round(r, 1), "chg24h": round(t["change_pct"], 2), "volume": round(vol_ratio, 2),
        "nearest_res": nearest_res, "res_touches": res_touches,
        "nearest_sup": nearest_sup, "sup_touches": sup_touches,
        "all_res": confirmed_res,
        "all_sup": confirmed_sup,
        "recent_high": recent_high,
        "recent_low_2h": recent_low_2h,
        "recent_low_6": recent_low_6,
        "e21": e21, "e50": e50,
        "e21_1h": e21_1h, "e50_1h": e50_1h,
        "gate_1h": gate_1h,
        "reason_1h": gate_reason,
        "chg_48h": chg_48h,
        "pos_in_48h": pos_in_48h,
        "recent_rejection": recent_rejection,
        "wick_entry_price": wick_entry_price,
        "matched_modes": matched_modes,
        "patterns": pattern_keys,
        "has_structure": has_structure,
    }
    return score, reasons, keys, signal_values


def normalize(raw, regime):
    rm = raw_max_score(regime)
    if rm <= 0:
        return 0.0
    return round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX, raw / rm * SCORE_MAX)), 2)


async def live_score(sym, t, regime, btc_ret, news_items=None, deriv_t=None, is_open_pos=False, saved_patterns=None, saved_mode=None, btc_candles=None):
    candles_15m = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "15", 120)
    candles_1h = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "60", 250)

    if len(candles_15m) < 60 or len(candles_1h) < 60:
        return None, candles_15m

    raw, _, keys_live, sv_live = score_symbol(
        candles_15m, candles_1h, t, regime,
        is_open_pos=is_open_pos,
        saved_patterns=saved_patterns,
        saved_mode=saved_mode
    )

    base = sym[:-4]
    sectors_map = await get_sectors_for_pool([base])
    ranks_map = await get_ranks_for_pool([base])
    sector = sectors_map.get(base, "Other")
    tier = tier_of(ranks_map.get(base))

    if btc_candles:
        corr = _corr_candles(candles_15m, btc_candles)
    elif isinstance(btc_ret, list) and btc_ret and isinstance(btc_ret[0], dict):
        corr = _corr_candles(candles_15m, btc_ret)
    else:
        corr = _corr(_returns([c["close"] for c in candles_15m[:-1]]), btc_ret[:-1] if btc_ret else [])

    tier_corr_limit = TIER_CORR_LIMITS.get(tier, 0.82)

    if corr > tier_corr_limit and regime == "neutral":
        raw -= 1
    elif abs(corr) < 0.25:
        raw += learner.weight("indep") * 1.3
    elif corr < 0.45:
        raw += learner.weight("indep")

    fng = get_fear_and_greed()
    if fng >= 80:
        raw -= 0.5
    elif fng <= 25:
        raw += 0.5

    if is_sector_hot(sector):
        raw += 0.5

    sb = learner.sector_bias(sector)
    if sb:
        raw += sb
    tb = learner.tier_bias(tier)
    if tb:
        raw += tb

    if news_items is not None:
        name = await get_coin_name(base)
        neg, pos_news, mentions, _ = check_sentiment(news_items, [base, name])
        if pos_news > neg:
            raw += learner.weight("news_pos")
        elif mentions >= 2:
            raw += learner.weight("hype")

    score10 = normalize(raw, regime)

    modes = sv_live.get("matched_modes", [])
    if "reversal" in modes:
        entry_mode_live = "reversal"
    elif "rocket" in modes:
        entry_mode_live = "rocket"
    else:
        entry_mode_live = "sniper"

    if deriv_t:
        funding = deriv_t.get("funding", 0)
        if funding > 0.05 and entry_mode_live == "rocket":
            score10 -= 0.5
        elif funding < -0.01:
            score10 += 0.5

    is_sqz = await check_coinglass_liquidation_threat(sym)
    if is_sqz:
        score10 -= 1.5

    score10 = round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX, score10)), 2)
    return score10, candles_15m


async def scan(regime, tickers, deriv_tickers, limit=20):
    tradable = [s for s, t in tickers.items() if is_tradable(s) and t["quote_volume"] >= 200_000 and t["last"] > 0]

    by_vol = sorted(tradable, key=lambda s: tickers[s]["quote_volume"], reverse=True)[:40]
    by_chg = sorted([s for s in tradable if 0 < tickers[s]["change_pct"] < 25], key=lambda s: tickers[s]["change_pct"], reverse=True)[:20]

    by_momentum = []
    for sym in tradable[:50]:
        candles = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "60", 168)
        if len(candles) >= 100:
            chg_7d = (candles[-1]["close"] - candles[0]["close"]) / candles[0]["close"] * 100
            if 5 < chg_7d < 50:
                by_momentum.append((sym, chg_7d))
    by_momentum = [s for s, _ in sorted(by_momentum, key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)][:15]

    by_volatility = []
    for sym in tradable[:50]:
        candles = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "15", 60)
        if len(candles) >= 30:
            a = atr(candles)
            last = candles[-1]["close"]
            atr_pct = (a / last) * 100 if last else 0
            if atr_pct > 2.5:
                by_volatility.append((sym, atr_pct))
    by_volatility = [s for s, _ in sorted(by_volatility, key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)][:10]

    by_dip = sorted([s for s in tradable if tickers[s]["change_pct"] <= -6.0], key=lambda s: tickers[s]["change_pct"])[:15]

    sources = await fetch_new_listings()
    try:
        rss = await fetch_listings_cache()
        now_ts = int(time.time())
        sources += [(l["symbol"], (now_ts - l["ts"]) / 3600) for l in rss]
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"RSS listings unavailable: {e}")

    by_listings = []
    seen = set()
    for sym, age_h in sorted(sources, key=lambda x: x[1]):
        if sym in seen or not (24 <= age_h <= 336):
            continue
        seen.add(sym)
        if sym in tickers and is_tradable(sym) and tickers[sym]["quote_volume"] >= 500_000:
            by_listings.append((sym, age_h))
    by_listings = by_listings[:10]

    pool = list(dict.fromkeys(by_vol + by_chg + by_momentum + by_volatility + by_dip + [s for s, _ in by_listings]))
    pool_bases = list({s[:-4] for s in pool})

    sectors_map = await get_sectors_for_pool(pool_bases)
    ranks_map = await get_ranks_for_pool(pool_bases)
    await fetch_missing_names(pool_bases)

    news_items = await fetch_news_cache()

    btc_candles = await market_data.get_kline("BTCUSDT", "15", 120)

    raw_max = raw_max_score(regime)
    fng = get_fear_and_greed()

    if not paper.market_history or paper.market_history[-1].get("regime") != regime:
        paper.market_history.append({
            "ts": int(time.time()),
            "regime": regime,
            "mode": getattr(learner, "current_risk_mode", "NORMAL")
        })
        paper.market_history = paper.market_history[-500:]
        paper.save()

    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(10)

    async def _analyze_sym(sym):
        async with sem:
            candles_15m = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "15", 120)
            candles_1h = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "60", 250)

            if len(candles_15m) < 60 or len(candles_1h) < 60:
                return None

            is_post_pump, _ = is_post_pump_dump(candles_15m)
            if is_post_pump:
                return None

            a = atr(candles_15m)
            last_price = tickers[sym]["last"]
            atr_pct = (a / last_price) * 100 if last_price else 0
            if a <= 0 or atr_pct < 0.25:
                return None

            score, reasons, keys, signal_values = score_symbol(candles_15m, candles_1h, tickers[sym], regime)

            gate_1h = signal_values.get("gate_1h", "1H_BAN")
            if gate_1h == "1H_BAN":
                return None

            if not signal_values.get("has_structure"):
                return None

            modes = signal_values.get("matched_modes", [])
            if gate_1h == "1H_SUPPORT_ONLY":
                if "reversal" not in modes:
                    return None
                entry_mode = "reversal"
            else:
                if "reversal" in modes:
                    entry_mode = "reversal"
                elif "rocket" in modes:
                    entry_mode = "rocket"
                else:
                    entry_mode = "sniper"

            # Hard Veto 1: Шпилька продаж на хаях блокирует трендовые входы sniper и rocket (кейс NEAR)
            if signal_values.get("recent_rejection") and entry_mode in ("sniper", "rocket"):
                return None

            base = sym[:-4]
            sector = sectors_map.get(base, "Other")
            tier = tier_of(ranks_map.get(base))

            # Точная синхронизированная по timestamp корреляция с BTC
            corr = _corr_candles(candles_15m, btc_candles)
            tier_corr_limit = TIER_CORR_LIMITS.get(tier, 0.82)

            # Hard Veto 2: В медвежьем рынке (BEAR) запрещаем трендовые sniper/rocket по монетам-зеркалам BTC (corr >= 0.65)
            if regime == "bear" and entry_mode in ("sniper", "rocket") and corr >= 0.65:
                return None

            is_independent_mover = abs(corr) < 0.25 and gate_1h == "1H_TREND_OK"

            if corr > tier_corr_limit and regime in ("neutral", "bear") and entry_mode != "reversal":
                score -= 1.2
                reasons.append(f"зеркало BTC ({tier}: corr {corr:.2f})")
            elif is_independent_mover:
                score += learner.weight("indep") * 1.3
                reasons.append(f"иммунитет от BTC (corr {corr:.2f})")
                keys.append("indep")
            elif corr < 0.45:
                score += learner.weight("indep")
                reasons.append(f"независима от BTC (corr {corr:.2f})")
                keys.append("indep")

            if fng >= 80:
                score -= 0.5
                reasons.append(f"F&G перегрев ({fng}): -0.5")
            elif fng <= 25:
                score += 0.5
                reasons.append(f"F&G страх ({fng}): +0.5")

            sb = learner.sector_bias(sector)
            if sb:
                score += sb
                reasons.append(f"сектор {sector}: {sb:+.2f}")

            if is_sector_hot(sector):
                score += 0.5
                reasons.append("TVL приток (DefiLlama): +0.5")

            tb = learner.tier_bias(tier)
            if tb:
                score += tb
                reasons.append(f"тир {TIER_EMOJI[tier]}: {tb:+.2f}")

            name = await get_coin_name(base)
            neg, pos_news, mentions, _ = check_sentiment(news_items, [base, name])

            is_toxic = neg >= 2 and neg >= (pos_news * 2) and neg >= (mentions * 0.33)
            if is_toxic:
                FILTERED_BY_NEWS[:] = [item for item in FILTERED_BY_NEWS if item["symbol"] != sym]
                FILTERED_BY_NEWS.append({"symbol": sym, "neg_count": f"{neg}/{mentions}", "time": int(time.time())})
                FILTERED_BY_NEWS[:] = FILTERED_BY_NEWS[-10:]
                return None

            if pos_news > neg:
                score += learner.weight("news_pos")
                reasons.append(f"позитивный новостной фон ({pos_news})")
                keys.append("news_pos")
            elif mentions >= 2:
                score += learner.weight("hype")
                reasons.append(f"медиа-хайп ({mentions} упом.)")
                keys.append("hype")

            score10 = (score / raw_max * SCORE_MAX) if raw_max > 0 else 0.0

            deriv_t = deriv_tickers.get(sym)
            if deriv_t:
                funding = deriv_t.get("funding", 0)
                if funding > 0.05 and entry_mode == "rocket":
                    score10 -= 0.5
                    reasons.append("перегрев лонгов Фьюч (-0.5)")
                elif funding < -0.01:
                    score10 += 0.5
                    reasons.append("шорт-сквиз потенциал Фьюч (+0.5)")

            is_sqz = await check_coinglass_liquidation_threat(sym)
            if is_sqz:
                score10 -= 1.5
                reasons.append("риск ликвидаций Coinglass (-1.5)")

            score10 = round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX, score10)), 2)

            return {
                "symbol": sym, "score": score10, "reasons": reasons,
                "reason_keys": keys, "atr": a, "last": last_price,
                "liquidity": tickers[sym]["quote_volume"],
                "corr": round(corr, 2), "atr_pct": round(atr_pct, 2),
                "sector": sector, "tier": tier,
                "signal_values": signal_values,
                "patterns": signal_values.get("patterns", []),
                "is_momentum": (entry_mode == "rocket"),
                "entry_mode": entry_mode,
                "nearest_res": signal_values.get("nearest_res"),
                "res_touches": signal_values.get("res_touches", 0),
                "nearest_sup": signal_values.get("nearest_sup"),
                "sup_touches": signal_values.get("sup_touches", 0),
                "recent_high": signal_values.get("recent_high"),
                "recent_low_2h": signal_values.get("recent_low_2h"),
                "recent_low_6": signal_values.get("recent_low_6"),
                "e21": signal_values.get("e21"),
                "e50": signal_values.get("e50"),
                "e21_1h": signal_values.get("e21_1h"),
                "e50_1h": signal_values.get("e50_1h"),
                "gate_1h": gate_1h,
                "reason_1h": signal_values.get("reason_1h", ""),
                "chg_48h": signal_values.get("chg_48h", 0.0),
                "pos_in_48h": signal_values.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5),
                "wick_entry_price": signal_values.get("wick_entry_price"),
                "is_independent": is_independent_mover,
                "size_mult": 1.0,
            }

    tasks = [_analyze_sym(sym) for sym in pool]
    results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)

    scored = [r for r in results if r is not None]
    scored.sort(key=lambda c: c["score"], reverse=True)

    thrs = get_thresholds(regime)
    neutral_thrs = get_thresholds("neutral")

    try:
        shadow.observe(scored, regime, thrs)
        shadow.tick(tickers)
        shadow.autotune()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"shadow error: {e}")

    public_url = os.getenv("RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL", "https://captain-rost-bot.onrender.com")
    parts_html, parts_plain = [], []
    for c in scored[:5]:
        chart_url = f"{public_url}/chart?symbol={c['symbol']}"
        mode = c.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
        eff_thrs = neutral_thrs if (regime == "bear" and c.get("is_independent")) else thrs
        thr = eff_thrs.get(mode, 6.0)
        mode_icon = "🚀" if mode == "rocket" else "🧲" if mode == "reversal" else "🏹"

        parts_html.append(f"{mode_icon} <a href='{chart_url}'><b>{c['symbol'][:-4]}</b></a> {TIER_EMOJI.get(c['tier'], '🐭')} · <i>{c['sector']}</i> · {c['score']:.1f}/{thr:g} · ₿ {c['corr']:.2f}")
        parts_plain.append(f"{c['symbol']} {c['score']:.1f}/{thr:g} [{'+'.join(c['reason_keys'][:5])}] btc{c['corr']:.2f}")

    SCAN_SUMMARY["text"] = " | ".join(parts_html) or "сигналов нет"
    SCAN_SUMMARY["thrs"] = thrs
    SCAN_SUMMARY["ts"] = time.time()
    logger.info(f"Scan top: {' | '.join(parts_plain) or 'сигналов нет'}")

    new_set = set(s for s, _ in by_listings)
    candidates = []
    for c in scored:
        mode = c.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
        eff_thrs = neutral_thrs if (regime == "bear" and c.get("is_independent")) else thrs
        thr = eff_thrs.get(mode, 6.0)
        if c["score"] < thr:
            continue
        c["thr"] = thr
        c["is_new"] = c["symbol"] in new_set
        candidates.append(c)

    return candidates[:limit]

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/scanner.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/shadow.py ---
import os
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

from loguru import logger
from bot.core.remote_state import download_state, upload_state
from bot.strategy.learner import learner

STATE_FILE = Path(os.getenv("STORAGE_DIR", "storage")) / "shadow.json"
REMOTE_PATH = "shadow.json"

OBS_GAP = 2.0
H1 = 3600
H4 = 4 * 3600
H24 = 24 * 3600
PUMP_PCT = 3.0
MAX_EPISODES = 300
COOLDOWN = 24 * 3600


def _band(score):
    if score < 5:
        return "0-5"
    if score < 6:
        return "5-6"
    if score < 7:
        return "6-7"
    if score < 8:
        return "7-8"
    return "8+"


class Shadow:
    """Теневой журнал: наблюдает монеты, использует 4ч оперативный контур + 24ч финал и калибрует пороги."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.episodes = {}
        self.agg = {}
        self.cooldown = {}
        self.tuning = {
            "auto": True,
            "nudge_rocket": 0.0, "nudge_sniper": 0.0, "nudge_reversal": 0.0,
            "hunt": -0.004, "near": -0.0015, "capture": +0.002,
            "sl_mult": 1.0, "tp_mult": 1.0,
            "signal_windows": {"rsi_hi": 90, "chg_hi": 30, "vol_lo": 1.3},
        }
        self._last_upload = 0.0
        self._last_tune = 0.0
        self._load()

    def _load(self):
        data = None
        try:
            if STATE_FILE.exists():
                data = json.loads(STATE_FILE.read_text())
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"shadow load error: {e}")
        if data is None:
            data = download_state(REMOTE_PATH)
        if data:
            self.episodes = data.get("episodes", {})
            self.agg = data.get("agg", {})
            self.cooldown = data.get("cooldown", {})
            self.tuning.update(data.get("tuning", {}))
            logger.info(f"shadow: загружен ({len(self.episodes)} эпизодов)")

    def save(self):
        payload = {"episodes": self.episodes, "agg": self.agg,
                   "cooldown": self.cooldown, "tuning": self.tuning}
        try:
            STATE_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            STATE_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"shadow save error: {e}")
        if time.time() - self._last_upload > 3600:
            self._last_upload = time.time()
            upload_state(REMOTE_PATH, payload)

    def observe(self, scored, regime, thrs):
        from bot.exchange.paper_exchange import paper
        now = time.time()

        for c in scored:
            sym = c["symbol"]
            mode = c.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
            thr = thrs.get(mode, 6.0)
            obs_thr = thr - OBS_GAP

            if sym in self.episodes:
                ep = self.episodes[sym]
                if c["score"] > ep["max_score"]:
                    ep["max_score"] = c["score"]
                continue

            if c["score"] < obs_thr:
                continue
            if now < self.cooldown.get(sym, 0):
                continue
            if len(self.episodes) >= MAX_EPISODES:
                continue

            traded = (sym in paper.positions or any(o["symbol"] == sym for o in paper.orders))

            self.episodes[sym] = {
                "ts": now, "price": c["last"], "last": c["last"],
                "max_score": c["score"],
                "regime": regime, "entry_mode": mode,
                "sector": c.get("sector"), "tier": c.get("tier"),
                "keys": c.get("reason_keys", []), "traded": bool(traded),
                "success": False,
                "hi": c["last"], "lo": c["last"], "p4": None,
                "lo_before_pump": c["last"], "pumped": False,
                "signal_values": c.get("signal_values", {}),
            }

    def mark_success(self, sym):
        if sym in self.episodes:
            self.episodes[sym]["success"] = True
            self.save()

    def tick(self, tickers):
        now = time.time()
        for sym, ep in list(self.episodes.items()):
            t = tickers.get(sym)
            if not t:
                continue
            last = t["last"]
            ep["last"] = last
            ep["hi"] = max(ep["hi"], last)
            ep["lo"] = min(ep["lo"], last)

            if not ep.get("pumped"):
                ep["lo_before_pump"] = min(ep.get("lo_before_pump", last), last)
                if last >= ep["price"] * (1 + PUMP_PCT / 100):
                    ep["pumped"] = True

            age = now - ep["ts"]
            if ep["p4"] is None and age >= H4:
                ep["p4"] = last
            if age >= H24:
                self._close(sym, ep, last)
        self.save()

    def _combined_agg(self) -> dict:
        """
        Объединяет закрытую 24ч статистику (self.agg) с оперативным контуром активных эпизодов
        старше 1 часа (age >= 3600), чтобы автотюн и /learn работали уже в первые часы после сброса!
        """
        merged = {}
        for k, v in self.agg.items():
            merged[k] = {
                "n": v.get("n", 0),
                "sum4": v.get("sum4", 0.0),
                "sum24": v.get("sum24", 0.0),
                "pumps4": v.get("pumps4", 0),
                "pumps24": v.get("pumps24", 0),
                "traded": v.get("traded", 0),
                "sum_mae": v.get("sum_mae", 0.0),
                "sum_mfe": v.get("sum_mfe", 0.0),
                "sum_pull": v.get("sum_pull", 0.0),
                "sum_pull_pump": v.get("sum_pull_pump", 0.0),
                "sum_mae_pump": v.get("sum_mae_pump", 0.0),
            }

        now = time.time()
        for sym, ep in self.episodes.items():
            age = now - ep.get("ts", now)
            dec = ep.get("price", 0.0)
            if age < H1 or dec <= 0:
                continue
            ref_last = ep.get("p4") or ep.get("last") or dec
            move_cur = (ref_last - dec) / dec * 100
            mae = (ep.get("lo", dec) - dec) / dec * 100
            mfe = (ep.get("hi", dec) - dec) / dec * 100
            pullback = (ep.get("lo_before_pump", ep.get("lo", dec)) - dec) / dec * 100
            did_pump = bool(ep.get("pumped") or mfe >= PUMP_PCT or move_cur >= PUMP_PCT)

            mode = ep.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
            key = f"{ep.get('regime', 'neutral')}|{mode}|{_band(ep.get('max_score', 6.0))}"
            a = merged.setdefault(key, {
                "n": 0, "sum4": 0.0, "sum24": 0.0, "pumps4": 0, "pumps24": 0,
                "traded": 0, "sum_mae": 0.0, "sum_mfe": 0.0, "sum_pull": 0.0,
                "sum_pull_pump": 0.0, "sum_mae_pump": 0.0,
            })
            a["n"] += 1
            a["sum4"] += move_cur
            a["sum24"] += move_cur
            a["sum_mae"] += mae
            a["sum_mfe"] += mfe
            a["sum_pull"] += mae
            if did_pump:
                a["pumps24"] += 1
                a["sum_pull_pump"] += pullback
                a["sum_mae_pump"] += mae
            if ep.get("success"):
                a["traded"] += 1

        return merged

    def _close(self, sym, ep, last):
        dec = ep["price"]
        self.episodes.pop(sym, None)
        self.cooldown[sym] = time.time() + COOLDOWN
        if dec <= 0:
            return

        move24 = (last - dec) / dec * 100
        move4 = ((ep["p4"] or last) - dec) / dec * 100
        mae = (ep["lo"] - dec) / dec * 100
        mfe = (ep["hi"] - dec) / dec * 100
        pullback = (ep.get("lo_before_pump", ep["lo"]) - dec) / dec * 100

        mode = ep.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
        key = f"{ep['regime']}|{mode}|{_band(ep['max_score'])}"

        a = self.agg.setdefault(key, {
            "n": 0, "sum4": 0.0, "sum24": 0.0, "pumps4": 0, "pumps24": 0,
            "traded": 0, "sum_mae": 0.0, "sum_mfe": 0.0, "sum_pull": 0.0,
            "sum_pull_pump": 0.0, "sum_mae_pump": 0.0,
            "keys_pump": {}, "keys_all": {},
        })
        a["n"] += 1
        a["sum4"] += move4
        a["sum24"] += move24
        a["sum_mae"] += mae
        a["sum_mfe"] += mfe
        a["sum_pull"] += mae

        if move4 >= PUMP_PCT:
            a["pumps4"] += 1

        did_pump = bool(ep.get("pumped") or mfe >= PUMP_PCT or move24 >= PUMP_PCT)
        if did_pump:
            a["pumps24"] += 1
            a["sum_pull_pump"] = a.get("sum_pull_pump", 0.0) + pullback
            a["sum_mae_pump"] = a.get("sum_mae_pump", 0.0) + mae

            for k in ep["keys"]:
                a["keys_pump"][k] = a["keys_pump"].get(k, 0) + 1
            sv = ep.get("signal_values", {})
            for k, v in sv.items():
                if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                    a.setdefault("signal_values_pump", {}).setdefault(k, []).append(v)

        for k in ep["keys"]:
            a["keys_all"][k] = a["keys_all"].get(k, 0) + 1

        if ep.get("success"):
            a["traded"] += 1

    def autotune(self):
        if not self.tuning["auto"]:
            return
        now = time.time()
        if now - self._last_tune < 1800:
            return
        self._last_tune = now

        active_agg = self._combined_agg()

        for mode in ["rocket", "sniper", "reversal"]:
            nudge = 0.0
            mode_pumps = 0
            mode_traded = 0
            mode_n = 0
            mode_sum24 = 0.0

            for key, a in active_agg.items():
                if f"|{mode}|" in key:
                    mode_n += a["n"]
                    mode_pumps += a["pumps24"]
                    mode_traded += a["traded"]
                    mode_sum24 += a.get("sum24", 0.0)

            if mode_n >= 5:
                pump_rate = mode_pumps / mode_n
                avg_24 = mode_sum24 / mode_n
                missed = max(0, mode_pumps - mode_traded)

                if avg_24 < -0.5 or pump_rate < 0.22:
                    nudge += 0.25
                elif missed >= 3 and pump_rate >= 0.40 and avg_24 > 0.5:
                    nudge -= 0.20

            self.tuning[f"nudge_{mode}"] = round(max(-0.5, min(0.5, nudge)), 2)

        total_pumps = 0
        total_missed_pumps = 0
        sum_pull_pump = 0.0
        sum_mae_pump = 0.0

        for a in active_agg.values():
            pumps = a.get("pumps24", 0)
            total_pumps += pumps
            total_missed_pumps += max(0, pumps - a.get("traded", 0))
            sum_pull_pump += a.get("sum_pull_pump", 0.0)
            sum_mae_pump += a.get("sum_mae_pump", 0.0)

        if total_pumps >= 5:
            avg_pull = sum_pull_pump / total_pumps
            avg_mae = sum_mae_pump / total_pumps

            if total_missed_pumps > 10:
                self.tuning["hunt"] = round(max(-0.01, min(-0.001, avg_pull / 100 * 0.3)), 4)
            else:
                self.tuning["hunt"] = round(max(-0.01, min(-0.002, avg_pull / 100 * 0.6)), 4)

            self.tuning["sl_mult"] = round(max(0.8, min(1.5, 1.0 + abs(avg_mae) / 100 * 0.3)), 2)

        self._apply_weight_lift()
        self._calibrate_signal_windows()
        self.save()
        logger.info(f"shadow autotune: {self.tuning}")

    def _apply_weight_lift(self):
        kp, ka = {}, {}
        for a in self.agg.values():
            for k, v in a.get("keys_pump", {}).items():
                kp[k] = kp.get(k, 0) + v
            for k, v in a.get("keys_all", {}).items():
                ka[k] = ka.get(k, 0) + v
        total_pump = sum(kp.values()) or 1
        total_all = sum(ka.values()) or 1
        base_rate = total_pump / total_all
        for k in list(learner.weights):
            if ka.get(k, 0) >= 10 and base_rate > 0:
                lift = (kp.get(k, 0) / ka.get(k, 0)) / base_rate
                if lift > 1.2:
                    learner.weights[k] = round(min(1.45, learner.weights[k] + 0.03), 3)
                elif lift < 0.8:
                    learner.weights[k] = round(max(0.55, learner.weights[k] - 0.03), 3)
        learner.save()

    def _calibrate_signal_windows(self):
        pump_values = {"rsi": [], "chg24h": [], "volume": []}
        for a in self.agg.values():
            sv = a.get("signal_values_pump", {})
            for k in pump_values:
                pump_values[k].extend(sv.get(k, []))
        if len(pump_values["rsi"]) < 50:
            return

        w = self.tuning["signal_windows"]
        rsi_sorted = sorted(pump_values["rsi"])
        rsi_p90 = rsi_sorted[int(len(rsi_sorted) * 0.9)]
        w["rsi_hi"] = int(round(w["rsi_hi"] * 0.8 + max(80, min(95, round(rsi_p90))) * 0.2))

        chg_sorted = sorted(pump_values["chg24h"])
        chg_p90 = chg_sorted[int(len(chg_sorted) * 0.9)]
        w["chg_hi"] = int(round(w["chg_hi"] * 0.8 + max(20, min(50, round(chg_p90))) * 0.2))

        vol_sorted = sorted(pump_values["volume"])
        vol_p10 = vol_sorted[int(len(vol_sorted) * 0.1)]
        w["vol_lo"] = round(w["vol_lo"] * 0.8 + max(1.0, min(3.0, round(vol_p10, 1))) * 0.2, 1)

    def signal_windows(self):
        return self.tuning.get("signal_windows", {"rsi_hi": 90, "chg_hi": 30, "vol_lo": 1.3})

    def hunt(self):
        return self.tuning["hunt"]

    def near(self):
        return self.tuning["near"]

    def capture(self):
        return self.tuning["capture"]

    def sl_mult(self):
        return self.tuning["sl_mult"] if self.tuning["auto"] else 1.0

    def tp_mult(self):
        return self.tuning["tp_mult"] if self.tuning["auto"] else 1.0

    def set_auto(self, on):
        self.tuning["auto"] = bool(on)
        self.save()

    def learn_lines(self):
        out = ["👁 <b>Теневой журнал</b> · упущенные возможности (4ч/24ч)"]
        out.append(f"   автотюн: {'🟢 вкл' if self.tuning['auto'] else '🔴 выкл'} · "
                   f"эпизодов: {len(self.episodes)}")
        shown = False
        reg_emoji = {"bull": "🟢", "neutral": "🟡", "bear": "🔴"}

        active_agg = self._combined_agg()
        for key in sorted(active_agg):
            a = active_agg[key]
            if a["n"] < 3:
                continue

            parts = key.split("|")
            if len(parts) == 3:
                reg, mode, band = parts
            else:
                continue

            avg = a["sum24"] / a["n"]
            avg_mfe = a.get("sum_mfe", 0.0) / a["n"]
            em = reg_emoji.get(reg, "⚪")
            mode_icon = "🚀" if mode == "rocket" else "🧲" if mode == "reversal" else "🏹"
            pumps_cnt = max(a.get("pumps24", 0), a.get("traded", 0))

            out.append(
                f"   {em} {mode_icon} {band}: {a['n']} набл. · ср. {avg:+.1f}% (пик +{avg_mfe:.1f}%) · "
                f"🎯 {a.get('traded', 0)}/{pumps_cnt} пампов"
            )
            shown = True

        if not shown:
            out.append("   (накапливается)")

        t = self.tuning
        w = t.get("signal_windows", {})
        out.append(f"   ⚙️ тюнинг: 🚀 {t.get('nudge_rocket', 0):+.2f} | 🏹 {t.get('nudge_sniper', 0):+.2f} | 🧲 {t.get('nudge_reversal', 0):+.2f}")
        out.append(f"   📏 откат {t['hunt']*100:+.2f}% · SL ×{t['sl_mult']:.2f}")
        out.append(f"   📏 RSI ≤{w.get('rsi_hi', 90)} · chg ≤{w.get('chg_hi', 30)}% · vol ≥{w.get('vol_lo', 1.3)}×")
        return out

    def stats_text(self):
        return "\n".join(self.learn_lines())

    def reset(self):
        self.episodes = {}
        self.agg = {}
        self.cooldown = {}
        self.tuning = {
            "auto": True,
            "nudge_rocket": 0.0, "nudge_sniper": 0.0, "nudge_reversal": 0.0,
            "hunt": -0.004, "near": -0.0015, "capture": +0.002,
            "sl_mult": 1.0, "tp_mult": 1.0,
            "signal_windows": {"rsi_hi": 90, "chg_hi": 30, "vol_lo": 1.3},
        }
        self.save()
        logger.info("shadow: журнал автотюна полностью сброшен")


shadow = Shadow()

from bot.strategy.learner import learner

# Блок 6.3: Оптимизация нижних границ сумм покупок (исключаем микро-ордера по $10-$15)
TIERS = [
    (150, 18, 30),
    (250, 25, 45),
    (500, 32, 80),
    (1000, 42, 125),
    (2500, 75, 250),
    (5000, 140, 500),
    (10000, 260, 1000),
]

TIER_OFFSET_BOUNDS = {
    "TOP20": (-0.0035, -0.0010),
    "MID":   (-0.0065, -0.0020),
    "SMALL": (-0.0100, -0.0035),
    "MICRO": (-0.0160, -0.0060),
}

TIER_ACCUM_BOUNDS = {
    "TOP20": (-0.0015, -0.0005),
    "MID":   (-0.0030, -0.0010),
    "SMALL": (-0.0045, -0.0015),
    "MICRO": (-0.0065, -0.0025),
}

TIER_ROCKET_BOUNDS = {
    "TOP20": (-0.0020, -0.0005),
    "MID":   (-0.0035, -0.0010),
    "SMALL": (-0.0050, -0.0020),
    "MICRO": (-0.0080, -0.0035),
}

# Блок 2.2: Лимитный вход в глубину тени для reversal (вместо покупки по рынку с +0.15%)
TIER_REVERSAL_BOUNDS = {
    "TOP20": (-0.0055, -0.0018),
    "MID":   (-0.0080, -0.0025),
    "SMALL": (-0.0110, -0.0035),
    "MICRO": (-0.0150, -0.0050),
}


def tier_limits(equity):
    for bound, mn, mx in TIERS:
        if equity <= bound:
            return mn, mx
    return max(260.0, equity * 0.035), equity * 0.10


def min_order_threshold(equity: float) -> float:
    """Минимальный допустимый размер ордера для фильтрации микро-покупок."""
    base_min, _ = tier_limits(equity)
    return max(18.0, round(base_min * 0.85, 2))


def portfolio_limits(equity):
    if equity <= 150:
        return 2, 2
    if equity <= 250:
        return 3, 2
    if equity <= 500:
        return 4, 3
    if equity <= 1000:
        return 6, 8
    if equity <= 2500:
        return 8, 10
    if equity <= 5000:
        return 10, 12
    return 12, 15


def buy_size(equity, score, thr, liquidity, free_usdt, entry_mode="sniper", grade="B"):
    base_min, base_max = tier_limits(equity)
    score_range = 10.0 - thr
    strength = 0.0 if score_range <= 0 else max(0.0, min(1.0, (score - thr) / score_range))

    size = base_min + (base_max - base_min) * strength
    max_allowed = base_max

    if liquidity < 500_000:
        size = base_min

    kelly = learner.kelly_multiplier(entry_mode)
    if kelly is not None:
        size *= (0.65 + 0.35 * kelly)

    # Блок 6.2: Динамический коэффициент доверия к Grade A+ [💎]
    if grade == "A+":
        size *= learner.grade_a_trust_mult()

    min_allowed = min_order_threshold(equity)
    size = max(min_allowed, min(size, max_allowed))
    size = min(size, free_usdt * 0.95, equity * 0.12)

    return round(size, 2)


def entry_offset(score, thr, regime, atr_pct, entry_mode="sniper", tier="SMALL", is_accumulation=False):
    from bot.strategy.shadow import shadow
    hunt = shadow.hunt()
    tier = tier or "SMALL"
    surplus = max(0.0, score - thr)
    strength_alpha = min(1.0, surplus / 2.5)

    if entry_mode == "rocket":
        bounds = TIER_ROCKET_BOUNDS.get(tier, TIER_ROCKET_BOUNDS["SMALL"])
        raw = -atr_pct / 100 * 0.20 * (1.0 - strength_alpha * 0.4)
        return max(bounds[0], min(bounds[1], raw))

    # Блок 2.2: Вход в глубину тени на проливах (Second Test / Base Entry)
    if entry_mode == "reversal":
        bounds = TIER_REVERSAL_BOUNDS.get(tier, TIER_REVERSAL_BOUNDS["SMALL"])
        raw = -atr_pct / 100 * 0.35 * (1.0 - strength_alpha * 0.25)
        return max(bounds[0], min(bounds[1], raw))

    if is_accumulation:
        bounds = TIER_ACCUM_BOUNDS.get(tier, TIER_ACCUM_BOUNDS["SMALL"])
        base_accum_target = -atr_pct / 100 * 0.20
        target = base_accum_target * (1.0 - strength_alpha * 0.5)
        if regime == "bear":
            target *= 1.2
        return max(bounds[0], min(bounds[1], target))

    bounds = TIER_OFFSET_BOUNDS.get(tier, TIER_OFFSET_BOUNDS["SMALL"])
    base_pullback = -atr_pct / 100 * 0.45

    if regime == "bear":
        target = min(hunt * 1.3, base_pullback * 1.3)
    elif regime == "bull":
        target = min(shadow.near(), base_pullback * 0.7)
    else:
        pullback_mult = 0.6 if strength_alpha >= 0.7 else (0.85 if strength_alpha >= 0.3 else 1.1)
        target = min(hunt * pullback_mult, base_pullback * pullback_mult)

    clamped_target = max(bounds[0], min(bounds[1], target))
    dynamic_offset = clamped_target + (bounds[1] - clamped_target) * (strength_alpha * 0.6)

    return max(bounds[0], min(bounds[1], dynamic_offset))

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/news/__init__.py ---
import os
import time

import httpx
from loguru import logger

CMC_BASE = "https://pro-api.coinmarketcap.com"

_cache = {"hype": None, "hype_ts": 0, "info": {}}


async def _get(path, params):
    key = os.getenv("CMC_API_KEY", "").strip()
    if not key:
        return None
    headers = {"X-CMC_PRO_API_KEY": key}
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10) as c:
            r = await c.get(CMC_BASE + path, params=params, headers=headers)
        data = r.json()
        if data.get("status", {}).get("error_code"):
            logger.error(f"CMC error: {data['status']}")
            return None
        return data
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"CMC request error: {e}")
        return None


async def get_hype_symbols() -> set:
    """Монеты из трендов CMC (кэш 30 минут)."""
    now = time.time()
    if _cache["hype"] is not None and now - _cache["hype_ts"] < 1800:
        return _cache["hype"]
    hype = set()
    for path in ["/v1/cryptocurrency/trending/latest",
                 "/v1/cryptocurrency/trending/most-visited"]:
        data = await _get(path, {"limit": 30})
        if data:
            arr = data.get("data")
            if isinstance(arr, dict):
                arr = arr.get("items") or []
            for item in arr or []:
                sym = (item.get("symbol") or "").upper()
                if sym:
                    hype.add(sym)
    _cache["hype"], _cache["hype_ts"] = hype, now
    return hype


async def get_coin_name(symbol: str) -> str:
    """Название монеты по тику (кэш 24 часа)."""
    info = _cache["info"].get(symbol)
    if info and time.time() - info["ts"] < 86400:
        return info["name"]
    data = await _get("/v1/cryptocurrency/info", {"symbol": symbol})
    name = ""
    if data:
        arr = data.get("data", {}).get(symbol) or data.get("data", {}).get(symbol.upper())
        if isinstance(arr, list) and arr:
            name = arr[0].get("name", "")
        elif isinstance(arr, dict):
            name = arr.get("name", "")
    _cache["info"][symbol] = {"name": name, "ts": time.time()}
    return name

def tier_of(rank):
    if rank is None:
        return "MICRO"
    if rank <= 20:
        return "TOP20"
    if rank <= 100:
        return "MID"
    if rank <= 500:
        return "SMALL"
    return "MICRO"

def _load_sectors():
    global _sector_cache
    try:
        if SECTOR_FILE.exists():
            _sector_cache = json.loads(SECTOR_FILE.read_text())
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"sectors load error: {e}")
        
    if not _sector_cache:
        data = download_state(SECTOR_REMOTE)
        if isinstance(data, dict) and data:
            _sector_cache = data
            
    cleaned = {k: v for k, v in _sector_cache.items() if v != "Other"}
    if len(cleaned) < len(_sector_cache):
        _sector_cache = cleaned
        
    logger.info(f"sectors: кэш загружен ({len(_sector_cache)} валидных монет)")


def _save_sectors():
    global _last_upload
    try:
        SECTOR_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        SECTOR_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(_sector_cache, ensure_ascii=False))
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"sectors save error: {e}")
    if time.time() - _last_upload > 3600:
        _last_upload = time.time()
        upload_state(SECTOR_REMOTE, _sector_cache)


_load_sectors()


def sector_of(base):
    return SECTORS.get(base) or _sector_cache.get(base) or "Other"


def _tags_to_sector(tags):
    for t in tags or []:
        s = SECTOR_TAG_MAP.get(t.lower())
        if s:
            return s
    return None


async def get_sectors_for_pool(bases):
    result = {}
    need = []
    now = time.time()
    
    for b in bases:
        if b in SECTORS:
            result[b] = SECTORS[b]
        elif b in _sector_cache:
            result[b] = _sector_cache[b]
        elif b in _missing_sectors_cooldown and now - _missing_sectors_cooldown[b] < 86400:
            result[b] = "Other"
        else:
            need.append(b)
            
    if not need:
        return result
        
    await asyncio.sleep(1.0)
    try:
        key = os.getenv("CMC_API_KEY", "").strip()
        headers = {"X-CMC_PRO_API_KEY": key} if key else {}
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10) as c:
            r = await c.get(f"{CMC_BASE}/v1/cryptocurrency/info",
                            params={"symbol": ",".join(need[:100])},
                            headers=headers)
            data = r.json().get("data", {})
            
        learned = 0
        for b in need:
            arr = data.get(b)
            sector = "Other"
            if isinstance(arr, list) and arr:
                sector = _tags_to_sector(arr[0].get("tags")) or "Other"
            elif isinstance(arr, dict):
                sector = _tags_to_sector(arr.get("tags")) or "Other"
                
            result[b] = sector
            
            if sector != "Other":
                _sector_cache[b] = sector
                learned += 1
            else:
                _missing_sectors_cooldown[b] = now
                
        if learned:
            _save_sectors()
            logger.info(f"sectors: кэш обновлен, выучено {learned} новых записей (всего: {len(_sector_cache)})")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"sectors fetch error: {e}")
        for b in need:
            result.setdefault(b, "Other")
    return result


RANKS_FILE = Path(os.getenv("STORAGE_DIR", "storage")) / "ranks.json"
_ranks = {"data": {}, "ts": 0.0}


def _load_ranks():
    try:
        if RANKS_FILE.exists():
            d = json.loads(RANKS_FILE.read_text())
            _ranks["data"] = d.get("data", {})
            _ranks["ts"] = float(d.get("ts", 0.0))
            logger.info(f"ranks: кэш загружен ({len(_ranks['data'])} рангов)")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"ranks load error: {e}")


_load_ranks()


async def get_ranks_for_pool(bases):
    now = time.time()
    if _ranks["data"] and now - _ranks["ts"] < 21600:
        return {b: _ranks["data"].get(b) for b in bases}
    fetched = {}
    try:
        key = os.getenv("CMC_API_KEY", "").strip()
        headers = {"X-CMC_PRO_API_KEY": key} if key else {}
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10) as c:
            r = await c.get(
                f"{CMC_BASE}/v1/cryptocurrency/listings/latest",
                params={"symbol": ",".join(bases[:100]), "limit": 200},
                headers=headers,
            )
            data = r.json().get("data", [])
        for item in data:
            fetched[item.get("symbol")] = item.get("cmc_rank")
        _ranks["data"].update(fetched)
        _ranks["ts"] = now
        try:
            RANKS_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            RANKS_FILE.write_text(json.dumps({"data": _ranks["data"], "ts": _ranks["ts"]}))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"ranks save error: {e}")
        logger.info(f"ranks: обновлено {len(fetched)} рангов CMC")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"ranks fetch error: {e}")
    return {b: fetched.get(b, _ranks["data"].get(b)) for b in bases}


async def _get(path, params):
    key = os.getenv("CMC_API_KEY", "").strip()
    if not key:
        return None
    headers = {"X-CMC_PRO_API_KEY": key}
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10) as c:
            r = await c.get(CMC_BASE + path, params=params, headers=headers)
        data = r.json()
        if data.get("status", {}).get("error_code"):
            logger.error(f"CMC error: {data['status']}")
            return None
        return data
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"CMC request error: {e}")
        return None


NAMES_FILE = Path(os.getenv("STORAGE_DIR", "storage")) / "names.json"
NAMES_REMOTE = "names.json"
_names_cache = {}
_last_names_upload = 0.0

def _load_names():
    global _names_cache
    try:
        if NAMES_FILE.exists():
            _names_cache = json.loads(NAMES_FILE.read_text())
            logger.info(f"names: кэш загружен ({len(_names_cache)} имен)")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"names load error: {e}")
        
    if not _names_cache:
        data = download_state(NAMES_REMOTE)
        if isinstance(data, dict) and data:
            _names_cache = data
            logger.info(f"names: кэш восстановлен из GitHub ({len(_names_cache)} имен)")

def _save_names():
    global _last_names_upload
    try:
        NAMES_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        NAMES_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(_names_cache, ensure_ascii=False))
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"names save error: {e}")
        
    if time.time() - _last_names_upload > 3600:
        _last_names_upload = time.time()
        upload_state(NAMES_REMOTE, _names_cache)

_load_names()


async def fetch_missing_names(symbols):
    need = [s for s in symbols if s not in _names_cache]
    if not need:
        return
    
    key = os.getenv("CMC_API_KEY", "").strip()
    headers = {"X-CMC_PRO_API_KEY": key} if key else {}
    
    learned = 0
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10) as c:
        for i in range(0, len(need), 100):
            chunk = need[i:i+100]
            try:
                r = await c.get(f"{CMC_BASE}/v1/cryptocurrency/info", params={"symbol": ",".join(chunk)}, headers=headers)
                data = r.json()
                if data and "data" in data:
                    for s in chunk:
                        arr = data["data"].get(s) or data["data"].get(s.upper())
                        name = ""
                        if isinstance(arr, list) and arr:
                            name = arr[0].get("name", "")
                        elif isinstance(arr, dict):
                            name = arr.get("name", "")
                        _names_cache[s] = name
                        learned += 1
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"CMC fetch_missing_names error: {e}")
            await asyncio.sleep(1.0)
            
    if learned:
        _save_names()
        logger.info(f"names: выучено {learned} новых имен монет (всего в базе: {len(_names_cache)})")


async def get_coin_name(symbol: str) -> str:
    if symbol in _names_cache:
        return _names_cache[symbol]
        
    data = await _get("/v1/cryptocurrency/info", {"symbol": symbol})
    name = ""
    if data and "data" in data:
        arr = data["data"].get(symbol) or data["data"].get(symbol.upper())
        if isinstance(arr, list) and arr:
            name = arr[0].get("name", "")
        elif isinstance(arr, dict):
            name = arr.get("name", "")
            
    _names_cache[symbol] = name
    _save_names()
    return name


def memory_stats():
    sector_counts = {}
    for sec in SECTORS.values():
        sector_counts[sec] = sector_counts.get(sec, 0) + 1
    learned = 0
    for base, sec in _sector_cache.items():
        if base not in SECTORS:
            sector_counts[sec] = sector_counts.get(sec, 0) + 1
            learned += 1
    all_bases = set(SECTORS.keys()) | set(_sector_cache.keys())
    tier_counts = {}
    for base in all_bases:
        t = tier_of(_ranks["data"].get(base))
        tier_counts[t] = tier_counts.get(t, 0) + 1
    return {
        "base": len(SECTORS),
        "learned": learned,
        "total": len(all_bases),
        "sectors": sector_counts,
        "tiers": tier_counts,
    }

def get_stats():
    key = os.getenv("CMC_API_KEY", "").strip()
    return {
        "api_key_set": bool(key),
        "cache_count": len(_names_cache),
        "sectors_learned": len(_sector_cache),
        "ranks_cached": len(_ranks["data"]),
    }

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/news/rss_news.py ---
import re
import time
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

import httpx
from loguru import logger

_cache = {"items": None, "ts": 0, "listings": None, "listings_ts": 0}

NEWS_FEEDS = [
    "https://cointelegraph.com/rss",
    "https://www.coindesk.com/arc/outboundfeeds/rss/",
    "https://decrypt.co/feed",
    "https://cryptoslate.com/feed/",
    "https://bitcoinist.com/feed/",
    "https://newsbtc.com/feed/"
]

NEG_WORDS = [
    "hack", "hacked", "hacking", "exploit", "exploited", "vulnerability",
    "rug pull", "rugpull", "scam", "fraud", "delist", "delisting",
    "lawsuit", "sued", "sec investigation", "ban", "banned",
    "bankrupt", "bankruptcy", "insolvent", "collapse", "collapsed",
    "ponzi", "pyramid", "exit scam", "fake", "fake volume"
]

POS_WORDS = [
    "partnership", "partnerships", "integration", "integrated",
    "etf approval", "etf approved", "listing", "listed", "launch", "launched",
    "adoption", "adopted", "institutional", "bullish", "breakout",
    "ath", "all time high", "record high", "surge", "soaring"
]


def _count(words, text):
    """Считает совпадения по границам слов (bank != ban)."""
    total = 0
    for w in words:
        pat = re.compile(r'\b' + re.escape(w) + r'\b', re.IGNORECASE)
        if pat.search(text):
            total += 1
    return total


def _parse(xml_text):
    items = []
    
    # Попытка 1: строгий XML парсер
    try:
        root = ET.fromstring(xml_text)
        for item in root.iter('item'):
            items.append({
                'title': item.findtext('title') or '',
                'desc': item.findtext('description') or '',
                'link': item.findtext('link') or '',
                'ts': int(time.time()),
            })
        return items
    except ET.ParseError:
        pass # Идем в Fallback
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"RSS XML parse failed, trying regex fallback: {e}")

    # Попытка 2: Бронебойный Regex (Fallback)
    try:
        item_pattern = re.compile(r'<item[^>]*>(.*?)</item>', re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)
        for block in item_pattern.finditer(xml_text):
            chunk = block.group(1)
            
            title_m = re.search(r'<title[^>]*>(?:<!\[CDATA\[)?(.*?)(?:\]\]>)?</title>', chunk, re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)
            desc_m = re.search(r'<description[^>]*>(?:<!\[CDATA\[)?(.*?)(?:\]\]>)?</description>', chunk, re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)
            link_m = re.search(r'<link[^>]*>(?:<!\[CDATA\[)?(.*?)(?:\]\]>)?</link>', chunk, re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)
            
            items.append({
                'title': title_m.group(1).strip() if title_m else '',
                'desc': desc_m.group(1).strip() if desc_m else '',
                'link': link_m.group(1).strip() if link_m else '',
                'ts': int(time.time()),
            })
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"RSS regex fallback failed: {e}")

    return items


def _parse_listings(xml_text):
    """Извлекает новые листинги из RSS Bybit — устойчиво к невалидному XML."""
    listings = []

    # Попытка 1: стандартный XML-парсер
    try:
        root = ET.fromstring(xml_text)
        for item in root.iter('item'):
            title = item.findtext('title') or ''
            link = item.findtext('link') or ''
            pub_date = item.findtext('pubDate')
            entry = _extract_listing(title, link, pub_date)
            if entry:
                listings.append(entry)
        return listings
    except ET.ParseError:
        pass  # невалидный XML — идём в fallback
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"Listings ET parse failed, trying regex fallback: {e}")

    # Попытка 2: regex-fallback — работает с битым XML/HTML
    try:
        item_pattern = re.compile(
            r'<item[^>]*>(.*?)</item>',
            re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL
        )
        for block in item_pattern.finditer(xml_text):
            chunk = block.group(1)
            title_m = re.search(r'<title[^>]*>(?:<!\[CDATA\[)?(.*?)(?:\]\]>)?</title>',
                                chunk, re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)
            link_m = re.search(r'<link[^>]*>(?:<!\[CDATA\[)?(.*?)(?:\]\]>)?</link>',
                               chunk, re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)
            date_m = re.search(r'<pubDate[^>]*>(.*?)</pubDate>',
                               chunk, re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)
            title = title_m.group(1).strip() if title_m else ''
            link = link_m.group(1).strip() if link_m else ''
            pub_date = date_m.group(1).strip() if date_m else None
            entry = _extract_listing(title, link, pub_date)
            if entry:
                listings.append(entry)
        if listings:
            logger.info(f"Listings parsed via regex fallback: {len(listings)} items")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Listings regex fallback also failed: {e}")

    return listings


def _extract_listing(title, link, pub_date):
    """Если заголовок про новый листинг — возвращает запись, иначе None."""
    match = re.search(r'New Listing:?\s+([A-Z0-9]+)\s*USDT', title, re.IGNORECASE)
    if not match:
        match = re.search(r'\b([A-Z0-9]{2,10})\s*USDT\s+(?:Now\s+)?Listed', title, re.IGNORECASE)
    if not match:
        return None
    symbol = match.group(1).upper() + 'USDT'
    ts = int(time.time())
    if pub_date:
        try:
            from email.utils import parsedate_to_datetime
            dt = parsedate_to_datetime(pub_date)
            ts = int(dt.timestamp())
        except Exception:
            pass
    return {'symbol': symbol, 'title': title.strip(), 'link': (link or '').strip(), 'ts': ts}


async def fetch_news_cache(force=False):
    """Свежие заголовки из RSS (кэш 15 минут)."""
    now = time.time()
    # Если force=True, игнорируем кэш и идем в сеть
    if not force and _cache["items"] is not None and now - _cache["ts"] < 900:
        return _cache["items"]
    all_items = []
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10, follow_redirects=True) as c:
        for url in NEWS_FEEDS:
            try:
                r = await c.get(url)
                all_items += _parse(r.text)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"RSS fetch error {url}: {e}")
    _cache["items"], _cache["ts"] = all_items, now
    return all_items


async def fetch_listings_cache(force=False):
    """Новые листинги Bybit (кэш 1 час)."""
    now = time.time()
    if not force and _cache["listings"] is not None and now - _cache["listings_ts"] < 3600:
        return _cache["listings"]
    listings = []
    for url in [
        "https://announcements.bybit.com/en/rss",
        "https://announcements.bybit.com/rss",
    ]:
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10, follow_redirects=True) as c:
                r = await c.get(url)
            found = _parse_listings(r.text)
            if found:
                listings = found
                break
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Listings fetch error {url}: {e}")
    _cache["listings"], _cache["listings_ts"] = listings, now
    return listings


def check_sentiment(items, keys):
    """Новостной фон по монете: (негатив, позитив, упоминания, заголовки)."""
    neg = pos = mentions = 0
    heads = []
    keys_low = [k.lower() for k in keys if k]
    for it in items:
        text = it["title"] + " " + it["desc"]
        if not any(k in text.lower() for k in keys_low):
            continue
        mentions += 1
        n = _count(NEG_WORDS, text)
        p = _count(POS_WORDS, text)
        if n > p:
            neg += 1
            heads.append("⚠️ " + it["title"])
        elif p > n:
            pos += 1
            heads.append("✅ " + it["title"])
    return neg, pos, mentions, heads[:2]


def get_stats():
    """Статистика RSS-лент для мониторинга."""
    now = time.time()
    cache_age = now - _cache["ts"] if _cache["ts"] else None
    items = _cache["items"] or []
    listings = _cache["listings"] or []

    neg_examples = []
    pos_examples = []
    for item in items[:20]:
        text = item["title"] + " " + item["desc"]
        n = _count(NEG_WORDS, text)
        p = _count(POS_WORDS, text)
        if n > p and len(neg_examples) < 3:
            neg_examples.append(item["title"][:80])
        elif p > n and len(pos_examples) < 3:
            pos_examples.append(item["title"][:80])

    now_ts = int(time.time())
    fresh_listings = [
        l for l in listings
        if 86400 <= now_ts - l['ts'] <= 1209600  # 24ч – 14 дней
    ]

    return {
        "cache_age_min": round(cache_age / 60, 1) if cache_age else None,
        "items_count": len(items),
        "feeds_working": len(items) > 0,
        "neg_examples": neg_examples,
        "pos_examples": pos_examples,
        "listings_count": len(fresh_listings),
        "listings_recent": [l['symbol'] for l in fresh_listings[:10]],
    }
